FILE: netlify\functions\_shared\sanitize.mjs
=====================================================
/**
 * TSDR -- input validation + sanitisation.
 * Defence in depth: DB CHECK constraints already cap lengths; here we reject
 * bad input early and neutralise HTML/script so stored text is render-safe.
 * We escape (not strip) so names like "A&B" survive intact but can't inject.
 */

const LIMITS = {
  honouree_name: 120,
  symbolic_star_name: 80,
  dedicator_name: 120,
  dedication_message: 1200,
  occasion: 32,
  session_ref: 200,
};

const OCCASIONS = new Set([
  'birthday','anniversary','wedding','engagement','memorial','newborn',
  'graduation','friendship','appreciation','milestone','other',
]);

const PRIVACY = new Set(['public','limited','private','unlisted']);

/** Escape HTML-significant characters. */
export function escapeHtml(s) {
  return String(s)
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
    .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
    .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
}

/**
 * Validate an email address (lightweight, deliberately permissive but safe).
 * Returns { email, ok }. We lower-case + trim; the real deliverability check is
 * whether the provider accepts it at send time.
 */
export function validateEmail(s) {
  const email = String(s == null ? '' : s).trim().toLowerCase();
  const ok = email.length >= 3 && email.length <= 254 &&
    /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email);
  return { email, ok };
}

/** Collapse control chars and trim; returns cleaned string. */
export function cleanText(s) {
  if (s == null) return '';
  // remove null bytes + C0 control chars except newline/tab
  return String(s).replace(/[\u0000-\u0008\u000B\u000C\u000E-\u001F]/g, '').trim();
}

/**
 * Validate + sanitise a dedication payload.
 * Returns { value, errors: [] }. Never throws.
 */
export function validateDedication(input) {
  const errors = [];
  const v = {};
  // Store CLEANED RAW text (control chars stripped, trimmed, length-capped).
  // We intentionally do NOT HTML-escape here: every HTML sink escapes at OUTPUT
  // time (explore.js/viewer.js/account.js via esc(); reveal.js via textContent;
  // certificate via safe()). Escaping at storage would double-encode entities and
  // show literal "&#39;" in textContent contexts. This is the store-raw/
  // escape-on-output pattern. SQL injection is prevented by parameterised RPCs.
  const req = (key, required = true) => {
    let s = cleanText(input?.[key]);
    if (required && s.length === 0) errors.push(`${key} is required`);
    if (LIMITS[key] && s.length > LIMITS[key]) {
      errors.push(`${key} exceeds ${LIMITS[key]} characters`);
      s = s.slice(0, LIMITS[key]);
    }
    v[key] = s;
    return s;
  };

  req('honouree_name', true);
  req('symbolic_star_name', true);
  req('dedicator_name', false);
  req('dedication_message', false);

  const occ = cleanText(input?.occasion).toLowerCase();
  if (!OCCASIONS.has(occ)) errors.push('invalid occasion');
  v.occasion = occ;

  const priv = cleanText(input?.privacy_level).toLowerCase();
  if (!PRIVACY.has(priv)) errors.push('invalid privacy_level');
  v.privacy_level = priv;

  // dedication_date optional; must be YYYY-MM-DD if present
  const d = cleanText(input?.dedication_date);
  if (d) {
    if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(d) || Number.isNaN(Date.parse(d))) {
      errors.push('dedication_date must be YYYY-MM-DD');
    } else {
      v.dedication_date = d;
    }
  } else {
    v.dedication_date = null;
  }

  return { value: v, errors };
}

/** Validate a reservation request (constellation/visibility optional). */
export function validateReservation(input) {
  const errors = [];
  const v = {};
  const con = cleanText(input?.constellation);
  if (con && !/^[A-Za-z]{3}$/.test(con)) errors.push('constellation must be a 3-letter code');
  v.constellation = con ? con.slice(0, 3) : null;

  const vis = cleanText(input?.visibility);
  const VIS = new Set(['naked_eye','binocular','small_telescope','telescope']);
  if (vis && !VIS.has(vis)) errors.push('invalid visibility');
  v.visibility = vis || null;
  return { value: v, errors };
}

/**
 * Normalise a registration number to canonical TSDR-YYYY-XXXXXXXX.
 * Tolerant of spacing, casing, and missing/extra dashes (users often type
 * "tsdr 2026 abcdefgh" or "tsdr2026abcdefgh"). We strip to alphanumerics, then
 * reconstruct the canonical dashed form before validating.
 */
export function normalizeRegNo(s) {
  let raw = String(s || '').toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9]/g, ''); // keep alnum only
  // Expect: TSDR + 4-digit year + 8-char code (32-symbol alphabet, no I/L/O/U)
  const m = /^TSDR(\d{4})([0-9A-HJ-NP-TV-Z]{8})$/.exec(raw);
  if (!m) {
    // Fall back: if it already looks canonical after light cleanup, accept it.
    const dashed = String(s || '').toUpperCase().replace(/\s+/g, '');
    return { regno: dashed, ok: /^TSDR-\d{4}-[0-9A-HJ-NP-TV-Z]{8}$/.test(dashed) };
  }
  const regno = `TSDR-${m[1]}-${m[2]}`;
  return { regno, ok: true };
}

FILE: netlify\functions\_shared\supabase.mjs
=====================================================
/**
 * TSDR -- Supabase clients.
 * -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * getServiceClient(): SERVICE-ROLE client for trusted server functions ONLY.
 *   Bypasses RLS by design. NEVER import this into browser code.
 * getUserClient(jwt): anon client scoped to the caller's JWT, so RLS applies --
 *   used to read the caller's own dedications in /account.
 * The dependency is imported lazily so unit tests (which inject a mock db) do
 * not require the package to be installed.
 * -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 */

let _createClient = null;
async function loadCreateClient() {
  if (_createClient) return _createClient;
  const mod = await import('@supabase/supabase-js');
  _createClient = mod.createClient;
  return _createClient;
}

export async function getServiceClient(env = process.env) {
  const createClient = await loadCreateClient();
  return createClient(env.SUPABASE_URL, env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY, {
    auth: { autoRefreshToken: false, persistSession: false },
    global: { headers: { 'X-TSDR-Context': 'service' } },
  });
}

export async function getUserClient(jwt, env = process.env) {
  const createClient = await loadCreateClient();
  return createClient(env.SUPABASE_URL, env.SUPABASE_ANON_KEY, {
    auth: { autoRefreshToken: false, persistSession: false },
    global: { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${jwt}` } },
  });
}

/**
 * Thin RPC wrapper that normalises the { data, error } shape into throw-on-error.
 * Works with either a real Supabase client or an injected mock exposing .rpc().
 */
export async function rpc(db, fn, args) {
  const { data, error } = await db.rpc(fn, args);
  if (error) {
    const e = new Error(error.message || `rpc ${fn} failed`);
    e.pgError = error;
    throw e;
  }
  return data;
}

FILE: netlify\functions\account.mjs
=====================================================
/**
 * GET /.netlify/functions/account
 * Returns the authenticated caller's dedications. Requires a valid Supabase JWT
 * in the Authorization: Bearer header. The user id comes from the VERIFIED token,
 * never from the client body (prevents IDOR).
 */
import { ok, fail, ERR, requireMethod } from './_shared/response.mjs';
import { corsHeaders, handlePreflight, cleanCors } from './_shared/cors.mjs';
import { getBearer, requireUser } from './_shared/auth.mjs';
import { rpc } from './_shared/supabase.mjs';

export async function core(event, deps) {
  const { db, verifyImpl, env = process.env } = deps;

  const pre = handlePreflight(event, env);
  if (pre) return pre;
  const cors = cleanCors(corsHeaders(event, env));

  const bad = requireMethod(event, 'GET');
  if (bad) return { ...bad, headers: { ...bad.headers, ...cors } };

  const jwt = getBearer(event);
  const { user, error } = await requireUser(jwt, verifyImpl);
  if (error || !user) return fail(401, ERR.UNAUTHORIZED, 'Please sign in.', cors);

  try {
    const rows = await rpc(db, 'tsdr_account_dedications', { p_user: user.id });
    return ok({ ok: true, dedications: rows || [] }, cors);
  } catch {
    return fail(500, ERR.INTERNAL, 'Could not load your dedications.', cors);
  }
}

export async function handler(event) {
  const { getServiceClient } = await import('./_shared/supabase.mjs');
  const db = await getServiceClient();
  const verifyImpl = async (jwt) => {
    const { data, error } = await db.auth.getUser(jwt);
    if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
    return { user: data.user };
  };
  return core(event, { db, verifyImpl });
}
=====================================================
FILE: netlify\functions\account-download.mjs
=====================================================
/**
 * POST /.netlify/functions/account-download   (Run C -- authenticated owner)
 * Body: { registration_number, type: 'certificate' | 'starmap' }
 * Header: Authorization: Bearer <supabase jwt>
 *
 * Lets a SIGNED-IN buyer re-download the certificate PDF / star-map PNG for a
 * dedication they OWN, for ANY privacy level, WITHOUT the keepsake token (which
 * is never stored raw). Ownership is proven by the VERIFIED JWT via
 * tsdr_owner_record; the client body user id is never trusted.
 *
 * Reuses the shared generator (issueArtifacts) + the shared R2 SigV4 signer
 * (signedGet), exactly like the buyer-facing issue-artifacts + download pair.
 *
 * Returns:
 *   { ok:true, url, expires_in:300 }                          signed R2 link
 *   { ok:false, code:'TSDR_STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE' }             R2 not configured
 *        -> the account page keeps the HTML certificate (Print / Save as PDF).
 *   401 / 404 for not-signed-in / not-owned-or-not-active.
 */
import { ok, fail, ERR, requireMethod, parseBody } from './_shared/response.mjs';
import { corsHeaders, handlePreflight, cleanCors } from './_shared/cors.mjs';
import { getBearer, requireUser } from './_shared/auth.mjs';
import { normalizeRegNo, cleanText } from './_shared/sanitize.mjs';
import { rpc } from './_shared/supabase.mjs';
import { clientKey, limit, supabaseHitImpl } from './_shared/ratelimit.mjs';
import { signedGet } from './_shared/r2.mjs';
import { issueArtifacts, r2Configured } from './_shared/artifacts.mjs';

export async function core(event, deps) {
  const {
    db, hitImpl, verifyImpl, env = process.env,
    deps3, issueImpl = issueArtifacts, signImpl = signedGet,
  } = deps;

  const pre = handlePreflight(event, env); if (pre) return pre;
  const cors = cleanCors(corsHeaders(event, env));
  const bad = requireMethod(event, 'POST'); if (bad) return { ...bad, headers: { ...bad.headers, ...cors } };

  const rl = await limit(hitImpl, clientKey(event, 'acctdl'), 20, 300);
  if (!rl.allowed) return fail(429, ERR.RATE_LIMITED, 'Too many requests.',
    { ...cors, 'Retry-After': String(rl.retryAfter) });

  const jwt = getBearer(event);
  const { user, error } = await requireUser(jwt, verifyImpl);
  if (error || !user) return fail(401, ERR.UNAUTHORIZED, 'Please sign in.', cors);

  const [body, perr] = parseBody(event);
  if (perr) return { ...perr, headers: { ...perr.headers, ...cors } };

  const type = cleanText(body.type);
  if (!['certificate', 'starmap'].includes(type))
    return fail(400, ERR.VALIDATION, 'Invalid download type.', cors);
  const { regno, ok: valid } = normalizeRegNo(body.registration_number || '');
  if (!valid) return fail(400, ERR.VALIDATION, 'A valid registration number is required.', cors);

  try {
    // Ownership + record (any privacy) resolved from the VERIFIED user id.
    const rows = await rpc(db, 'tsdr_owner_record', { p_user: user.id, p_regno: regno });
    const rec = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows[0] : rows;
    if (!rec || !rec.dedication_id) {
      return fail(404, ERR.NOT_FOUND, 'No matching dedication for your account.', cors);
    }

    let certKey = rec.certificate_object_key;
    let mapKey = rec.star_map_object_key;

    // Need generation? Only possible when storage is configured.
    const needKey = type === 'certificate' ? !certKey : !mapKey;
    if (needKey) {
      if (!r2Configured(env)) {
        return ok({ ok: false, code: 'TSDR_STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE',
          message: 'Downloadable files are not enabled on this deployment yet. You can still ' +
            'open the certificate page and use Print / Save as PDF.' }, cors);
      }
      const res = await issueImpl({ db, env, dedicationId: rec.dedication_id, deps3, reason: 'initial' });
      if (res && res.skipped) {
        return ok({ ok: false, code: 'TSDR_STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE' }, cors);
      }
      certKey = (res && res.certificate_key) || certKey;
      mapKey = (res && res.star_map_key) || mapKey;
    }

    const objectKey = type === 'certificate' ? certKey : mapKey;
    if (!objectKey) return fail(404, ERR.NOT_FOUND, 'Artefact not available.', cors);

    const url = signImpl(objectKey, 300, env); // 5-minute link
    return ok({ ok: true, url, expires_in: 300 }, cors);
  } catch (e) {
    if (/R2 env/.test(e && e.message || '')) return fail(503, ERR.INTERNAL, 'Storage not configured.', cors);
    return fail(500, ERR.INTERNAL, 'Could not prepare your download just now.', cors);
  }
}

export async function handler(event) {
  const { getServiceClient } = await import('./_shared/supabase.mjs');
  const { realArtifactDeps } = await import('./_shared/artifacts.mjs');
  const db = await getServiceClient();
  const verifyImpl = async (jwt) => {
    const { data, error } = await db.auth.getUser(jwt);
    if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
    return { user: data.user };
  };
  // Only wire the heavy libs when storage is actually configured.
  const deps3 = r2Configured(process.env) ? await realArtifactDeps() : undefined;
  return core(event, { db, hitImpl: supabaseHitImpl(db), verifyImpl, deps3 });
}

=====================================================
FILE: netlify\functions\admin.mjs
=====================================================
/**
 * POST /.netlify/functions/admin
 * Secure admin operations. Requires a valid Supabase JWT whose email is in the
 * TSDR_ADMIN_ALLOWED_EMAILS allow-list. Every mutating action is audited by the
 * underlying SECURITY DEFINER RPC (registry_events).
 * Body: { action, ...args }
 *   action in { metrics, correct, set_status, review }
 * Dangerous actions (set_status) require { confirm: true }.
 */
import { ok, fail, ERR, requireMethod, parseBody } from './_shared/response.mjs';
import { corsHeaders, handlePreflight, cleanCors } from './_shared/cors.mjs';
import { getBearer, requireUser, isAdminEmail } from './_shared/auth.mjs';
import { cleanText, normalizeRegNo } from './_shared/sanitize.mjs';
import { rpc } from './_shared/supabase.mjs';

export async function core(event, deps) {
  const { db, verifyImpl, env = process.env } = deps;

  const pre = handlePreflight(event, env);
  if (pre) return pre;
  const cors = cleanCors(corsHeaders(event, env));

  const bad = requireMethod(event, 'POST');
  if (bad) return { ...bad, headers: { ...bad.headers, ...cors } };

  const jwt = getBearer(event);
  const { user, error } = await requireUser(jwt, verifyImpl);
  if (error || !user) return fail(401, ERR.UNAUTHORIZED, 'Sign in required.', cors);
  if (!isAdminEmail(user.email, env)) return fail(403, ERR.FORBIDDEN, 'Admins only.', cors);

  const [body, perr] = parseBody(event);
  if (perr) return { ...perr, headers: { ...perr.headers, ...cors } };

  const action = cleanText(body.action);
  const admin = user.email;

  try {
    switch (action) {
      case 'metrics': {
        const m = await rpc(db, 'tsdr_admin_metrics', {});
        return ok({ ok: true, metrics: m }, cors);
      }
      case 'correct': {
        const { regno, ok: valid } = normalizeRegNo(body.registration_number);
        if (!valid) return fail(400, ERR.VALIDATION, 'Bad registration number.', cors);
        await rpc(db, 'tsdr_admin_correct', {
          p_admin: admin,
          p_registration_number: regno,
          p_field: cleanText(body.field),
          p_new_value: cleanText(body.new_value),
          p_reason: cleanText(body.reason) || 'admin correction',
        });
        return ok({ ok: true }, cors);
      }
      case 'set_status': {
        if (body.confirm !== true)
          return fail(400, ERR.VALIDATION, 'Confirmation required for status change.', cors);
        const { regno, ok: valid } = normalizeRegNo(body.registration_number);
        if (!valid) return fail(400, ERR.VALIDATION, 'Bad registration number.', cors);
        await rpc(db, 'tsdr_admin_set_status', {
          p_admin: admin,
          p_registration_number: regno,
          p_status: cleanText(body.status),
          p_reason: cleanText(body.reason) || 'admin action',
        });
        return ok({ ok: true }, cors);
      }
      case 'get_price': {
        const p = await rpc(db, 'tsdr_get_price', {});
        const amount = Number(Array.isArray(p) ? (p[0]?.tsdr_get_price ?? p[0]) : p);
        return ok({ ok: true, price_inr: amount }, cors);
      }
      case 'set_price': {
        const amount = Number(body.amount);
        if (!Number.isFinite(amount) || amount < 0)
          return fail(400, ERR.VALIDATION, 'A valid non-negative price is required.', cors);
        const p = await rpc(db, 'tsdr_admin_set_price', { p_admin: admin, p_amount: amount });
        const saved = Number(Array.isArray(p) ? (p[0]?.tsdr_admin_set_price ?? p[0]) : p);
        return ok({ ok: true, price_inr: saved }, cors);
      }
      case 'review': {
        const { regno, ok: valid } = normalizeRegNo(body.registration_number);
        if (!valid) return fail(400, ERR.VALIDATION, 'Bad registration number.', cors);
        await rpc(db, 'tsdr_admin_review', {
          p_admin: admin,
          p_registration_number: regno,
          p_decision: cleanText(body.decision),
          p_notes: cleanText(body.notes) || '',
        });
        return ok({ ok: true }, cors);
      }
      default:
        return fail(400, ERR.BAD_REQUEST, 'Unknown action.', cors);
    }
  } catch (e) {
    const msg = e.pgError?.message || e.message || '';
    if (/NOT_FOUND/.test(msg)) return fail(404, ERR.NOT_FOUND, 'Record not found.', cors);
    if (/FIELD_NOT_CORRECTABLE|INVALID_STATUS|INVALID_DECISION/.test(msg))
      return fail(400, ERR.VALIDATION, msg, cors);
    return fail(500, ERR.INTERNAL, 'Admin action failed.', cors);
  }
}

export async function handler(event) {
  const { getServiceClient } = await import('./_shared/supabase.mjs');
  const db = await getServiceClient();
  const verifyImpl = async (jwt) => {
    const { data, error } = await db.auth.getUser(jwt);
    if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
    return { user: data.user };
  };
  return core(event, { db, verifyImpl });
}
=====================================================
FILE: netlify\functions\download.mjs
=====================================================
/**
 * GET /.netlify/functions/download?type=certificate|starmap&rn=...&t=...
 * Returns a short-lived signed R2 URL for a private artefact after authorising
 * the requester: public records allow certificate/starmap by rn; private/
 * unlisted require the access token. Never returns the object key or a public URL.
 */
import { ok, fail, ERR, requireMethod } from './_shared/response.mjs';
import { corsHeaders, handlePreflight, cleanCors } from './_shared/cors.mjs';
import { normalizeRegNo, cleanText } from './_shared/sanitize.mjs';
import { rpc } from './_shared/supabase.mjs';
import { clientKey, limit, supabaseHitImpl } from './_shared/ratelimit.mjs';
import { signedGet } from './_shared/r2.mjs';

export async function core(event, deps) {
  const { db, hitImpl, env = process.env, signImpl = signedGet } = deps;
  const pre = handlePreflight(event, env); if (pre) return pre;
  const cors = cleanCors(corsHeaders(event, env));
  const bad = requireMethod(event, 'GET'); if (bad) return { ...bad, headers: { ...bad.headers, ...cors } };

  const rl = await limit(hitImpl, clientKey(event, 'download'), 30, 300);
  if (!rl.allowed) return fail(429, ERR.RATE_LIMITED, 'Too many requests.',
    { ...cors, 'Retry-After': String(rl.retryAfter) });

  const q = event.queryStringParameters || {};
  const type = cleanText(q.type);
  if (!['certificate', 'starmap'].includes(type))
    return fail(400, ERR.VALIDATION, 'Invalid download type.', cors);
  const token = cleanText(q.t);
  const { regno, ok: valid } = normalizeRegNo(q.rn || '');

  try {
    const rows = await rpc(db, 'tsdr_artifact_key', {
      p_type: type,
      p_regno: valid ? regno : null,
      p_token: token || null,
    });
    const r = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows[0] : rows;
    if (!r || !r.object_key) return fail(404, ERR.NOT_FOUND, 'Artefact not available.', cors);

    const url = signImpl(r.object_key, 300, env); // 5-minute link
    return ok({ ok: true, url, expires_in: 300 }, cors);
  } catch (e) {
    if (/R2 env/.test(e.message || '')) return fail(503, ERR.INTERNAL, 'Storage not configured.', cors);
    return fail(500, ERR.INTERNAL, 'Could not create a download link.', cors);
  }
}

export async function handler(event) {
  const { getServiceClient } = await import('./_shared/supabase.mjs');
  const db = await getServiceClient();
  return core(event, { db, hitImpl: supabaseHitImpl(db) });
}
=====================================================
FILE: netlify\functions\draft.mjs
=====================================================
/**
 * POST /.netlify/functions/draft
 * Creates a DRAFT dedication bound to a valid held reservation.
 * Body: { reservation_id, reservation_token, purchaser_user_id?,
 *         honouree_name, symbolic_star_name, dedicator_name?, occasion,
 *         dedication_date?, dedication_message?, privacy_level,
 *         buyer_email (required), buyer_phone? }
 * Returns: { ok, dedication_id, keepsake_token }
 *   The keepsake_token is generated here (32 bytes), its sha-256 hash is stored
 *   server-side (private_access_token_hash), and the RAW token is returned ONCE
 *   to the browser so it can open the reveal + certificate for ANY privacy level
 *   via ?rn=..&t=.. links after activation. The raw token is never stored.
 * No registration number is assigned here -- only verified payment (or a 100%
 * promo) activates it.
 */
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import { ok, fail, ERR, requireMethod, parseBody } from './_shared/response.mjs';
import { corsHeaders, handlePreflight, cleanCors } from './_shared/cors.mjs';
import { validateDedication, validateEmail, cleanText } from './_shared/sanitize.mjs';
import { rpc } from './_shared/supabase.mjs';
import { clientKey, limit, supabaseHitImpl } from './_shared/ratelimit.mjs';

export async function core(event, deps) {
  const { db, hitImpl, env = process.env } = deps;

  const pre = handlePreflight(event, env);
  if (pre) return pre;
  const cors = cleanCors(corsHeaders(event, env));

  const bad = requireMethod(event, 'POST');
  if (bad) return { ...bad, headers: { ...bad.headers, ...cors } };

  const rl = await limit(hitImpl, clientKey(event, 'draft'), 30, 600);
  if (!rl.allowed) return fail(429, ERR.RATE_LIMITED, 'Too many attempts.',
    { ...cors, 'Retry-After': String(rl.retryAfter) });

  const [body, perr] = parseBody(event);
  if (perr) return { ...perr, headers: { ...perr.headers, ...cors } };

  const reservationId = cleanText(body.reservation_id);
  const reservationToken = cleanText(body.reservation_token);
  if (!reservationId || !reservationToken) {
    return fail(400, ERR.VALIDATION, 'reservation_id and reservation_token are required.', cors);
  }

  // Buyer email is REQUIRED (delivery + lookup key). Phone is optional.
  const { email, ok: emailOk } = validateEmail(body.buyer_email);
  if (!emailOk) {
    return fail(400, ERR.VALIDATION, 'A valid email address is required so we can deliver your keepsake.', cors);
  }
  const buyerPhone = cleanText(body.buyer_phone).slice(0, 32) || null;

  const { value, errors } = validateDedication(body);
  if (errors.length) return fail(400, ERR.VALIDATION, errors.join('; '), cors);

  // Keepsake token: raw returned to client once; only the hash is stored.
  const keepsakeToken = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
  const keepsakeHash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(keepsakeToken).digest('hex');

  try {
    const dedId = await rpc(db, 'tsdr_create_draft_dedication', {
      p_reservation_id: reservationId,
      p_reservation_token: reservationToken,
      p_purchaser: body.purchaser_user_id || null,
      p_honouree: value.honouree_name,
      p_symbolic_name: value.symbolic_star_name,
      p_dedicator: value.dedicator_name || null,
      p_occasion: value.occasion,
      p_dedication_date: value.dedication_date,
      p_message: value.dedication_message || null,
      p_privacy: value.privacy_level,
      p_buyer_email: email,
      p_buyer_phone: buyerPhone,
      p_keepsake_token_hash: keepsakeHash,
    });
    // The RPC returns the dedication id (uuid) or a single-row wrapper.
    const dedicationId = (dedId && dedId.dedication_id) ||
      (Array.isArray(dedId) ? dedId[0] : dedId);
    return ok({ ok: true, dedication_id: dedicationId, keepsake_token: keepsakeToken }, cors);
  } catch (e) {
    const msg = e.pgError?.message || e.message || '';
    if (/RESERVATION_EXPIRED/.test(msg))
      return fail(409, ERR.RESERVATION_EXPIRED, 'Your star hold has expired. Please reserve again.', cors);
    if (/RESERVATION_(NOT_FOUND|NOT_HELD|TOKEN_MISMATCH)/.test(msg))
      return fail(400, ERR.RESERVATION_INVALID, 'Reservation is invalid.', cors);
    if (/BUYER_EMAIL_REQUIRED/.test(msg))
      return fail(400, ERR.VALIDATION, 'A valid email address is required.', cors);
    return fail(500, ERR.INTERNAL, 'Could not create the dedication draft.', cors);
  }
}

export async function handler(event) {
  const { getServiceClient } = await import('./_shared/supabase.mjs');
  const db = await getServiceClient();
  return core(event, { db, hitImpl: supabaseHitImpl(db) });
}

/**
 * Scheduled function -- expires stale reservations and frees their stars.
 * Configured via netlify.toml [functions."expire-reservations"] schedule.
 * Event-driven cleanup (no client polling), keeping costs low.
 */
import { rpc } from './_shared/supabase.mjs';

export async function core(deps) {
  const { db } = deps;
  const freed = await rpc(db, 'tsdr_expire_reservations', {});
  return { freed: Number(freed) || 0 };
}

export async function handler() {

  const db = await getServiceClient();

    const { freed } = await core({ db });

  }

FILE: netlify\functions\explore-public.mjs

/**
 * GET /.netlify/functions/explore-public
 * Returns a small list of PUBLIC, review-passed dedications for the Explore page.
 * Reads ONLY the public-safe view (v_public_dedications) via an RPC -- never
 * private/unlisted records, never sensitive fields. Cached briefly at the edge.
 */
import { ok, fail, ERR, requireMethod } from './_shared/response.mjs';
import { corsHeaders, handlePreflight, cleanCors } from './_shared/cors.mjs';
import { rpc } from './_shared/supabase.mjs';

export async function core(event, deps) {
  const { db, env = process.env } = deps;
  const pre = handlePreflight(event, env); if (pre) return pre;
  const cors = cleanCors(corsHeaders(event, env));
  const bad = requireMethod(event, 'GET'); if (bad) return { ...bad, headers: { ...bad.headers, ...cors } };

  try {
    const rows = await rpc(db, 'tsdr_explore_public', { p_limit: 24 });
    return ok({ ok: true, dedications: rows || [] },
      { ...cors, 'Cache-Control': 'public, max-age=120' });
  } catch {
    // Fail soft: the page falls back to labelled demo records.
    return ok({ ok: true, dedications: [] }, cors);
  }
}

export async function handler(event) {
  const { getServiceClient } = await import('./_shared/supabase.mjs');
  const db = await getServiceClient();
  return core(event, { db });
}
=====================================================
FILE: netlify\functions\generate-certificate.mjs
=====================================================
/**
 * POST /.netlify/functions/generate-certificate
 * Internal/admin certificate + star-map (re)generation. Gated by a shared secret
 * (X-TSDR-Internal) so anonymous users can never trigger heavy generation here.
 * The buyer-facing, idempotent, authorised path is issue-artifacts.mjs; both now
 * share the same generator (_shared/artifacts.mjs), so behaviour is identical.
 * Heavy deps (pdf-lib/sharp) are imported lazily so cold starts stay cheap.
 */
import { ok, fail, ERR, requireMethod, parseBody } from './_shared/response.mjs';
import { issueArtifacts, r2Configured } from './_shared/artifacts.mjs';

export async function core(event, deps) {
  const { db, env = process.env, deps3 } = deps; // deps3 injects libs for tests
  const bad = requireMethod(event, 'POST'); if (bad) return bad;

  // internal-only guard
  const secret = (event.headers || {})['x-tsdr-internal'] || (event.headers || {})['X-TSDR-Internal'];
  if (!env.TSDR_INTERNAL_SECRET || secret !== env.TSDR_INTERNAL_SECRET) {
    return fail(401, ERR.UNAUTHORIZED, 'Internal endpoint.');
  }

  const [body, perr] = parseBody(event); if (perr) return perr;
  const dedicationId = String(body.dedication_id || '');
  if (!dedicationId) return fail(400, ERR.VALIDATION, 'dedication_id required.');

  if (!r2Configured(env)) {
    return fail(503, ERR.INTERNAL, 'Storage (R2) is not configured on this deployment.');
  }

  try {
    const res = await issueArtifacts({ db, env, dedicationId, deps3, reason: body.reason || 'initial' });
    return ok({ ok: true, certificate_key: res.certificate_key, star_map_key: res.star_map_key,
      certificate_hash: res.certificate_hash, version: res.version });
  } catch (e) {
    if (e.code === 'NOT_ACTIVE') return fail(404, ERR.NOT_FOUND, 'Dedication not found or not active.');
    return fail(500, ERR.INTERNAL, 'Certificate generation failed: ' + (e.message || ''));
  }
}

export async function handler(event) {
  const { getServiceClient } = await import('./_shared/supabase.mjs');
  const { realArtifactDeps } = await import('./_shared/artifacts.mjs');
  const db = await getServiceClient();
  const deps3 = await realArtifactDeps();
  return core(event, { db, env: process.env, deps3 });
}
=====================================================
FILE: netlify\functions\issue-artifacts.mjs
=====================================================
/**
 * POST /.netlify/functions/issue-artifacts
 * Buyer-facing, idempotent "ensure my certificate PDF + star map exist".
 * Body: { registration_number?, keepsake_token? }  (one is required)
 * Authorisation mirrors downloads: a public record can be targeted by its
 * registration number; a private/unlisted record requires the keepsake token.
 * Returns:
 *   { ok:true, already:true, downloads:{...} }               already generated
 *   { ok:true, generated:true, downloads:{...} }             generated just now
 *   { ok:false, code:'TSDR_STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE' }            R2 not configured
 *        -> the client keeps the Run A HTML certificate (Print / Save as PDF).
 *
 * Heavy generation runs at most once per dedication (idempotent: if the keys
 * already exist we skip straight to returning the download descriptors). This
 * keeps PDF/sharp work OFF the payment path entirely.
 */
import { ok, fail, ERR, requireMethod, parseBody } from './_shared/response.mjs';
import { corsHeaders, handlePreflight, cleanCors } from './_shared/cors.mjs';
import { normalizeRegNo, cleanText } from './_shared/sanitize.mjs';
import { rpc } from './_shared/supabase.mjs';
import { clientKey, limit, supabaseHitImpl } from './_shared/ratelimit.mjs';
import { issueArtifacts, r2Configured } from './_shared/artifacts.mjs';

// Client-usable download descriptors (the actual signed URL comes from /download).
function downloadDescriptors(regno, token) {
  const q = (type) => '/.netlify/functions/download?type=' + type +
    (regno ? '&rn=' + encodeURIComponent(regno) : '') +
    (token ? '&t=' + encodeURIComponent(token) : '');
  return { certificate: q('certificate'), starmap: q('starmap') };
}

export async function core(event, deps) {
  const { db, hitImpl, env = process.env, deps3, issueImpl = issueArtifacts } = deps;
  const pre = handlePreflight(event, env); if (pre) return pre;
  const cors = cleanCors(corsHeaders(event, env));
  const bad = requireMethod(event, 'POST'); if (bad) return { ...bad, headers: { ...bad.headers, ...cors } };

  // Generation is expensive: keep the limit tight but idempotent-friendly.
  const rl = await limit(hitImpl, clientKey(event, 'issue'), 12, 600);
  if (!rl.allowed) return fail(429, ERR.RATE_LIMITED, 'Too many requests.',
    { ...cors, 'Retry-After': String(rl.retryAfter) });

  const [body, perr] = parseBody(event);
  if (perr) return { ...perr, headers: { ...perr.headers, ...cors } };

  const token = cleanText(body.keepsake_token);
  const { regno, ok: valid } = normalizeRegNo(body.registration_number || '');
  if (!token && !valid) {
    return fail(400, ERR.VALIDATION, 'A keepsake token or registration number is required.', cors);
  }

  try {
    // Resolve the dedication + any existing artifact keys, honouring privacy.
    const rows = await rpc(db, 'tsdr_artifacts_target', {
      p_regno: valid ? regno : null,
      p_token: token || null,
    });
    const t = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows[0] : rows;
    if (!t || !t.dedication_id) {
      return fail(404, ERR.NOT_FOUND, 'No matching active dedication.', cors);
    }

    const downloads = downloadDescriptors(valid ? regno : null, token || null);

    // Already generated -> return immediately (no heavy work).
    if (t.certificate_object_key && t.star_map_object_key) {
      return ok({ ok: true, already: true, downloads }, cors);
    }

    // Storage not configured -> graceful, honest (client keeps HTML certificate).
    if (!r2Configured(env)) {
      return ok({ ok: false, code: 'TSDR_STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE',
        message: 'Downloadable PDF is not enabled yet on this deployment. You can still ' +
          'print or save the certificate from this page.' }, cors);
    }

    const res = await issueImpl({ db, env, dedicationId: t.dedication_id, deps3, reason: 'initial' });
    if (res && res.skipped) {
      return ok({ ok: false, code: 'TSDR_STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE' }, cors);
    }
    return ok({ ok: true, generated: true, downloads }, cors);
  } catch (e) {
    if (e && e.code === 'NOT_ACTIVE') return fail(404, ERR.NOT_FOUND, 'Dedication not active.', cors);
    return fail(500, ERR.INTERNAL, 'Could not prepare downloads just now.', cors);
  }
}

export async function handler(event) {
  const { getServiceClient } = await import('./_shared/supabase.mjs');
  const { realArtifactDeps } = await import('./_shared/artifacts.mjs');
  const db = await getServiceClient();
  // Only wire the heavy libs when storage is actually configured.
  const deps3 = r2Configured(process.env) ? await realArtifactDeps() : undefined;
  return core(event, { db, hitImpl: supabaseHitImpl(db), deps3 });
}
=====================================================
FILE: netlify\functions\keepsake.mjs
=====================================================
/**
 * GET /.netlify/functions/keepsake?rn=..&t=..
 * Returns the FULL public-safe record for the HTML certificate + keepsake page.
 * Authorisation:
 *   - ?t=<keepsake token>  -> any privacy level (owner link), or
 *   - ?rn=<reg number>     -> public, review-passed records only.
 * Never returns buyer email/phone, order refs, or the token. Rate-limited.
 */
import { ok, fail, ERR, requireMethod } from './_shared/response.mjs';
import { corsHeaders, handlePreflight, cleanCors } from './_shared/cors.mjs';
import { normalizeRegNo, cleanText } from './_shared/sanitize.mjs';
import { rpc } from './_shared/supabase.mjs';
import { clientKey, limit, supabaseHitImpl } from './_shared/ratelimit.mjs';

export async function core(event, deps) {
  const { db, hitImpl, env = process.env } = deps;
  const pre = handlePreflight(event, env); if (pre) return pre;
  const cors = cleanCors(corsHeaders(event, env));
  const bad = requireMethod(event, 'GET'); if (bad) return { ...bad, headers: { ...bad.headers, ...cors } };

  const rl = await limit(hitImpl, clientKey(event, 'keepsake'), 60, 300);
  if (!rl.allowed) return fail(429, ERR.RATE_LIMITED, 'Too many requests.',
    { ...cors, 'Retry-After': String(rl.retryAfter) });

  const q = event.queryStringParameters || {};
  const token = cleanText(q.t);
  const { regno, ok: valid } = normalizeRegNo(q.rn || '');
  if (!token && !valid) {
    return fail(400, ERR.BAD_REQUEST, 'Provide a keepsake token or a registration number.', cors);
  }

  try {
    const rows = await rpc(db, 'tsdr_certificate_view', {
      p_regno: valid ? regno : null,
      p_token: token || null,
    });
    const r = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows[0] : rows;
    if (!r) return fail(404, ERR.NOT_FOUND, 'This keepsake is unavailable or private.', cors);

    return ok({
      ok: true,
      registration_number: r.registration_number,
      symbolic_star_name: r.symbolic_star_name,
      honouree_name: r.honouree_name,
      dedicator_name: r.dedicator_name,
      dedication_message: r.dedication_message,
      occasion: r.occasion,
      dedication_date: r.dedication_date || null,
      privacy_level: r.privacy_level,
      constellation: r.constellation,
      source_catalog: r.source_catalog,
      source_identifier: r.source_identifier,
      right_ascension: r.right_ascension,
      declination: r.declination,
      apparent_magnitude: r.apparent_magnitude ?? null,
      visibility_class: r.visibility_class,
      activated_at: r.activated_at || null,
    }, cors);
  } catch {
    return fail(500, ERR.INTERNAL, 'Keepsake is temporarily unavailable.', cors);
  }
}

export async function handler(event) {
  const { getServiceClient } = await import('./_shared/supabase.mjs');
  const db = await getServiceClient();
  return core(event, { db, hitImpl: supabaseHitImpl(db) });
}
=====================================================
FILE: netlify\functions\media-upload.mjs
=====================================================
/**
 * POST /.netlify/functions/media-upload
 * Issues a short-lived signed PUT URL for direct-to-R2 upload of dedication
 * media, after validating type/size and ownership. The browser uploads to R2
 * with the signed URL; no media bytes pass through the function (keeps it cheap).
 * Body: { dedication_id, media_type: 'image'|'audio'|'video', mime, bytes }
 * Returns: { ok, upload_url, object_key, expires_in }
 */
import { ok, fail, ERR, requireMethod, parseBody } from './_shared/response.mjs';
import { corsHeaders, handlePreflight, cleanCors } from './_shared/cors.mjs';
import { getBearer, requireUser } from './_shared/auth.mjs';
import { cleanText } from './_shared/sanitize.mjs';
import { rpc } from './_shared/supabase.mjs';
import { signedPut } from './_shared/r2.mjs';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';

const LIMITS = {
  image: { max: 8 * 1024 * 1024, mimes: ['image/jpeg', 'image/png', 'image/webp'] },
  audio: { max: 20 * 1024 * 1024, mimes: ['audio/mpeg', 'audio/mp4', 'audio/aac', 'audio/ogg'] },
  video: { max: 80 * 1024 * 1024, mimes: ['video/mp4', 'video/webm'] },
};

export async function core(event, deps) {
  const { db, verifyImpl, env = process.env, signImpl = signedPut } = deps;
  const pre = handlePreflight(event, env); if (pre) return pre;
  const cors = cleanCors(corsHeaders(event, env));
  const bad = requireMethod(event, 'POST'); if (bad) return { ...bad, headers: { ...bad.headers, ...cors } };

  const jwt = getBearer(event);
  const { user, error } = await requireUser(jwt, verifyImpl);
  if (error || !user) return fail(401, ERR.UNAUTHORIZED, 'Sign in required.', cors);

  const [body, perr] = parseBody(event);
  if (perr) return { ...perr, headers: { ...perr.headers, ...cors } };

  const dedicationId = cleanText(body.dedication_id);
  const mediaType = cleanText(body.media_type);
  const mime = cleanText(body.mime);
  const bytes = Number(body.bytes || 0);

  const spec = LIMITS[mediaType];
  if (!spec) return fail(400, ERR.VALIDATION, 'Unsupported media_type.', cors);
  if (!spec.mimes.includes(mime)) return fail(400, ERR.VALIDATION, `Unsupported ${mediaType} type.`, cors);
  if (!(bytes > 0 && bytes <= spec.max))
    return fail(400, ERR.VALIDATION, `File too large (max ${(spec.max / 1048576) | 0} MB).`, cors);

  try {
    // Verify ownership + get an object key + persist a pending media row (server-side).
    const key = `media/${dedicationId}/${crypto.randomBytes(12).toString('hex')}`;
    const owned = await rpc(db, 'tsdr_media_register', {
      p_user: user.id, p_dedication_id: dedicationId, p_media_type: mediaType,
      p_object_key: key, p_mime: mime, p_bytes: bytes,
    });
    if (!owned) return fail(403, ERR.FORBIDDEN, 'You do not own this dedication.', cors);

    const uploadUrl = signImpl(key, 600, env);
    return ok({ ok: true, upload_url: uploadUrl, object_key: key, expires_in: 600 }, cors);
  } catch (e) {
    const msg = e.pgError?.message || e.message || '';
    if (/NOT_OWNER|FORBIDDEN/.test(msg)) return fail(403, ERR.FORBIDDEN, 'Not permitted.', cors);
    if (/R2 env/.test(msg)) return fail(503, ERR.INTERNAL, 'Media storage not configured.', cors);
    return fail(500, ERR.INTERNAL, 'Could not start upload.', cors);
  }
}

export async function handler(event) {
  const { getServiceClient } = await import('./_shared/supabase.mjs');
  const db = await getServiceClient();
  const verifyImpl = async (jwt) => {
    const { data, error } = await db.auth.getUser(jwt);
    if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
    return { user: data.user };
  };
  return core(event, { db, verifyImpl });
}
=====================================================
FILE: netlify\functions\pay-init.mjs
=====================================================
/**
 * POST /.netlify/functions/pay-init
 * Creates a Cashfree order for a pending dedication and returns the
 * payment_session_id for the client SDK. The server computes the price -- the
 * client never sends the amount (prevents amount tampering).
 * Body: { dedication_id, customer_phone?, promo_code? }
 * Returns: { ok, order_id, payment_session_id }  (paid path)
 *          { ok, free, registration_number, keepsake_token }  (100% promo path)
 *
 * Run A changes:
 *   - Price is read from an ADMIN-EDITABLE source (tsdr_get_price), not hard-coded.
 *     PRICE_INR remains only as a safe fallback if the RPC is unavailable.
 *   - Cashfree-absent is handled GRACEFULLY: when the gateway keys are not set we
 *     do NOT throw a 502. We return ok:false + TSDR_PAYMENTS_UNAVAILABLE with a
 *     clear, resumable message (the dedication stays pending_payment, so the buyer
 *     can retry later or use the free promo path). No "clear cache and retry".
 *   - The free-promo path returns the keepsake_token so the client can open the
 *     reveal + certificate for any privacy level.
 */
import { ok, fail, ERR, requireMethod, parseBody } from './_shared/response.mjs';
import { corsHeaders, handlePreflight, cleanCors } from './_shared/cors.mjs';
import { cleanText } from './_shared/sanitize.mjs';
import { rpc } from './_shared/supabase.mjs';
import { clientKey, limit, supabaseHitImpl } from './_shared/ratelimit.mjs';
import { createOrder } from './_shared/cashfree.mjs';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';

// Fallback only -- the authoritative price is tsdr_get_price() (migration 0012).
export const PRICE_INR = { standard: 1499.00 };

// Read the admin-editable price; fall back to PRICE_INR.standard on any error.
async function serverPrice(db) {
  try {
    const p = await rpc(db, 'tsdr_get_price', {});
    const n = Number(Array.isArray(p) ? (p[0]?.tsdr_get_price ?? p[0]) : p);
    if (Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 0) return n;
  } catch { /* fall through to default */ }
  return PRICE_INR.standard;
}

// True only when a live Cashfree order can actually be created.
function cashfreeConfigured(env) {
  return Boolean(env.CASHFREE_APP_ID && env.CASHFREE_SECRET_KEY);
}

export async function core(event, deps) {
  const { db, hitImpl, env = process.env, fetchImpl = globalThis.fetch,
    createOrderImpl = createOrder } = deps;

  const pre = handlePreflight(event, env);
  if (pre) return pre;
  const cors = cleanCors(corsHeaders(event, env));

  const bad = requireMethod(event, 'POST');
  if (bad) return { ...bad, headers: { ...bad.headers, ...cors } };

  const rl = await limit(hitImpl, clientKey(event, 'pay'), 20, 600);
  if (!rl.allowed) return fail(429, ERR.RATE_LIMITED, 'Too many attempts.',
    { ...cors, 'Retry-After': String(rl.retryAfter) });

  const [body, perr] = parseBody(event);
  if (perr) return { ...perr, headers: { ...perr.headers, ...cors } };

  const dedicationId = cleanText(body.dedication_id);
  if (!dedicationId) return fail(400, ERR.VALIDATION, 'dedication_id is required.', cors);

  const promoCode = cleanText(body.promo_code); // optional

  try {
    // Verify the dedication exists & is pending (server-side; never trust client).
    const ded = await rpc(db, 'tsdr_get_pending_for_payment', { p_dedication_id: dedicationId });
    const row = Array.isArray(ded) ? ded[0] : ded;
    if (!row) return fail(404, ERR.NOT_FOUND, 'Dedication not found or not payable.', cors);

    let amount = await serverPrice(db);        // admin-editable, server-authoritative
    const currency = 'INR';
    let discountPercent = 0;

    // ---- Optional promo: validate + apply against the SERVER price ----
    if (promoCode) {
      const q = await rpc(db, 'tsdr_promo_quote', { p_code: promoCode, p_amount: amount });
      const quote = Array.isArray(q) ? q[0] : q;
      if (!quote || !quote.valid) {
        const reason = (quote && quote.reason) || 'PROMO_INVALID';
        return fail(400, ERR.VALIDATION, 'Promo code is not valid (' + reason + ').', cors);
      }
      amount = Number(quote.final_amount);
      discountPercent = Number(quote.percent_off) || 0;

      // ---- 100%-off -> complete for FREE, skipping Cashfree entirely ----
      if (amount <= 0) {
        const idem = 'promo:' + dedicationId + ':' + promoCode.toUpperCase();
        const rows = await rpc(db, 'tsdr_redeem_free_dedication', {
          p_dedication_id: dedicationId,
          p_code: promoCode,
          p_idempotency_key: idem,
        });
        const r = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows[0] : rows;
        return ok({
          ok: true,
          free: true,
          registration_number: r.registration_number,
          already_processed: r.already_processed,
          discount_percent: discountPercent,
        }, cors);
      }
    }

    // ---- Paid path requires Cashfree keys. Handle their absence gracefully. ----
    if (!cashfreeConfigured(env)) {
      // Not an error state we should 5xx on: the dedication is safely pending and
      // resumable. Tell the buyer plainly and offer the free/testing path.
      return ok({
        ok: false,
        code: 'TSDR_PAYMENTS_UNAVAILABLE',
        resumable: true,
        dedication_id: dedicationId,
        amount,
        message: 'Card payments are not enabled on this deployment yet. Your dedication ' +
          'is saved and can be completed later. For testing, apply a 100% promo code ' +
          'to finish now.',
      }, cors);
    }

    // Order id != registration number, and unpredictable.
    const orderId = 'TSDRORD-' + crypto.randomBytes(12).toString('hex');

    // Persist the intended order + expected amount so the webhook can match.
    // (Idempotent + re-attachable while the dedication is still pending, so a
    //  repeat/second attempt resumes cleanly rather than failing.)
    await rpc(db, 'tsdr_attach_order', {
      p_dedication_id: dedicationId,
      p_order_reference: orderId,
      p_amount: amount,
      p_currency: currency,
    });

    const base = env.TSDR_PUBLIC_BASE_URL;
    let order;
    try {
      order = await createOrderImpl({
        orderId,
        amount,
        currency,
        customerId: dedicationId,
        customerPhone: cleanText(body.customer_phone) || '0000000000',
        returnUrl: `${base}/reveal.html?order={order_id}`,
        notifyUrl: `${base}/.netlify/functions/pay-webhook`,
      }, env, fetchImpl);
    } catch (ce) {
      // Gateway reachable-but-failed: still resumable, still not a scary 502.
      return ok({
        ok: false,
        code: 'TSDR_PAYMENTS_UNAVAILABLE',
        resumable: true,
        dedication_id: dedicationId,
        amount,
        message: 'We could not start the secure checkout just now. Your dedication is ' +
          'saved -- please try again in a moment.',
        detail: (ce && ce.message) ? String(ce.message).slice(0, 160) : undefined,
      }, cors);
    }

    return ok({
      ok: true,
      order_id: orderId,
      payment_session_id: order.payment_session_id,
      amount,
      discount_percent: discountPercent,
    }, cors);
  } catch (e) {
    const msg = e.pgError?.message || e.message || '';
    if (/NOT_PENDING/.test(msg))
      return fail(409, ERR.DEDICATION_NOT_PENDING, 'This dedication is not awaiting payment.', cors);
    if (/PROMO_(INVALID|INACTIVE|EXPIRED|EXHAUSTED|NOT_FREE)/.test(msg))
      return fail(400, ERR.VALIDATION, 'Promo code could not be applied (' + msg + ').', cors);
    return fail(502, ERR.INTERNAL, 'Payment initialisation failed.', cors);
  }
}

export async function handler(event) {
  const { getServiceClient } = await import('./_shared/supabase.mjs');
  const db = await getServiceClient();
  return core(event, { db, hitImpl: supabaseHitImpl(db) });
}
=====================================================
FILE: netlify\functions\pay-status.mjs
=====================================================
/**
 * GET /.netlify/functions/pay-status?order=<order_reference>   (Run D)
 * -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * The Cashfree return URL is /reveal.html?order=<order_reference>. Because the
 * webhook activates asynchronously, the returning buyer's browser polls this
 * endpoint to learn when the dedication is active and to obtain the registration
 * number, then routes to the reveal (public: rn alone; private/unlisted: rn +
 * the keepsake token the client stored locally before checkout).
 *
 * Returns ONLY public-safe status (no names/message/PII/token/coords):
 *   { ok:true, status:'pending_payment', registration_number:null }
 *   { ok:true, status:'active', registration_number:'TSDR-2026-XXXXXXXX',
 *     privacy_level:'public' }
 *   404 when the order reference is unknown.
 *
 * Never says "clear cache": pending is a normal, resumable state -- the client
 * keeps polling briefly, then offers a manual "check again".
 */
import { ok, fail, ERR, requireMethod } from './_shared/response.mjs';
import { corsHeaders, handlePreflight, cleanCors } from './_shared/cors.mjs';
import { cleanText } from './_shared/sanitize.mjs';
import { rpc } from './_shared/supabase.mjs';
import { clientKey, limit, supabaseHitImpl } from './_shared/ratelimit.mjs';

export async function core(event, deps) {
  const { db, hitImpl, env = process.env } = deps;
  const pre = handlePreflight(event, env); if (pre) return pre;
  const cors = cleanCors(corsHeaders(event, env));
  const bad = requireMethod(event, 'GET'); if (bad) return { ...bad, headers: { ...bad.headers, ...cors } };

  // Polling-friendly but bounded: 60 checks / 5 min per client.
  const rl = await limit(hitImpl, clientKey(event, 'paystatus'), 60, 300);
  if (!rl.allowed) return fail(429, ERR.RATE_LIMITED, 'Too many requests.',
    { ...cors, 'Retry-After': String(rl.retryAfter) });

  const q = event.queryStringParameters || {};
  const order = cleanText(q.order);
  if (!order) return fail(400, ERR.VALIDATION, 'An order reference is required.', cors);

  try {
    const rows = await rpc(db, 'tsdr_order_status', { p_order_reference: order });
    const r = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows[0] : rows;
    if (!r) return fail(404, ERR.NOT_FOUND, 'Order not found.', cors);
    return ok({
      ok: true,
      status: r.registration_status,
      registration_number: r.registration_number || null,
      privacy_level: r.privacy_level || null,
    }, cors);
  } catch {
    return fail(500, ERR.INTERNAL, 'Could not read the order status.', cors);
  }
}

export async function handler(event) {
  const { getServiceClient } = await import('./_shared/supabase.mjs');
  const db = await getServiceClient();
  return core(event, { db, hitImpl: supabaseHitImpl(db) });
}

=====================================================
FILE: netlify\functions\pay-webhook.mjs
=====================================================
/**
 * POST /.netlify/functions/pay-webhook   (server-to-server; Cashfree -> us)
 * -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * SECURITY GATES (in order):
 *   1. Verify HMAC signature over (x-webhook-timestamp + RAW body) using the
 *      Cashfree client secret. Reject on mismatch. (No CORS -- not a browser.)
 *   2. Only act on SUCCESS payment events.
 *   3. Match order_id -> dedication and assert amount & currency equal the
 *      server-recorded expected values (blocks amount/currency tampering).
 *   4. Activate idempotently via tsdr_activate_paid_dedication(idempotency_key):
 *      duplicate / out-of-order webhooks are safe no-ops returning the same
 *      registration number.
 * Always returns 200 to Cashfree once the signature is valid and the event is
 * understood, so the gateway stops retrying; internal issues are logged/audited.
 * -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 */
import { json, fail, ERR } from './_shared/response.mjs';
import { verifyWebhookSignature, extractPaymentEvent } from './_shared/cashfree.mjs';
import { rpc } from './_shared/supabase.mjs';

export async function core(event, deps) {
  const { db, env = process.env } = deps;

  if ((event.httpMethod || '').toUpperCase() !== 'POST') {
    return fail(405, ERR.METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED, 'Use POST.');
  }

  const headers = event.headers || {};
  const signature = headers['x-webhook-signature'] || headers['X-Webhook-Signature'];
  const timestamp = headers['x-webhook-timestamp'] || headers['X-Webhook-Timestamp'];
  const rawBody = event.body || '';

  // GATE 1 -- signature
  if (!verifyWebhookSignature(rawBody, signature, timestamp, env)) {
    return fail(401, ERR.SIGNATURE_INVALID, 'Invalid signature.');
  }

  let payload;
  try { payload = JSON.parse(rawBody); }
  catch { return fail(400, ERR.BAD_REQUEST, 'Invalid JSON.'); }

  const evt = extractPaymentEvent(payload);

  // GATE 2 -- only successful payments activate. Record others for the audit.
  if (String(evt.paymentStatus).toUpperCase() !== 'SUCCESS') {
    try {
      await rpc(db, 'tsdr_record_payment_event', {
        p_order_reference: evt.orderId || null,
        p_provider: 'cashfree',
        p_provider_payment_id: evt.paymentId || null,
        p_status: (evt.paymentStatus || 'unknown').toLowerCase(),
        p_amount: evt.amount ?? 0,
        p_currency: evt.currency || 'INR',
      });
    } catch { /* non-fatal; ack anyway */ }
    return json(200, { ok: true, ignored: true, reason: 'non-success event recorded' });
  }

  // GATE 3 + 4 -- match amount/currency and activate idempotently, in one RPC.
  try {
    const idem = `cashfree:${evt.orderId}:${evt.paymentId}`;
    const rows = await rpc(db, 'tsdr_activate_by_order', {
      p_order_reference: evt.orderId,
      p_idempotency_key: idem,
      p_provider: 'cashfree',
      p_provider_payment_id: evt.paymentId || null,
      p_amount: evt.amount,
      p_currency: evt.currency,
    });
    const r = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows[0] : rows;
    return json(200, {
      ok: true,
      registration_number: r.registration_number,
      already_processed: r.already_processed,
    });
  } catch (e) {
    const msg = e.pgError?.message || e.message || '';
    if (/AMOUNT_MISMATCH/.test(msg))
      return fail(400, ERR.AMOUNT_MISMATCH, 'Amount mismatch.');
    if (/CURRENCY_MISMATCH/.test(msg))
      return fail(400, ERR.CURRENCY_MISMATCH, 'Currency mismatch.');
    if (/ORDER_NOT_FOUND/.test(msg))
      return fail(404, ERR.NOT_FOUND, 'Order not found.');
    // Unknown internal error: still 200 to avoid infinite retries, but flag it.
    return json(200, { ok: false, code: ERR.INTERNAL, message: 'Deferred for review.' });
  }
}

export async function handler(event) {
  const { getServiceClient } = await import('./_shared/supabase.mjs');
  const db = await getServiceClient();
  return core(event, { db });
}
=====================================================
FILE: netlify\functions\public-config.mjs
=====================================================
/* =============================================================================
   TSDR -- dynamic public config, served from Netlify ENV VARS.
   File: netlify/functions/public-config.mjs

   WHY THIS EXISTS:
   The browser cannot read Netlify environment variables. A static
   /js/config.js kept failing to deploy (gitignored). This Function reads the
   SAME env vars you already set for the backend and returns them as the
   window.* globals the pages expect. So there is NO config.js file to manage.

   These values are PUBLIC and RLS-protected (anon key only) -- safe to expose.
   A netlify.toml redirect maps /js/config.js -> this function (see below).
   ============================================================================= */
export async function handler() {
  const url  = process.env.SUPABASE_URL || '';
  const anon = process.env.SUPABASE_ANON_KEY || '';
  const mode = process.env.TSDR_CF_MODE || 'sandbox';

  const body =
    'window.TSDR_SUPABASE_URL='  + JSON.stringify(url)  + ';\n' +
    'window.TSDR_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=' + JSON.stringify(anon) + ';\n' +
    'window.TSDR_CF_MODE=' + JSON.stringify(mode) + ';\n';

  return {
    statusCode: 200,
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/javascript; charset=utf-8',
      'Cache-Control': 'no-store',
    },
    body,
  };
}
=====================================================
FILE: netlify\functions\reserve.mjs
=====================================================
/**
 * POST /.netlify/functions/reserve
 * Atomically holds one eligible star for the caller's session.
 * Body: { session_ref, constellation?, visibility? }
 * Returns: { ok, reservation_id, reservation_token, star: {...}, expires_at }
 * The reservation_token is returned ONCE; the client must send it back to /draft.
 */
import { ok, fail, ERR, requireMethod, parseBody } from './_shared/response.mjs';
import { corsHeaders, handlePreflight, cleanCors } from './_shared/cors.mjs';
import { validateReservation, cleanText } from './_shared/sanitize.mjs';
import { rpc } from './_shared/supabase.mjs';
import { clientKey, limit, supabaseHitImpl } from './_shared/ratelimit.mjs';

export async function core(event, deps) {
  const { db, hitImpl, env = process.env } = deps;

  const pre = handlePreflight(event, env);
  if (pre) return pre;
  const cors = cleanCors(corsHeaders(event, env));

  const bad = requireMethod(event, 'POST');
  if (bad) return { ...bad, headers: { ...bad.headers, ...cors } };

  // Rate limit: 30 reservation attempts / 10 min per IP.
  const rl = await limit(hitImpl, clientKey(event, 'reserve'), 30, 600);
  if (!rl.allowed) return fail(429, ERR.RATE_LIMITED, 'Too many attempts. Please slow down.',
    { ...cors, 'Retry-After': String(rl.retryAfter) });

  const [body, perr] = parseBody(event);
  if (perr) return { ...perr, headers: { ...perr.headers, ...cors } };

  const sessionRef = cleanText(body.session_ref);
  if (!sessionRef) return fail(400, ERR.VALIDATION, 'session_ref is required.', cors);

  const { value, errors } = validateReservation(body);
  if (errors.length) return fail(400, ERR.VALIDATION, errors.join('; '), cors);

  try {
    const rows = await rpc(db, 'tsdr_reserve_star', {
      p_session_ref: sessionRef,
      p_constellation: value.constellation,
      p_visibility: value.visibility,
      p_hold_minutes: Number(env.TSDR_RESERVATION_MINUTES || 20),
    });
    const r = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows[0] : rows;
    return ok({
      ok: true,
      reservation_id: r.reservation_id,
      reservation_token: r.reservation_token,
      expires_at: r.expires_at,
      star: {
        source_identifier: r.source_identifier,
        constellation: r.constellation,
        right_ascension: r.right_ascension,
        declination: r.declination,
        visibility_class: r.visibility_class,
      },
    }, cors);
  } catch (e) {
    const msg = e.pgError?.message || e.message || '';
    if (/NO_STAR_AVAILABLE/.test(msg)) {
      return fail(409, ERR.NO_STAR_AVAILABLE,
        'No eligible star is available right now. Try a different preference.', cors);
    }
    return fail(500, ERR.INTERNAL, 'Could not reserve a star.', cors);
  }
}

// Netlify entrypoint (thin): wires real deps.
export async function handler(event) {
  const { getServiceClient } = await import('./_shared/supabase.mjs');
  const db = await getServiceClient();
  return core(event, { db, hitImpl: supabaseHitImpl(db) });
}
=====================================================
FILE: netlify\functions\reveal.mjs
=====================================================
/**
 * GET /.netlify/functions/reveal?t=<token>   (private/unlisted)
 * GET /.netlify/functions/reveal?rn=<regno>   (public only)
 * GET /.netlify/functions/reveal?rn=<regno>   with Authorization: Bearer <jwt>
 *                                             (OWNER replay, any privacy -- Run C)
 * Returns a privacy-filtered reveal payload for the reveal experience.
 *  - public: returns name/honouree/message/star + neighbours.
 *  - unlisted/private: requires the high-entropy access token (hash matched
 *    server-side) OR a verified owner JWT for a record the caller owns.
 *    rn alone (no token, no owner JWT) never unlocks a private record.
 * Rate-limited; never leaks tokens or internal ids.
 */
import { ok, fail, ERR, requireMethod } from './_shared/response.mjs';
import { corsHeaders, handlePreflight, cleanCors } from './_shared/cors.mjs';
import { normalizeRegNo, cleanText } from './_shared/sanitize.mjs';
import { getBearer, requireUser } from './_shared/auth.mjs';
import { rpc } from './_shared/supabase.mjs';
import { clientKey, limit, supabaseHitImpl } from './_shared/ratelimit.mjs';

export async function core(event, deps) {
  const { db, hitImpl, env = process.env, verifyImpl } = deps;
  const pre = handlePreflight(event, env); if (pre) return pre;
  const cors = cleanCors(corsHeaders(event, env));
  const bad = requireMethod(event, 'GET'); if (bad) return { ...bad, headers: { ...bad.headers, ...cors } };

  const rl = await limit(hitImpl, clientKey(event, 'reveal'), 40, 300);
  if (!rl.allowed) return fail(429, ERR.RATE_LIMITED, 'Too many requests.',
    { ...cors, 'Retry-After': String(rl.retryAfter) });

  const q = event.queryStringParameters || {};
  const token = cleanText(q.t);
  const { regno, ok: valid } = normalizeRegNo(q.rn || '');

  // OWNER replay (Run C): only when a Bearer JWT is present AND a verifier is
  // wired. The user id comes from the VERIFIED token, never the client body, so
  // this cannot read another buyer's record. Skipped entirely for token/public.
  const jwt = getBearer(event);

  try {
    let r = null;
    if (token) {
      const rows = await rpc(db, 'tsdr_reveal_by_token', { p_token: token });
      r = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows[0] : rows;
    } else if (jwt && verifyImpl && valid) {
      const { user } = await requireUser(jwt, verifyImpl);
      if (user) {
        const rows = await rpc(db, 'tsdr_owner_record', { p_user: user.id, p_regno: regno });
        r = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows[0] : rows;
      }
      // If the owner lookup found nothing (not theirs / not active), fall back to
      // the public path so a signed-in user viewing a public rn still works.
      if (!r) {
        const rows = await rpc(db, 'tsdr_reveal_public', { p_regno: regno });
        r = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows[0] : rows;
      }
    } else if (valid) {
      const rows = await rpc(db, 'tsdr_reveal_public', { p_regno: regno });
      r = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows[0] : rows;
    } else {
      return fail(400, ERR.BAD_REQUEST, 'Provide a reveal token or a registration number.', cors);
    }
    if (!r) return fail(404, ERR.NOT_FOUND, 'This dedication is unavailable or private.', cors);

    // neighbours for the viewer (public-safe star coords only)
    let neighbors = [];
    try {
      neighbors = await rpc(db, 'tsdr_star_neighbors', {
        p_ra: r.right_ascension, p_dec: r.declination, p_fov: 14, p_max: 200,
      }) || [];
    } catch { /* neighbours optional */ }

    return ok({
      ok: true,
      mode: r.reveal_mode || modeFromOccasion(r.occasion),
      symbolic_star_name: r.symbolic_star_name,
      honouree_name: r.honouree_name,
      dedicator_name: r.dedicator_name,
      dedication_message: r.dedication_message,
      occasion: r.occasion,
      dedication_date: r.dedication_date || null,
      constellation: r.constellation,
      source_identifier: r.source_identifier,
      right_ascension: r.right_ascension,
      declination: r.declination,
      apparent_magnitude: r.apparent_magnitude ?? null,
      visibility_class: r.visibility_class,
      registration_number: r.registration_number,
      neighbors,
    }, cors);
  } catch {
    return fail(500, ERR.INTERNAL, 'Reveal is temporarily unavailable.', cors);
  }
}

function modeFromOccasion(occ) {
  return ({
    wedding: 'romance', engagement: 'romance', anniversary: 'romance',
    memorial: 'memorial', newborn: 'newborn', friendship: 'friendship',
    graduation: 'achievement', appreciation: 'friendship', milestone: 'achievement',
  })[occ] || 'celebration';
}

export async function handler(event) {
  const { getServiceClient } = await import('./_shared/supabase.mjs');
  const db = await getServiceClient();
  const verifyImpl = async (jwt) => {
    const { data, error } = await db.auth.getUser(jwt);
    if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
    return { user: data.user };
  };
  return core(event, { db, hitImpl: supabaseHitImpl(db), verifyImpl });
}
=====================================================
FILE: netlify\functions\send-keepsake.mjs
=====================================================
/**
 * POST /.netlify/functions/send-keepsake
 * Emails the buyer their keepsake links (reveal + certificate + verify) after
 * activation. The buyer's email is stored server-side; the caller proves it may
 * trigger a send by presenting the keepsake token (hash-matched server-side).
 * Body: { registration_number, keepsake_token }
 * Returns:
 *   { ok:true, delivered:true }                       -> email sent via Resend
 *   { ok:true, delivered:false, reason:'email_not_configured', links:{...} }
 *        -> HONEST fallback: no key on this deploy; we return the links so the
 *           client can show them on-screen. We NEVER fake "sent".
 */
import { ok, fail, ERR, requireMethod, parseBody } from './_shared/response.mjs';
import { corsHeaders, handlePreflight, cleanCors } from './_shared/cors.mjs';
import { cleanText, normalizeRegNo } from './_shared/sanitize.mjs';
import { rpc } from './_shared/supabase.mjs';
import { clientKey, limit, supabaseHitImpl } from './_shared/ratelimit.mjs';
import { sendEmail, emailConfigured, keepsakeEmail } from './_shared/email.mjs';

function links(base, regno, token) {
  const t = encodeURIComponent(token);
  const rn = encodeURIComponent(regno);
  return {
    reveal: `${base}/reveal.html?rn=${rn}&t=${t}`,
    certificate: `${base}/certificate.html?rn=${rn}&t=${t}`,
    verify: `${base}/verify.html?rn=${rn}`,
  };
}

export async function core(event, deps) {
  const { db, hitImpl, env = process.env, sendImpl = sendEmail } = deps;
  const pre = handlePreflight(event, env); if (pre) return pre;
  const cors = cleanCors(corsHeaders(event, env));
  const bad = requireMethod(event, 'POST'); if (bad) return { ...bad, headers: { ...bad.headers, ...cors } };

  const rl = await limit(hitImpl, clientKey(event, 'sendkeepsake'), 10, 600);
  if (!rl.allowed) return fail(429, ERR.RATE_LIMITED, 'Too many requests.',
    { ...cors, 'Retry-After': String(rl.retryAfter) });

  const [body, perr] = parseBody(event);
  if (perr) return { ...perr, headers: { ...perr.headers, ...cors } };

  const token = cleanText(body.keepsake_token);
  const { regno, ok: valid } = normalizeRegNo(body.registration_number || '');
  if (!token) return fail(400, ERR.VALIDATION, 'keepsake_token is required.', cors);

  try {
    const rows = await rpc(db, 'tsdr_keepsake_contact', { p_token: token });
    const r = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows[0] : rows;
    if (!r || !r.registration_number) {
      return fail(404, ERR.NOT_FOUND, 'No matching active dedication for that token.', cors);
    }
    // If the client passed an rn too, it must match the token's record.
    if (valid && regno !== r.registration_number) {
      return fail(400, ERR.VALIDATION, 'Token does not match that registration number.', cors);
    }

    const base = env.TSDR_PUBLIC_BASE_URL || '';
    const url = links(base, r.registration_number, token);

    if (!emailConfigured(env) || !r.buyer_email) {
      // Honest fallback -- nothing was sent; return links for on-screen display.
      return ok({
        ok: true, delivered: false,
        reason: r.buyer_email ? 'email_not_configured' : 'no_buyer_email',
        links: url,
      }, cors);
    }

    const msg = keepsakeEmail({
      symbolicName: r.symbolic_star_name,
      honouree: r.honouree_name,
      revealUrl: url.reveal,
      certificateUrl: url.certificate,
      verifyUrl: url.verify,
    });
    const result = await sendImpl({ to: r.buyer_email, ...msg }, env);

    if (result.delivered) {
      return ok({ ok: true, delivered: true }, cors);
    }
    // Configured but the send failed -- be honest, return links as fallback.
    return ok({ ok: true, delivered: false, reason: result.reason || 'email_send_failed',
      links: url }, cors);
  } catch {
    return fail(500, ERR.INTERNAL, 'Could not process the keepsake delivery.', cors);
  }
}

export async function handler(event) {
  const { getServiceClient } = await import('./_shared/supabase.mjs');
  const db = await getServiceClient();
  return core(event, { db, hitImpl: supabaseHitImpl(db) });
}
=====================================================
FILE: netlify\functions\verify.mjs
=====================================================
/**
 * GET /.netlify/functions/verify?rn=TSDR-YYYY-XXXXXXXX
 * Enumeration-resistant, rate-limited public verification.
 * - Invalid format -> 400 (no DB hit).
 * - Not found OR private-but-existing -> identical 404 shape (no oracle).
 * - Found & safe -> returns whitelisted fields only (never names/message).
 */
import { ok, fail, ERR, requireMethod } from './_shared/response.mjs';
import { corsHeaders, handlePreflight, cleanCors } from './_shared/cors.mjs';
import { normalizeRegNo } from './_shared/sanitize.mjs';
import { rpc } from './_shared/supabase.mjs';
import { clientKey, limit, supabaseHitImpl } from './_shared/ratelimit.mjs';

export async function core(event, deps) {
  const { db, hitImpl, env = process.env } = deps;

  const pre = handlePreflight(event, env);
  if (pre) return pre;
  const cors = cleanCors(corsHeaders(event, env));

  const bad = requireMethod(event, 'GET');
  if (bad) return { ...bad, headers: { ...bad.headers, ...cors } };

  // Strict rate limit to resist brute-force enumeration: 15 / 5 min per IP.
  const rl = await limit(hitImpl, clientKey(event, 'verify'), 15, 300);
  if (!rl.allowed) return fail(429, ERR.RATE_LIMITED, 'Too many verification attempts.',
    { ...cors, 'Retry-After': String(rl.retryAfter) });

  const q = (event.queryStringParameters || {});
  const { regno, ok: valid } = normalizeRegNo(q.rn || q.registration_number || '');
  if (!valid) {
    return fail(400, ERR.BAD_REQUEST,
      'Please enter a registration number like TSDR-2026-XXXXXXXX.', cors);
  }

  try {
    const rows = await rpc(db, 'tsdr_verify', { p_regno: regno });
    const r = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows[0] : rows;
    // Uniform 404 for both "not found" and any record not exposed by the view.
    if (!r) return fail(404, ERR.NOT_FOUND, 'No active registration matches that number.', cors);

    return ok({
      ok: true,
      registration_number: r.registration_number,
      registration_status: r.registration_status,
      privacy_level: r.privacy_level,
      occasion: r.occasion,
      activated_at: r.activated_at,
      constellation: r.constellation,
      right_ascension: r.right_ascension,
      declination: r.declination,
      catalog: { source: r.source_catalog, identifier: r.source_identifier },
      is_public: r.is_public,
      has_message: r.has_message,
    }, cors);
  } catch {
    return fail(500, ERR.INTERNAL, 'Verification is temporarily unavailable.', cors);
  }
}

export async function handler(event) {
  const { getServiceClient } = await import('./_shared/supabase.mjs');
  const db = await getServiceClient();
  return core(event, { db, hitImpl: supabaseHitImpl(db) });
}

FILE: public\account\index.html

<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
<head>

/* =============================================================================
   TSDR -- HTML/CSS printable certificate (Run A). Pure external CSS (CSP-safe,
   no inline styles). Screen shows a premium framed certificate; @media print
   strips the page chrome so window.print() yields a clean A4 sheet.
   ============================================================================= */

  --c-navy:#070b16; --c-ink:#0a1122; --c-ivory:#f5f0e4; --c-dim:#cbc6b6;
  --c-gold:#c9a25f; --c-gold-soft:#e8cf94; --c-line:rgba(201,162,95,.4);
  --c-serif:"Cormorant Garamond","Iowan Old Style",Palatino,Georgia,serif;
  --c-sans:"Inter",system-ui,-apple-system,"Segoe UI",Roboto,sans-serif;
}

  backdrop-filter:blur(8px);border-bottom:1px solid var(--c-line);
}

/* =============================================================================
   THE STAR DEDICATION REGISTRY " Design System  v2 (visual upgrade)
   Premium - cinematic - "observatory-at-night" - institutional - accessible.
   Constraints honoured: NO inline styles (strict CSP style-src 'self'); ALL
   original class names + CSS variables kept (HTML + JS read --ivory/--gold/
   --midnight at runtime; the inline SVG seal uses the same gold family).
   ============================================================================= */

:root {
  /* ---- palette (original names kept, enriched values) ---------------------- */
  --midnight:      #070b16;   /* page base " deep, faintly warm blue-black      */
  --navy:          #0c142b;   /* deep indigo                                    */
  --navy-2:        #111c39;   /* raised indigo (cards)                          */
  --ivory:         #f5f0e4;   /* warm parchment ivory                           */
  --ivory-dim:     #cbc6b6;   /* softened ivory (body copy)                     */
  --gold:          #c9a25f;   /* antique brass                                  */
  --gold-soft:     #e8cf94;   /* lit brass / gold highlight                     */
  --stellar:       #93b4ff;   /* soft stellar blue                              */

  --line-soft:     rgba(245,240,228,0.09);
  --ok:            #74c69a;
  --warn:          #e2b062;

  --ink:           #0a1122;

  --panel-lo:      #0d1730;

           Palatino, "Book Antiqua", Georgia, "Times New Roman", serif;

    <dl class="faq">
      <dt>Do I legally own the star?</dt>
      <dd>No. A dedication is symbolic. It does not grant ownership of a celestial object and does
        not replace its scientific catalogue designation.</dd>
      <dt>Is the star real?</dt>
      <dd>Yes. Each dedication is linked to a genuine star from the open HYG catalogue, with real
        J2000 coordinates and a scientific identifier.</dd>
      <dt>Is the name officially recognised?</dt>
      <dd>No. The symbolic name is recorded only within this registry. No government or scientific
        body (NASA, ESA, IAU) recognises it.</dd>
      <dt>What do I receive?</dt>
      <dd>A permanent registry record with a unique number, a premium PDF certificate, a
        data-driven star map, a cinematic reveal, and an interactive viewer to find the star.</dd>
      <dt>Can I keep it private?</dt>
      <dd>Yes. Choose public, limited, private or unlisted. Private and unlisted records never
        appear in public search and require a secure link or ownership to view.</dd>
      <dt>How do I verify a dedication?</dt>
      <dd>Enter the registration number on the <a href="/verify.html">Verify</a> page. Verification
        confirms existence, status and astronomical details without exposing private content.</dd>
      <dt>Can I get a refund or correct a typo?</dt>
      <dd>See our <a href="/legal/refunds.html">Refund &amp; Correction Policy</a>. Eligible text
        can be corrected; corrections are recorded in an audit trail.</dd>
      <dt>Will I be able to see the star?</dt>
      <dd>The certificate and viewer state whether the star is naked-eye, binocular or telescope
        class. We never promise naked-eye visibility for a star that doesn't support it.</dd>
    </dl>
  </div></main>
  <footer class="site-footer"><div class="wrap footer-legal dim">
    <p class="mb-0">&copy; <span data-year>2026</span> The Star Dedication Registry.
    Star data derived from the HYG Database (CC BY-SA 4.0).</p>
  </div></footer>

        permanent registry record, a keepsake certificate, and a star you can find again.</p>
