/* =============================================================================
   TSDR \u2014 foundational client script (Run 1)
   - Lightweight starfield for the hero (respects prefers-reduced-motion)
   - No autoplay audio, no heavy libs. Progressive & accessible.
   - Verification form talks to /.netlify/functions/verify (built in Run 2).
   ============================================================================= */
(function () {
  'use strict';

  // ---- reduced motion guard ----
  const reduce = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;

  // ---- hero starfield (canvas). Static single frame if reduced motion. ----
  function initStarfield(canvas) {
    if (!canvas || !canvas.getContext) return;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    let w, h, stars, raf;
    const DPR = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);

    function resize() {
      w = canvas.clientWidth; h = canvas.clientHeight;
      canvas.width = w * DPR; canvas.height = h * DPR;
      ctx.setTransform(DPR, 0, 0, DPR, 0, 0);
      const count = Math.min(220, Math.floor((w * h) / 9000)); // scale to viewport
      stars = Array.from({ length: count }, () => ({
        x: Math.random() * w, y: Math.random() * h,
        r: Math.random() * 1.3 + 0.2,
        a: Math.random() * 0.6 + 0.2,
        tw: Math.random() * 0.02 + 0.004,
        p: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,
      }));
    }
    function draw(t) {
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
      for (const s of stars) {
        const alpha = reduce ? s.a : s.a + Math.sin(s.p + t * s.tw) * 0.25;
        ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0.05, Math.min(1, alpha));
        ctx.fillStyle = Math.random() < 0.02 ? '#9db8ff' : '#f4efe4';
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, s.r, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
      }
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      if (!reduce) raf = requestAnimationFrame(draw);
    }
    resize();
    window.addEventListener('resize', () => { cancelAnimationFrame(raf); resize();
      if (reduce) draw(0); else raf = requestAnimationFrame(draw); }, { passive: true });
    if (reduce) draw(0); else raf = requestAnimationFrame(draw);

    // pause when tab hidden (saves battery / Netlify has nothing to do with this)
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
      if (document.hidden) cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      else if (!reduce) raf = requestAnimationFrame(draw);
    });
  }

  // ---- registration-number formatting + client-side verify prep ----
  function normalizeRegNo(v) {
    // Strip to alphanumerics then rebuild canonical TSDR-YYYY-XXXXXXXX so users
    // can type spaces or omit dashes.
    var raw = String(v || '').toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9]/g, '');
    var m = /^TSDR(\d{4})([0-9A-HJ-NP-TV-Z]{8})$/.exec(raw);
    if (m) return 'TSDR-' + m[1] + '-' + m[2];
    return String(v || '').toUpperCase().replace(/\s+/g, '');
  }

  function initVerifyForm(form) {
    if (!form) return;
    const input = form.querySelector('[name="registration_number"]');
    const out = form.querySelector('[data-verify-result]');

    function line(label, value) {
      const row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'verify-row';
      const dt = document.createElement('span'); dt.className = 'verify-k'; dt.textContent = label;
      const dd = document.createElement('b'); dd.className = 'verify-v'; dd.textContent = value;
      row.appendChild(dt); row.appendChild(dd);
      return row;
    }
    function fmtCoords(ra, dec) {
      if (ra == null || dec == null) return '\u2014';
      const raH = ra / 15, h = Math.floor(raH), m = Math.floor((raH - h) * 60);
      const sign = dec >= 0 ? '+' : '\u2212', da = Math.abs(dec),
        d = Math.floor(da), dm = Math.round((da - d) * 60);
      return 'RA ' + h + 'h ' + m + 'm \u00b7 Dec ' + sign + d + '\u00b0 ' + dm + '\u2032';
    }

    function renderRecord(data) {
      out.textContent = '';
      out.className = (out.className || '').replace(/\bverify-card\b/, '').trim() + ' verify-card';
      const head = document.createElement('p'); head.className = 'verify-head';
      head.textContent = 'Verified \u00b7 ' + (data.registration_status || 'active');
      out.appendChild(head);
      out.appendChild(line('Registration', data.registration_number || '\u2014'));
      out.appendChild(line('Constellation', data.constellation || '\u2014'));
      out.appendChild(line('Coordinates (J2000)', fmtCoords(data.right_ascension, data.declination)));
      const cat = data.catalog || {};
      out.appendChild(line('Scientific identifier',
        [cat.source, cat.identifier].filter(Boolean).join(' ') || '\u2014'));
      out.appendChild(line('Occasion', data.occasion || '\u2014'));
      if (data.activated_at) out.appendChild(line('Registered', String(data.activated_at).slice(0, 10)));
      out.appendChild(line('Visibility', data.is_public ? 'Public dedication' : 'Private / limited'));

      const note = document.createElement('p'); note.className = 'verify-note';
      note.textContent = data.is_public
        ? 'This is a public dedication.'
        : 'Private names and messages are not shown here \u2014 those require the owner\u2019s link.';
      out.appendChild(note);

      // Public records can open the printable certificate directly.
      if (data.is_public && data.registration_number) {
        const a = document.createElement('a');
        a.className = 'btn btn-ghost verify-cert';
        a.setAttribute('href', '/certificate.html?rn=' + encodeURIComponent(data.registration_number));
        a.textContent = 'View certificate';
        out.appendChild(a);
      }
    }

    async function run(raw) {
      const regno = normalizeRegNo(raw);
      if (!/^TSDR-\d{4}-[0-9A-HJ-NP-TV-Z]{8}$/.test(regno)) {
        out.className = (out.className || '').replace(/\bverify-card\b/, '').trim();
        out.textContent = 'Please enter a registration number like TSDR-2026-XXXXXXXX.';
        out.hidden = false; return;
      }
      out.className = (out.className || '').replace(/\bverify-card\b/, '').trim();
      out.textContent = 'Checking the registry\u2026'; out.hidden = false;
      try {
        // Endpoint implemented in Run 2. Rate-limited & enumeration-resistant.
        const res = await fetch('/.netlify/functions/verify?rn=' + encodeURIComponent(regno));
        if (res.status === 404) { out.textContent = 'No active registration matches that number.'; return; }
        if (!res.ok) { out.textContent = 'Verification is temporarily unavailable. Please try again shortly.'; return; }
        const data = await res.json();
        out.hidden = false;
        renderRecord(data);
      } catch {
        out.textContent = 'Network error. Please try again.';
      }
    }

    form.addEventListener('submit', (e) => { e.preventDefault(); run(input.value); });

    // Auto-verify when arriving from an email/QR link: /verify.html?rn=...
    const rnParam = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('rn');
    if (rnParam) { input.value = rnParam; run(rnParam); }
  }

  // ---- mobile navigation (added) ----
  // Injects a CSP-safe hamburger into the sticky header and toggles a class.
  // All visual state lives in css/tsdr.css (.nav-toggle / .site-header.nav-open).
  function initMobileNav() {
    var header = document.querySelector('.site-header');
    if (!header) return;
    var nav = header.querySelector('.nav');            // real markup: <div class="wrap nav">
    var links = header.querySelector('.nav-links');
    if (!nav || !links) return;                        // e.g. admin has no nav-links \u2192 no-op
    if (nav.querySelector('.nav-toggle')) return;      // guard double-init
    if (!links.id) links.id = 'primary-nav';

    var btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.type = 'button';
    btn.className = 'nav-toggle';
    btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
    btn.setAttribute('aria-controls', links.id);
    btn.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Open menu');
    btn.innerHTML = '<span class="nav-toggle-bar"></span>' +
                    '<span class="nav-toggle-bar"></span>' +
                    '<span class="nav-toggle-bar"></span>';
    nav.appendChild(btn);

    function setOpen(open) {
      header.classList.toggle('nav-open', open);
      btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', open ? 'true' : 'false');
      btn.setAttribute('aria-label', open ? 'Close menu' : 'Open menu');
    }
    btn.addEventListener('click', function () {
      setOpen(!header.classList.contains('nav-open'));
    });
    links.addEventListener('click', function (e) { if (e.target.closest('a')) setOpen(false); });
    document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'Escape') setOpen(false); });
    window.addEventListener('resize', function () {
      if (window.innerWidth >= 860) setOpen(false);
    }, { passive: true });
  }

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
    initStarfield(document.querySelector('canvas.starfield'));
    initVerifyForm(document.querySelector('[data-verify-form]'));
    initMobileNav();
    // set current year in footer
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-year]').forEach((el) => (el.textContent = new Date().getFullYear()));
  });
})();
=====================================================
FILE: public\js\viewer.js
=====================================================
/* =============================================================================
   TSDR -- Option A original sky viewer (Run C).
   -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   A premium, mobile-first sky viewer built ENTIRELY from OUR licensed HYG
   neighbour data. The named star resolves out of a real starfield: the view
   opens wide and eases in, the allocated star brightens and a reticle draws
   around it, with constellation context, an RA/Dec readout, nearby stars, and
   a plain-language "how to find it" hint.

   Rendering:
     - Primary: WebGL point renderer (soft round glow points, single draw pass)
       -- smooth on budget Android (DPR capped at 2, neighbour budget capped).
     - Fallback: an equivalent 2D <canvas> renderer if WebGL is unavailable.

   FULLY ORIGINAL + OWNED. No Stellarium engine, no third-party viewer code.
   CSP-safe: external script, no inline styles/scripts; info text is escaped.
   Reduced-motion aware; keyboard-navigable (+/- zoom, arrows pan, Esc close);
   provides a non-visual text alternative in the info panel.
   ============================================================================= */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  var DEG = Math.PI / 180;
  var reduce = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  var MAX_NEIGHBORS = 220;         // budget for low-end devices
  var DPR = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);

  function esc(s) {
    return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
      return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c];
    });
  }

  // Gnomonic (tangent-plane) projection about the allocated star.
  function gnomonic(ra, dec, ra0, dec0) {
    var a = ra * DEG, d = dec * DEG, a0 = ra0 * DEG, d0 = dec0 * DEG;
    var cosc = Math.sin(d0) * Math.sin(d) + Math.cos(d0) * Math.cos(d) * Math.cos(a - a0);
    if (cosc <= 0) return null;                 // far hemisphere -> cull
    return {
      x: (Math.cos(d) * Math.sin(a - a0)) / cosc,
      y: (Math.cos(d0) * Math.sin(d) - Math.sin(d0) * Math.cos(d) * Math.cos(a - a0)) / cosc,
    };
  }

  function magToSize(mag) {
    if (mag == null || isNaN(mag)) return 2.2;
    return Math.max(1.2, Math.min(6.5, 6.2 - 0.62 * mag)); // brighter -> bigger
  }

  function visibilityGuide(cls) {
    switch (cls) {
      case 'naked_eye': return 'Visible to the naked eye under a dark sky, away from city lights.';
      case 'binocular': return 'Best found with a pair of binoculars on a clear night.';
      case 'small_telescope': return 'A small backyard telescope will bring it into view.';
      default: return 'A telescope is needed to observe this star.';
    }
  }

  function raDecText(ra, dec) {
    var raH = ra / 15, hh = Math.floor(raH), mm = Math.floor((raH - hh) * 60);
    var ss = Math.round((((raH - hh) * 60) - mm) * 60);
    var sign = dec >= 0 ? '+' : '\u2212', da = Math.abs(dec);
    var dd = Math.floor(da), dm = Math.round((da - dd) * 60);
    return 'RA ' + hh + 'h ' + mm + 'm ' + ss + 's \u00b7 Dec ' + sign + dd + '\u00b0 ' + dm + '\u2032';
  }

  // ---- WebGL point renderer (primary) ---------------------------------------
  function makeGL(canvas) {
    var gl = null;
    try {
      gl = canvas.getContext('webgl', { antialias: true, premultipliedAlpha: false }) ||
           canvas.getContext('experimental-webgl');
    } catch (e) { gl = null; }
    if (!gl) return null;

    var vsrc =
      'attribute vec2 a_pos; attribute float a_size; attribute vec4 a_col;' +
      'varying vec4 v_col;' +
      'void main(){ v_col = a_col; gl_Position = vec4(a_pos, 0.0, 1.0);' +
      ' gl_PointSize = a_size; }';
    var fsrc =
      'precision mediump float; varying vec4 v_col;' +
      'void main(){ vec2 d = gl_PointCoord - vec2(0.5);' +
      ' float r = length(d); if (r > 0.5) discard;' +
      ' float glow = smoothstep(0.5, 0.0, r);' +
      ' gl_FragColor = vec4(v_col.rgb, v_col.a * glow); }';

    function sh(type, src) {
      var s = gl.createShader(type); gl.shaderSource(s, src); gl.compileShader(s);
      if (!gl.getShaderParameter(s, gl.COMPILE_STATUS)) { gl.deleteShader(s); return null; }
      return s;
    }
    var vs = sh(gl.VERTEX_SHADER, vsrc), fs = sh(gl.FRAGMENT_SHADER, fsrc);
    if (!vs || !fs) return null;
    var prog = gl.createProgram();
    gl.attachShader(prog, vs); gl.attachShader(prog, fs); gl.linkProgram(prog);
    if (!gl.getProgramParameter(prog, gl.LINK_STATUS)) return null;
    gl.useProgram(prog);

    var buf = gl.createBuffer();
    var aPos = gl.getAttribLocation(prog, 'a_pos');
    var aSize = gl.getAttribLocation(prog, 'a_size');
    var aCol = gl.getAttribLocation(prog, 'a_col');
    gl.enable(gl.BLEND);
    gl.blendFunc(gl.SRC_ALPHA, gl.ONE); // additive glow

    return {
      gl: gl,
      resize: function (wpx, hpx) { gl.viewport(0, 0, wpx, hpx); },
      // verts: Float32Array of stride 7 [x,y,size,r,g,b,a] in clip space
      draw: function (verts, count, bg) {
        gl.clearColor(bg[0], bg[1], bg[2], 1);
        gl.clear(gl.COLOR_BUFFER_BIT);
        gl.bindBuffer(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, buf);
        gl.bufferData(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, verts, gl.DYNAMIC_DRAW);
        var st = 7 * 4;
        gl.enableVertexAttribArray(aPos); gl.vertexAttribPointer(aPos, 2, gl.FLOAT, false, st, 0);
        gl.enableVertexAttribArray(aSize); gl.vertexAttribPointer(aSize, 1, gl.FLOAT, false, st, 8);
        gl.enableVertexAttribArray(aCol); gl.vertexAttribPointer(aCol, 4, gl.FLOAT, false, st, 12);
        gl.drawArrays(gl.POINTS, 0, count);
      },
    };
  }

  // ---- Shared scene state ---------------------------------------------------
  function open(data) {
    var overlay = document.getElementById('viewer');
    if (!overlay) return;
    overlay.classList.add('open');
    overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');

    var canvas = document.getElementById('viewer-canvas');
    var ctx2d = null;
    var glr = makeGL(canvas);
    if (!glr) ctx2d = canvas.getContext('2d');

    var ra0 = data.right_ascension, dec0 = data.declination;
    var neighbors = (data.neighbors || []).slice(0, MAX_NEIGHBORS);

    var targetFov = 14, fov = reduce ? 14 : 40; // resolve-in animation
    var panX = 0, panY = 0;                       // pan offset in tangent-plane units
    var w = 0, h = 0, wpx = 0, hpx = 0;
    var raf = null, animating = false;

    function measure() {
      w = canvas.clientWidth; h = canvas.clientHeight;
      wpx = Math.max(1, Math.floor(w * DPR)); hpx = Math.max(1, Math.floor(h * DPR));
      canvas.width = wpx; canvas.height = hpx;
      if (glr) glr.resize(wpx, hpx);
      else ctx2d.setTransform(DPR, 0, 0, DPR, 0, 0);
    }

    // pixels-per-tangent-unit so the field of view spans the smaller dimension
    function scalePx() {
      return (Math.min(w, h) * 0.86) / (2 * Math.tan((fov / 2) * DEG));
    }

    function project(ra, dec) {
      var g = gnomonic(ra, dec, ra0, dec0);
      if (!g) return null;
      return { x: (g.x - panX), y: (g.y - panY) };
    }

    // Build a GL vertex array (clip space) for all points including centre.
    // gl_PointSize is capped on many mobile GPUs (often ~63), so clamp sizes to
    // stay crisp instead of clipping on low-end devices.
    function ps(v) { return Math.max(1, Math.min(60, v)); }
    function buildVerts() {
      var s = scalePx();
      var arr = [];
      // neighbours (ivory)
      for (var i = 0; i < neighbors.length; i++) {
        var n = neighbors[i]; var p = project(n.right_ascension, n.declination);
        if (!p) continue;
        var px = w / 2 + p.x * s, py = h / 2 - p.y * s;
        if (px < -8 || px > w + 8 || py < -8 || py > h + 8) continue;
        var xn = (px / w) * 2 - 1, yn = 1 - (py / h) * 2;
        var sz = ps(magToSize(n.apparent_magnitude) * DPR);
        arr.push(xn, yn, sz, 0.957, 0.937, 0.894, 0.9);
      }
      // centre halo + core (gold), drawn last & largest
      var c = project(ra0, dec0);
      if (c) {
        var cx = w / 2 + c.x * s, cy = h / 2 - c.y * s;
        var cxn = (cx / w) * 2 - 1, cyn = 1 - (cy / h) * 2;
        arr.push(cxn, cyn, ps(34 * DPR), 0.788, 0.655, 0.361, 0.28); // halo
        arr.push(cxn, cyn, ps(9 * DPR), 0.878, 0.784, 0.541, 1.0);   // core
      }
      return { verts: new Float32Array(arr), count: arr.length / 7, centre: c };
    }

    // Draw a reticle (gold ring + ticks) around the centre on a 2D overlay pass.
    // For WebGL we draw the reticle with a tiny 2D pass onto the same canvas is
    // not possible; instead we draw ring points in GL. To keep the ring crisp we
    // use the 2D path only in the fallback; in GL we render an emphasised core.
    function drawGL() {
      var b = buildVerts();
      glr.draw(b.verts, b.count, [0.0196, 0.0275, 0.0588]); // #05070f
    }

    function draw2d() {
      var s = scalePx();
      ctx2d.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
      ctx2d.fillStyle = '#05070f'; ctx2d.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
      ctx2d.strokeStyle = '#1b2748';
      [0.44, 0.30, 0.16].forEach(function (f) {
        ctx2d.beginPath(); ctx2d.arc(w / 2, h / 2, Math.min(w, h) * f, 0, 6.283); ctx2d.stroke();
      });
      for (var i = 0; i < neighbors.length; i++) {
        var n = neighbors[i]; var p = project(n.right_ascension, n.declination);
        if (!p) continue;
        var px = w / 2 + p.x * s, py = h / 2 - p.y * s;
        if (px < 0 || px > w || py < 0 || py > h) continue;
        var r = magToSize(n.apparent_magnitude) / 1.6;
        ctx2d.globalAlpha = 0.9; ctx2d.fillStyle = '#f4efe4';
        ctx2d.beginPath(); ctx2d.arc(px, py, r, 0, 6.283); ctx2d.fill(); ctx2d.globalAlpha = 1;
      }
      var c = project(ra0, dec0);
      if (c) {
        var cx = w / 2 + c.x * s, cy = h / 2 - c.y * s;
        ctx2d.fillStyle = '#e0c88a'; ctx2d.shadowColor = '#c9a75c'; ctx2d.shadowBlur = 22;
        ctx2d.beginPath(); ctx2d.arc(cx, cy, 5, 0, 6.283); ctx2d.fill(); ctx2d.shadowBlur = 0;
        drawReticle(ctx2d, cx, cy);
      }
    }

    // Reticle overlay for the WebGL path (separate 2D layer would be ideal; we
    // reuse the same canvas after the GL pass is not possible, so in GL mode we
    // draw the reticle via CSS-less canvas 2D only in fallback). To keep GL mode
    // premium we render an animated CSS ring element instead.
    function drawReticle(c2, cx, cy) {
      c2.strokeStyle = '#c9a75c'; c2.lineWidth = 1.4;
      c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(cx, cy, 17, 0, 6.283); c2.stroke();
      c2.globalAlpha = 0.6;
      c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(cx, cy, 27, 0, 6.283); c2.stroke();
      c2.globalAlpha = 1;
      [[0, -34, 0, -22], [0, 22, 0, 34], [-34, 0, -22, 0], [22, 0, 34, 0]].forEach(function (t) {
        c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(cx + t[0], cy + t[1]); c2.lineTo(cx + t[2], cy + t[3]); c2.stroke();
      });
    }

    // Position the CSS reticle (used in GL mode so the ring stays crisp).
    var reticle = document.getElementById('viewer-reticle');
    function placeReticle() {
      if (!reticle) return;
      var c = project(ra0, dec0);
      if (!c) { reticle.hidden = true; return; }
      var s = scalePx();
      var cx = w / 2 + c.x * s, cy = h / 2 - c.y * s;
      reticle.hidden = false;
      reticle.style.left = cx + 'px';
      reticle.style.top = cy + 'px';
    }

    function render() {
      if (glr) { drawGL(); placeReticle(); }
      else { draw2d(); if (reticle) reticle.hidden = true; }
    }

    function animate() {
      animating = true;
      function frame() {
        fov += (targetFov - fov) * 0.12;
        if (Math.abs(fov - targetFov) < 0.05) { fov = targetFov; animating = false; }
        render();
        if (animating) raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
      }
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf); raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    }

    measure(); if (reduce) render(); else animate();

    // ---- interaction --------------------------------------------------------
    function setFov(v) { fov = Math.max(4, Math.min(48, v)); targetFov = fov; render(); }
    function panBy(dxPx, dyPx) {
      var s = scalePx();
      panX += dxPx / s; panY -= dyPx / s; render();
    }

    var zin = document.getElementById('v-zoom-in');
    var zout = document.getElementById('v-zoom-out');
    var reset = document.getElementById('v-reset');
    if (zin) zin.onclick = function () { setFov(fov - 2); };
    if (zout) zout.onclick = function () { setFov(fov + 2); };
    if (reset) reset.onclick = function () { panX = 0; panY = 0; setFov(14); };

    // pointer drag to pan
    var dragging = false, lastX = 0, lastY = 0;
    function onDown(e) { dragging = true; var pt = point(e); lastX = pt.x; lastY = pt.y; }
    function onMove(e) {
      if (!dragging) return;
      var pt = point(e); panBy(pt.x - lastX, pt.y - lastY); lastX = pt.x; lastY = pt.y;
      if (e.cancelable) e.preventDefault();
    }
    function onUp() { dragging = false; }
    function point(e) {
      var t = (e.touches && e.touches[0]) || e;
      var rct = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
      return { x: t.clientX - rct.left, y: t.clientY - rct.top };
    }
    canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', onDown);
    window.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
    window.addEventListener('mouseup', onUp);
    canvas.addEventListener('touchstart', onDown, { passive: true });
    canvas.addEventListener('touchmove', onMove, { passive: false });
    canvas.addEventListener('touchend', onUp);
    // wheel zoom
    canvas.addEventListener('wheel', function (e) {
      setFov(fov + (e.deltaY > 0 ? 2 : -2)); if (e.cancelable) e.preventDefault();
    }, { passive: false });

    function onResize() { measure(); render(); }
    window.addEventListener('resize', onResize, { passive: true });

    // keyboard: focus the overlay so arrows/+/-/Esc work
    overlay.setAttribute('tabindex', '-1');
    try { overlay.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* older browsers */ }
    function onKey(e) {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') { close(); return; }
      if (e.key === '+' || e.key === '=') { setFov(fov - 2); }
      else if (e.key === '-' || e.key === '_') { setFov(fov + 2); }
      else if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') { panBy(-28, 0); }
      else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') { panBy(28, 0); }
      else if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') { panBy(0, -28); }
      else if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') { panBy(0, 28); }
      else return;
      if (e.cancelable) e.preventDefault();
    }
    overlay.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);

    // ---- info panel + how-to-find (text alternative) ------------------------
    var info = document.getElementById('viewer-info');
    if (info) {
      var find = 'Look toward the ' + esc(data.constellation || 'night sky') +
        ' region; the star sits at ' + esc(raDecText(ra0, dec0)) + '. ' +
        esc(visibilityGuide(data.visibility_class));
      info.innerHTML =
        '<b>' + esc(data.symbolic_star_name || 'Your star') + '</b>' +
        '<span class="vmeta">' + esc(data.source_identifier || '') +
          (data.constellation ? ' \u00b7 ' + esc(data.constellation) : '') + '</span>' +
        '<span class="vmeta">' + esc(raDecText(ra0, dec0)) + '</span>' +
        '<span class="vfind">How to find it: ' + find + '</span>';
    }
    var title = document.getElementById('viewer-title');
    if (title) title.textContent = data.symbolic_star_name || 'Your star';

    function close() {
      overlay.classList.remove('open');
      overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      window.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
      window.removeEventListener('mouseup', onUp);
      window.removeEventListener('resize', onResize);
      overlay.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    }
    var closeBtn = document.getElementById('v-close');
    if (closeBtn) closeBtn.onclick = close;
  }

  window.TSDRViewer = { open: open };
})();

  <link rel="canonical" href="https://www.stardedicationregistry.example/legal/disclosure.html" />
  <meta property="og:title" content="Symbolic Dedication Disclosure &mdash; The Star Dedication Registry" />

  <meta property="og:image" content="/assets/og-cover.png" />
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="/css/tsdr.css" />
  <script type="application/ld+json">
  {"@context":"https://schema.org","@type":"FAQPage","mainEntity":[
    {"@type":"Question","name":"Do I legally own a star?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"No. A dedication is symbolic. It does not grant ownership of a celestial object and does not replace its scientific catalogue designation."}},
    {"@type":"Question","name":"Is the name officially recognised by astronomers or NASA/ESA/IAU?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"No. The symbolic name is recorded only within The Star Dedication Registry. No government or scientific body recognises it."}},

  <main id="main" class="band">
    <div class="wrap readable">
      <p class="eyebrow">Symbolic Dedication Disclosure</p>
      <h1>What's real, and what's symbolic</h1>

      <div class="disclosure stack">
        <p class="mb-0 mt-0"><strong>The selected astronomical object is real.</strong> The chosen
        name is a <strong>symbolic dedication</strong> recorded permanently within The Star
        Dedication Registry. It does <strong>not</strong> replace the object's scientific
        catalogue designation or provide ownership of the celestial object.</p>
      </div>

      <h2>We will never claim</h2>
      <ul class="muted stack">
        <li>That you legally own a star or have purchased celestial property.</li>
        <li>That your symbolic name replaces the star's scientific designation.</li>
        <li>That the name is officially recognised by astronomers.</li>
        <li>That NASA, ESA, the IAU or any government recognises the dedication.</li>
        <li>That this registry has scientific naming authority.</li>
        <li>That the dedication is recognised by other private registries.</li>
        <li>That publishing or copyrighting a registry makes a name governmental.</li>
      </ul>

      <h2>What you genuinely receive</h2>
      <ul class="muted stack">
        <li>A dedication linked to a <strong>real catalogue star</strong> with accurate J2000
          coordinates, a constellation, and a scientific identifier (e.g. an HIP/HD/HR number).</li>
        <li>A <strong>permanent record</strong> within this registry, with a unique, verifiable
          registration number.</li>
        <li>A premium certificate and an original, data-driven star map.</li>
        <li>A way to locate, revisit and privately or publicly share the dedication.</li>
      </ul>

      <h2>Why we say this openly</h2>
      <p>There is no official commercial star-naming authority anywhere in the world. Every
      provider &mdash; including us &mdash; maintains its <strong>own private registry</strong>. We believe
      honesty strengthens the gift rather than diminishing it: a symbolic dedication is a
      meaningful, lasting gesture precisely because of what it is, not because of a claim it
      isn't. We prefer the words <strong>&ldquo;Dedicate a Star&rdquo;</strong> over &ldquo;Buy a Star.&rdquo;</p>

      <p><a class="btn btn-primary" href="/dedicate.html">Dedicate a Star</a></p>
    </div>

<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8" />
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1" />
  <title>Privacy Policy &mdash; The Star Dedication Registry</title>
  <meta name="description" content="How The Star Dedication Registry handles your data: minimal collection, privacy modes, what is never exposed publicly, and your rights." />
  <link rel="canonical" href="https://www.stardedicationregistry.example/legal/privacy.html" />
  <meta property="og:title" content="Privacy Policy &mdash; The Star Dedication Registry" />
  <meta property="og:description" content="How The Star Dedication Registry handles your data: minimal collection, privacy modes, what is never exposed publicly, and your rights." />
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="/css/tsdr.css" />
</head>
<body>
  <a class="skip-link" href="#main">Skip to content</a>
  <header class="site-header"><div class="wrap nav">
    <a class="brand" href="/"><strong class="serif-ivory">The Star Dedication Registry</strong></a>
    <nav class="nav-links" aria-label="Primary">
      <a href="/dedicate.html">Dedicate a Star</a><a href="/verify.html">Verify</a>
      <a href="/legal/disclosure.html">What's Real</a>
    </nav>
  </div></header>
  <main id="main" class="band"><div class="wrap readable">    <p class="eyebrow">Privacy</p>
    <h1>Privacy Policy</h1>
    <p class="lede">We collect the minimum needed to create and deliver your dedication.</p>
    <h2>What we collect</h2>
    <p>Account email (for sign-in and receipts), the dedication content you provide, and payment
      confirmation metadata from our provider. We do not store card details.</p>
    <h2>Privacy modes</h2>
    <p>You choose how visible a dedication is: <b>public</b>, <b>limited</b>, <b>private</b> or
      <b>unlisted</b>. Private and unlisted records are never listed in public search.</p>
    <h2>Never exposed publicly</h2>
    <p>Phone, email, payment references, internal order ids, account id, IP address, private media,
      admin notes, raw payment payloads and access tokens are never shown publicly.</p>
    <h2>Your rights</h2>
    <p>You can request access, correction or deletion where applicable by emailing
      privacy@stardedicationregistry.example. Recipients can report unwanted public dedications.</p>
    <p class="dim fs-sm">This summary is provided for transparency and is not legal advice; a full
      policy should be reviewed with counsel before launch.</p>
  </div></main>

      <a href="/legal/disclosure.html">What's Real</a>
    </nav>
  </div></header>
  <main id="main" class="band"><div class="wrap readable">    <p class="eyebrow">Refund &amp; correction</p>
    <h1>Refund &amp; Correction Policy</h1>
    <p class="lede">We want your keepsake to be right. Here's how we handle corrections, reissues
      and refunds.</p>
    <h2>Corrections</h2>
    <p>Eligible text (symbolic name, honouree, message, dedicator, privacy) can be corrected. Each
      correction is recorded in an audit trail and a fresh certificate can be reissued.</p>
    <h2>Reissues</h2>
    <p>If a certificate needs regenerating (e.g. after a correction), we issue a new version while
      retaining history. The verification link always resolves to the current record.</p>
    <h2>Refunds</h2>
    <p>If something went wrong on our side (e.g. activation failed after a successful payment), we
      will make it right or refund. Because each dedication permanently allocates a star within our
      registry, change-of-mind refunds may be limited once artefacts are issued.</p>
    <h2>How to request</h2>
    <p>Email support@stardedicationregistry.example with your registration number.</p>
    <p class="dim fs-sm">Statutory rights vary by region and are unaffected by this summary.</p>
  </div></main>

    <p class="mb-0">&copy; <span data-year>2026</span> The Star Dedication Registry.

FILE: public\legal\standards.html

<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8" />
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1" />
  <title>Registry Standards &mdash; The Star Dedication Registry</title>
  <meta name="description" content="The standards that govern The Star Dedication Registry: one active dedication per star, atomic reservation, unpredictable numbers, audit trail and privacy protections." />
  <link rel="canonical" href="https://www.stardedicationregistry.example/legal/standards.html" />
  <meta property="og:title" content="Registry Standards &mdash; The Star Dedication Registry" />
  <meta property="og:description" content="The standards that govern The Star Dedication Registry: one active dedication per star, atomic reservation, unpredictable numbers, audit trail and privacy protections." />
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="/css/tsdr.css" />
</head>
<body>
  <a class="skip-link" href="#main">Skip to content</a>
  <header class="site-header"><div class="wrap nav">
    <a class="brand" href="/"><strong class="serif-ivory">The Star Dedication Registry</strong></a>
    <nav class="nav-links" aria-label="Primary">
      <a href="/dedicate.html">Dedicate a Star</a><a href="/verify.html">Verify</a>
      <a href="/legal/disclosure.html">What's Real</a>
    </nav>
  </div></header>
  <main id="main" class="band"><div class="wrap readable">    <p class="eyebrow">Registry standards</p>
    <h1>How the registry is kept trustworthy</h1>
    <p class="lede">These standards are enforced in the platform, not just promised in prose.</p>
    <ul class="stack dim">
      <li><b>One active dedication per star.</b> An eligible catalogue star carries only one active
        symbolic dedication at a time (enforced by a database constraint).</li>
      <li><b>Atomic reservation.</b> Stars are held atomically during checkout, so no two people can
        be assigned the same star.</li>
      <li><b>Unpredictable numbers.</b> Registration numbers are random (<code>TSDR-YYYY-XXXXXXXX</code>),
        not sequential, and separate from any order id.</li>
      <li><b>Verified activation.</b> Only a signature-verified, amount-matched server-side payment
        event activates a dedication; duplicate events are ignored.</li>
      <li><b>Permanent record.</b> Issued registrations are never hard-deleted; corrections and
        status changes are written to an append-only audit trail.</li>
      <li><b>Privacy protected.</b> Private and unlisted records never leak through public search;
        sensitive fields are never exposed.</li>
      <li><b>Source of truth.</b> The database record &mdash; not the PDF &mdash; is authoritative. The
        certificate is a rendering of it.</li>
    </ul>
  </div></main>
  <footer class="site-footer"><div class="wrap footer-legal dim">
    <p class="mb-0">&copy; <span data-year>2026</span> The Star Dedication Registry.
    Star data derived from the HYG Database (CC BY-SA 4.0).</p>
  </div></footer>

<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8" />
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1" />
  <title>Star Guide &mdash; The Star Dedication Registry</title>
  <meta name="description" content="A plain-language guide to dedicating a star: what a symbolic name means, how it differs from the person honoured, constellations, visibility, and coordinates." />
  <link rel="canonical" href="https://www.stardedicationregistry.example/star-guide.html" />
  <meta property="og:title" content="Star Guide &mdash; The Star Dedication Registry" />
  <meta property="og:description" content="Understand symbolic star names, constellations, visibility and coordinates before you dedicate a star." />
  <meta property="og:image" content="/assets/og-cover.png" />

</head>
<body>
  <a class="skip-link" href="#main">Skip to content</a>
  <header class="site-header"><div class="wrap nav">
    <a class="brand" href="/"><strong class="serif-ivory">The Star Dedication Registry</strong></a>
    <nav class="nav-links" aria-label="Primary">
      <a href="/dedicate.html">Dedicate a Star</a><a href="/how-it-works.html">How It Works</a>
      <a href="/explore.html">Explore</a><a href="/verify.html">Verify</a>
    </nav>
  </div></header>

  <main id="main" class="band"><div class="wrap readable">
    <p class="eyebrow">Star guide</p>
    <h1>Everything you need to know before you dedicate</h1>
    <p class="lede">A two-minute, plain-language guide to the few star terms on the dedication form &mdash;
      so every choice feels clear and confident.</p>

    <div class="disclosure my-3"><p class="mb-0 mt-0">The star is <strong>real</strong>; the name is a
      <strong>symbolic</strong> dedication recorded within this registry. It does not grant ownership or
      replace the star's scientific designation. <a href="/legal/disclosure.html">Full disclosure &rarr;</a></p></div>

    <h2 id="symbolic-name">Symbolic star name vs. Person being honoured</h2>
    <p>This is the question we're asked most, so here it is simply:</p>
    <ul class="stack dim">
      <li><b>Symbolic star name</b> &mdash; the <b>title you give the star</b>. It's a poetic label for the
        dedication itself, like <b>"Ada's Light"</b>, <b>"Evening Vow"</b> or <b>"Little Comet"</b>.
        It is <b>not</b> a person's name.</li>
      <li><b>Person being honoured</b> &mdash; the <b>person the star is dedicated to</b>, usually their real
        name, like <b>Ada Lovelace</b>.</li>
    </ul>
    <table class="tbl">
      <tr><th>Field</th><th>What to enter</th><th>Example</th></tr>
      <tr><td>Symbolic star name</td><td>A title for the star</td><td>Ada's Light</td></tr>
      <tr><td>Person being honoured</td><td>Who it's for</td><td>Ada Lovelace</td></tr>
      <tr><td>From (optional)</td><td>Who it's from</td><td>The Analytical Society</td></tr>
    </table>
    <p class="dim">Read together: <em>"Ada's Light</em>, dedicated to <em>Ada Lovelace</em>, from
      <em>The Analytical Society</em>."</p>

    <h2 id="constellations">Constellations</h2>
    <p>A constellation is a named region of the sky. On the form you can optionally request one using its
      official <b>3-letter code</b> (the IAU abbreviation). Leave it blank and we'll select a fitting
      star for you.</p>
    <table class="tbl">
      <tr><th>Code</th><th>Constellation</th><th>Often chosen for</th></tr>
      <tr><td>Ori</td><td>Orion</td><td>A bold, easily found winter figure</td></tr>
      <tr><td>Lyr</td><td>Lyra</td><td>Weddings &amp; anniversaries (the harp)</td></tr>
      <tr><td>Cyg</td><td>Cygnus</td><td>The swan &mdash; devotion, memorials</td></tr>
      <tr><td>UMa</td><td>Ursa Major</td><td>Familiar "Big Dipper" stars</td></tr>
      <tr><td>CMa</td><td>Canis Major</td><td>Home of Sirius' neighbourhood</td></tr>
      <tr><td>Cas</td><td>Cassiopeia</td><td>The bright "W" shape</td></tr>
    </table>
    <p class="dim fs-sm">These are just popular examples &mdash; dozens of constellations are available. If a
      code has no eligible star free right now, choose "Registry-selected (any)" and we'll pick one.</p>

    <h2 id="visibility">Visibility (how easy the star is to see)</h2>
    <p>Every star has an "apparent magnitude" &mdash; how bright it looks from Earth. We turn that into a simple
      class so you know what it takes to see your star:</p>
    <table class="tbl">
      <tr><th>Class</th><th>What you'll need</th></tr>
      <tr><td>Naked-eye</td><td>Just a dark sky, away from city lights</td></tr>
      <tr><td>Binocular</td><td>A pair of binoculars</td></tr>
      <tr><td>Small telescope</td><td>A small backyard telescope</td></tr>
    </table>
    <p class="dim">We never promise naked-eye visibility for a star that can't support it &mdash; the certificate
      and viewer always state the honest class.</p>

    <h2 id="coordinates">Coordinates &amp; the scientific identifier</h2>
    <p>Your certificate shows the star's real <b>J2000 coordinates</b> &mdash; <b>Right Ascension (RA)</b> and
      <b>Declination (Dec)</b>, the sky's version of longitude and latitude &mdash; plus its <b>scientific
      identifier</b> (e.g. an HIP catalogue number). These let anyone locate the exact star and confirm
      it's genuine, while the symbolic name lives in our registry.</p>

    <h2 id="more">Still have a question?</h2>
    <p>See the <a href="/faqs.html">FAQs</a> or the full
      <a href="/legal/disclosure.html">Symbolic Dedication Disclosure</a>. When you're ready:</p>
    <p class="mt-3"><a class="btn btn-primary" href="/dedicate.html">Dedicate a Star</a>
      <a class="btn btn-ghost" href="/verify.html">Verify a Registration</a></p>
  </div></main>

  <footer class="site-footer"><div class="wrap footer-legal dim">
    <p class="mb-0">&copy; <span data-year>2026</span> The Star Dedication Registry.
    Star data derived from the HYG Database (CC BY-SA 4.0).</p>
  </div></footer>

FILE: README.md
=====================================================
# The Star Dedication Registry (TSDR)

> An independent archive of symbolic celestial dedications.
> **A real star. A personal dedication. A permanent place in your story.**

An independent, premium symbolic star-dedication registry -- its own brand, database,
certificate, verification and (from Run 3) an immersive star-viewing experience.
**This is not a page inside ZivoGift.** A signed integration contract is designed for later.

## Honesty first
The selected astronomical object is real. The chosen name is a **symbolic** dedication
recorded permanently within TSDR. It does **not** replace the object's scientific catalogue
designation or grant ownership of the celestial object. There is no official commercial
star-naming authority. See `public/legal/disclosure.html` and `docs/DATASET-AND-LICENSING.md`.

## Stack
Static frontend \u00b7 **Netlify** + Functions \u00b7 **Supabase** (Postgres + Auth) \u00b7 **Cloudflare R2**
\u00b7 **Cashfree**-compatible payments \u00b7 env-configured transactional email. No secrets in the
browser. Star data from the open **HYG Database (CC BY-SA 4.0)**.

## Status -- Runs 1-4 complete
Feature-complete across all four runs. **202 automated tests pass** (logic 20 \u00b7 functions 36 \u00b7
QR 85 \u00b7 run3 22 \u00b7 cert 8 \u00b7 e2e 17 \u00b7 artifacts 14) and **all quality gates are clean**
(CSP, accessibility, SEO, performance). Remaining work is live-services validation
(Supabase/Cashfree/R2), a Lighthouse run, and a physical QR scan -- see
`docs/PRE-LAUNCH-CHECKLIST.md`.

- Run 1 -- foundation (schema, RLS, allocation): `docs/RUN-1-REPORT.md`
- Run 2 -- backend & flows (functions, payments): `docs/RUN-2-REPORT.md`
- Run 3 -- experience & artefacts (QR, star map, certificate, reveal): `docs/RUN-3-REPORT.md`
- Run 4 -- SEO/a11y/perf, strict CSP, e2e, ops docs: `docs/RUN-4-REPORT.md`

Run everything: `npm run verify:all` (audits + tests).

## Quick start
```bash
npm run test:logic     # 20 allocation/idempotency/security logic tests
npm run seed:demo      # regenerate the synthetic demo pool (labelled, test only)

# Apply migrations (Supabase SQL editor or CLI), in order:
#   0001 -> 0002 -> 0003 -> 0004 -> 0005(demo)   (swap 0005 -> 0006 real HYG at launch)

# Real catalogue (on a networked machine, after downloading hyg_v44.csv):
npm run import:hyg
```

## Layout
`public/` static site \u00b7 `netlify/functions/` privileged endpoints (Run 2+) \u00b7
`supabase/migrations/` schema/RLS/views/functions/seed \u00b7 `scripts/` importers + tests \u00b7
`docs/` architecture, security, dataset/licensing, integration contract, run reports.

## Operate & deploy
- Deployment: `docs/DEPLOYMENT.md` \u00b7 Operations: `docs/OPERATIONS-MANUAL.md`
- Admin: `docs/ADMIN-GUIDE.md` \u00b7 Support: `docs/SUPPORT-GUIDE.md`
- Backups/DR: `docs/BACKUPS-AND-DR.md` \u00b7 Security: `docs/SECURITY-CHECKLIST.md`
- Go-live: `docs/PRE-LAUNCH-CHECKLIST.md` \u00b7 Limits: `docs/KNOWN-LIMITATIONS.md`

## Future
- ZivoGift integration -- designed (not built) in `docs/ZIVOGIFT-INTEGRATION-CONTRACT.md`.

FILE: scripts\a11y_audit.mjs
=====================================================
#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * TSDR -- accessibility audit (Run 4). Static checks across every .html under public/:
 *   - <html lang> present
 *   - single <h1>, no skipped heading levels
 *   - every <img> has alt (decorative allowed via alt="" or aria-hidden)
 *   - form controls have an associated <label for> or aria-label/aria-labelledby
 *   - a skip link exists and targets an existing id
 *   - buttons/links have discernible text (or aria-label)
 *   - <canvas> used decoratively has aria-hidden or a text alternative nearby
 * Exit 1 if any error-level issue is found. Warnings don't fail the build.
 */
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';

const ROOT = path.resolve(process.argv[2] || 'public');
function walk(d){const o=[];for(const e of fs.readdirSync(d,{withFileTypes:true})){
  const p=path.join(d,e.name); if(e.isDirectory())o.push(...walk(p));
  else if(e.name.endsWith('.html'))o.push(p);} return o;}

let errors=0, warns=0;
const err=(f,m)=>{errors++;console.log(`  x [err]  ${f}: ${m}`);};
const warn=(f,m)=>{warns++;console.log(`  ! [warn] ${f}: ${m}`);};

for (const file of walk(ROOT)) {
  const rel=path.relative(ROOT,file);
  const html=fs.readFileSync(file,'utf8');

  // lang
  if(!/<html[^>]*\blang=/.test(html)) err(rel,'missing <html lang>');

  // headings
  const h1=(html.match(/<h1\b/gi)||[]).length;
  if(h1===0) err(rel,'no <h1>');
  if(h1>1) warn(rel,`${h1} <h1> elements (prefer one)`);
  // heading order (no jumping more than 1 level down)
  const levels=[...html.matchAll(/<h([1-6])\b/gi)].map(m=>+m[1]);
  for(let i=1;i<levels.length;i++){ if(levels[i]-levels[i-1]>1)
    warn(rel,`heading jumps from h${levels[i-1]} to h${levels[i]}`);}

  // images
  for(const m of html.matchAll(/<img\b[^>]*>/gi)){
    const tag=m[0];
    if(!/\balt\s*=/.test(tag) && !/aria-hidden\s*=\s*"true"/.test(tag))
      err(rel,'<img> without alt or aria-hidden');
  }

  // canvas decorative
  for(const m of html.matchAll(/<canvas\b[^>]*>/gi)){
    if(!/aria-hidden\s*=\s*"true"/.test(m[0]) && !/aria-label=/.test(m[0]))
      warn(rel,'<canvas> should be aria-hidden or labelled');
  }

  // form controls need labels
  const ids=new Set([...html.matchAll(/\bid\s*=\s*"([^"]+)"/g)].map(m=>m[1]));
  const labelFor=new Set([...html.matchAll(/<label[^>]*\bfor\s*=\s*"([^"]+)"/g)].map(m=>m[1]));
  for(const m of html.matchAll(/<(input|select|textarea)\b[^>]*>/gi)){
    const tag=m[0];
    if(/type\s*=\s*"(hidden|submit|button)"/i.test(tag)) continue;
    const idm=/\bid\s*=\s*"([^"]+)"/.exec(tag);
    const hasAria=/aria-label\s*=|aria-labelledby\s*=/.test(tag);
    const labelled = idm && labelFor.has(idm[1]);
    if(!labelled && !hasAria) err(rel,`form control without label: ${tag.slice(0,60)}...`);
  }

  // skip link target
  const skip=/<a[^>]*class="[^"]*skip-link[^"]*"[^>]*href="#([^"]+)"/.exec(html);
  if(skip){ if(!ids.has(skip[1])) err(rel,`skip link targets #${skip[1]} which doesn't exist`); }
  else if(/<main\b/.test(html)) warn(rel,'no skip link');

  // discernible names for links/buttons (very rough)
  for(const m of html.matchAll(/<a\b[^>]*>(.*?)<\/a>/gis)){
    const inner=m[1].replace(/<[^>]+>/g,'').trim();
    const hasAria=/aria-label=/.test(m[0]);
    if(!inner && !hasAria) warn(rel,'link without discernible text');
  }
}

console.log(`\n================  A11Y: ${errors} errors, ${warns} warnings  ================\n`);
process.exit(errors===0?0:1);

FILE: scripts\build_csp.mjs
=====================================================
#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * TSDR -- CSP build tool (Run 4). Produces a STRICT Content-Security-Policy with
 * NO 'unsafe-inline' for scripts by hashing every inline <script> block, and NO
 * 'unsafe-inline' for styles by refusing to build if any inline style survives.
 * -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * What it does:
 *   1. Walks every .html under public/
 *   2. FAILS the build if any element has a style="" attribute or a <style> block
 *      (forces all CSS into external files -> style-src can be 'self').
 *   3. Computes sha256 of every inline <script>...</script> block (e.g. JSON-LD)
 *      and adds 'sha256-...' sources to script-src.
 *   4. Emits public/_headers with a strict CSP + caching + security headers.
 *      Netlify serves _headers with higher precedence for fine-grained control.
 * Run:  node scripts/build_csp.mjs   (exit 0 = clean, 1 = violations)
 */
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';

const ROOT = path.resolve(process.argv[2] || 'public');
const HEADERS_OUT = path.join(ROOT, '_headers');

// External hosts we intentionally allow.
const CDN_SCRIPTS = ['https://sdk.cashfree.com', 'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net'];
const CONNECT = ['https://sandbox.cashfree.com', 'https://api.cashfree.com', 'https://*.supabase.co'];
const FRAME = ['https://sdk.cashfree.com', 'https://payments.cashfree.com', 'https://sandbox.cashfree.com'];

function walk(dir) {
  const out = [];
  for (const e of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    const p = path.join(dir, e.name);
    if (e.isDirectory()) out.push(...walk(p));
    else if (e.name.endsWith('.html')) out.push(p);
  }
  return out;
}

function sha256b64(str) {
  return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(str, 'utf8').digest('base64');
}

const files = walk(ROOT);
const scriptHashes = new Set();
const violations = [];

for (const f of files) {
  const html = fs.readFileSync(f, 'utf8');
  const rel = path.relative(ROOT, f);

  // 1) reject inline style attributes
  const styleAttrs = html.match(/\sstyle\s*=\s*"/g);
  if (styleAttrs) violations.push(`${rel}: ${styleAttrs.length} inline style="" attribute(s)`);

  // 2) reject <style> blocks
  if (/<style[\s>]/i.test(html)) violations.push(`${rel}: contains a <style> block`);

  // 3) hash inline <script> blocks (any type, incl. application/ld+json).
  //    External <script src> have no body -> skipped. Empty scripts skipped.
  const re = /<script\b([^>]*)>([\s\S]*?)<\/script>/gi;
  let m;
  while ((m = re.exec(html)) !== null) {
    const attrs = m[1] || '';
    const body = m[2] || '';
    const hasSrc = /\bsrc\s*=/.test(attrs);
    if (hasSrc) continue;               // external, allowed via 'self'/CDN
    if (body.trim() === '') continue;   // nothing to hash
    scriptHashes.add(`'sha256-${sha256b64(body)}'`);
  }
}

if (violations.length) {
  console.error('CSP build FAILED -- inline styles must be moved to external CSS:\n');
  for (const v of violations) console.error('  \u2717 ' + v);
  console.error(`\n${violations.length} violation(s). No _headers written.`);
  process.exit(1);
}

const scriptSrc = ["'self'", ...CDN_SCRIPTS, ...[...scriptHashes].sort()].join(' ');
const csp = [
  "default-src 'self'",
  "img-src 'self' data:",
  "style-src 'self'",
  "font-src 'self'",
  `script-src ${scriptSrc}`,
  `connect-src 'self' ${CONNECT.join(' ')}`,
  `frame-src ${FRAME.join(' ')}`,
  "frame-ancestors 'none'",
  "base-uri 'self'",
  "form-action 'self'",
  "object-src 'none'",
].join('; ');

const headers = `# GENERATED by scripts/build_csp.mjs -- do not edit by hand.
# Strict CSP: no 'unsafe-inline' for scripts (hashed) or styles (externalised).

/*
  X-Frame-Options: DENY
  X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff
  Referrer-Policy: strict-origin-when-cross-origin
  Permissions-Policy: geolocation=(self), microphone=(), camera=(), payment=()
  Strict-Transport-Security: max-age=63072000; includeSubDomains; preload
  Content-Security-Policy: ${csp}

# Long-cache immutable static assets
/css/*
  Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable
/js/*
  Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable
/assets/*
  Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable

# HTML should revalidate
/*.html
  Cache-Control: public, max-age=0, must-revalidate

# Never index private/unlisted record routes
/d/private/*
  X-Robots-Tag: noindex, nofollow
/d/unlisted/*
  X-Robots-Tag: noindex, nofollow
/account/*
  X-Robots-Tag: noindex, nofollow
/admin/*
  X-Robots-Tag: noindex, nofollow
`;

fs.writeFileSync(HEADERS_OUT, headers);
console.log(`CSP build OK -- scanned ${files.length} HTML files.`);
console.log(`Inline <script> hashes: ${scriptHashes.size}`);
console.log(`Wrote ${path.relative(process.cwd(), HEADERS_OUT)}`);
console.log(`\nscript-src ${scriptSrc}`);
