FILE: scripts\generate_demo_pool.mjs
=====================================================
#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * TSDR -- DEMO pool generator (RUN-1 test data only)
 * -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * HONESTY: This produces SYNTHETIC placeholder records so Run 1 can exercise the
 * schema, allocation logic and (later) the viewer BEFORE the real HYG import is
 * run on a networked machine. Every row is stamped:
 *     source_catalog   = 'TSDR-DEMO'
 *     source_release   = 'SYNTHETIC -- NOT A REAL CATALOGUE'
 *     additional_metadata.demo = true
 * so it can never be mistaken for, or shipped as, real astronomical data.
 * The production pool comes from scripts/import_hyg.mjs (HYG, CC BY-SA 4.0).
 *
 * It also writes a JSON copy for the allocation-logic test + future viewer.
 *
 * USAGE:  node scripts/generate_demo_pool.mjs --count 300 \
 *           --sql ./supabase/migrations/0005_seed_demo_pool.sql \
 *           --json ./scripts/_demo_pool.json
 */
import fs from 'node:fs';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';

const args = Object.fromEntries(process.argv.slice(2).reduce((a, cur, i, arr) => {
  if (cur.startsWith('--')) a.push([cur.slice(2), arr[i + 1]?.startsWith('--') ? true : arr[i + 1]]);
  return a;
}, []));
const COUNT = Number(args.count ?? 300);
const SQL   = args.sql  || './supabase/migrations/0005_seed_demo_pool.sql';
const JSONP = args.json || './scripts/_demo_pool.json';

// IAU 88 constellations (3-letter) -- used to spread coverage realistically.
const CONS = ['And','Ant','Aps','Aqr','Aql','Ara','Ari','Aur','Boo','Cae','Cam','Cnc',
'CVn','CMa','CMi','Cap','Car','Cas','Cen','Cep','Cet','Cha','Cir','Col','Com','CrA',
'CrB','Crv','Crt','Cru','Cyg','Del','Dor','Dra','Equ','Eri','For','Gem','Gru','Her',
'Hor','Hya','Hyi','Ind','Lac','Leo','LMi','Lep','Lib','Lup','Lyn','Lyr','Men','Mic',
'Mon','Mus','Nor','Oct','Oph','Ori','Pav','Peg','Per','Phe','Pic','Psc','PsA','Pup',
'Pyx','Ret','Sge','Sgr','Sco','Scl','Sct','Ser','Sex','Tau','Tel','Tri','TrA','Tuc',
'UMa','UMi','Vel','Vir','Vol','Vul'];

const rndByte = () => crypto.randomBytes(1)[0];
const rnd = () => crypto.randomBytes(4).readUInt32BE(0) / 0xffffffff; // uniform [0,1)

function visibilityClass(mag) {
  if (mag <= 6.0) return 'naked_eye';
  if (mag <= 9.0) return 'binocular';
  if (mag <= 12.0) return 'small_telescope';
  return 'telescope';
}

const q = (s) => (s == null || s === '') ? 'null' : `'${String(s).replace(/'/g, "''")}'`;

const rows = [];
let validationErrors = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < COUNT; i++) {
  // RA uniform on [0,360); Dec via arcsin for area-uniform sphere sampling.
  const ra = rnd() * 360;
  const dec = Math.asin(2 * rnd() - 1) * 180 / Math.PI;
  const mag = +(2.0 + rnd() * 8.5).toFixed(2);       // 2.0 .. 10.5
  const con = CONS[Math.floor(rnd() * CONS.length)];
  const ci  = +(-0.3 + rnd() * 2.0).toFixed(3);
  const dist = +(3 + rnd() * 300).toFixed(2);
  const vis = visibilityClass(mag);
  const srcId = `DEMO-${String(i + 1).padStart(5, '0')}`;

  // ---- validate before we ever emit (mirrors DB CHECK constraints) ----
  if (!(ra >= 0 && ra < 360)) { validationErrors++; continue; }
  if (!(dec >= -90 && dec <= 90)) { validationErrors++; continue; }

  rows.push({
    source_catalog: 'TSDR-DEMO',
    source_release: 'SYNTHETIC -- NOT A REAL CATALOGUE',
    source_identifier: srcId,
    right_ascension: +ra.toFixed(6),
    declination: +dec.toFixed(6),
    constellation: con,
    apparent_magnitude: mag,
    color_index: ci,
    distance_parsecs: dist,
    visibility_class: vis,
    additional_metadata: { demo: true },
    allocation_status: 'available',
  });
}

// ---- write JSON (for tests + viewer prototype) ----
fs.writeFileSync(JSONP, JSON.stringify(rows, null, 0));

// ---- write SQL seed ----
const head =
`-- =============================================================================
-- TSDR -- DEMO seed pool (GENERATED by scripts/generate_demo_pool.mjs)
-- *** SYNTHETIC DATA -- NOT REAL ASTRONOMY. FOR DEV/TEST ONLY. ***
-- Replace with the real HYG import (0006_seed_catalog.sql) before launch.
-- Generated ${new Date().toISOString()} -- ${rows.length} rows.
-- =============================================================================
begin;
`;
let body = `insert into star_catalog
 (source_catalog, source_release, source_identifier, right_ascension, declination,
  constellation, apparent_magnitude, color_index, distance_parsecs, visibility_class,
  additional_metadata, allocation_status) values\n`;
body += rows.map((r) =>
  `(${q(r.source_catalog)}, ${q(r.source_release)}, ${q(r.source_identifier)}, ` +
  `${r.right_ascension}, ${r.declination}, ${q(r.constellation)}, ${r.apparent_magnitude}, ` +
  `${r.color_index}, ${r.distance_parsecs}, ${q(r.visibility_class)}, ` +
  `'{"demo":true}'::jsonb, 'available')`
).join(',\n');
body += `\non conflict (source_catalog, source_identifier) do nothing;\ncommit;\n`;

fs.writeFileSync(SQL, head + body);

// ---- report ----
const consUsed = new Set(rows.map((r) => r.constellation)).size;
const byVis = rows.reduce((m, r) => ((m[r.visibility_class] = (m[r.visibility_class] || 0) + 1), m), {});
console.log(JSON.stringify({
  requested: COUNT, generated: rows.length, validationErrors,
  constellationsCovered: consUsed, visibilityBreakdown: byVis,
  raRange: [Math.min(...rows.map(r=>r.right_ascension)).toFixed(3), Math.max(...rows.map(r=>r.right_ascension)).toFixed(3)],
  decRange: [Math.min(...rows.map(r=>r.declination)).toFixed(3), Math.max(...rows.map(r=>r.declination)).toFixed(3)],
  wroteSql: SQL, wroteJson: JSONP,
}, null, 2));
=====================================================
FILE: scripts\import_hyg.mjs
=====================================================
#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * TSDR -- Production catalogue importer (HYG v4.x -> curated allocation pool)
 * -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * LICENCE: HYG is CC BY-SA 4.0 (astronexus / David Nash). The DERIVED curated
 * pool we emit is therefore ALSO CC BY-SA 4.0 and MUST carry attribution
 * (see docs/DATASET-AND-LICENSING.md). This script writes that attribution into
 * every generated seed file header.
 *
 * WHAT IT DOES
 *   1. Reads a local HYG CSV (download once from the official source, offline).
 *   2. Filters to a *curated* premium pool (not the whole catalogue):
 *        - valid, finite RA/Dec/magnitude
 *        - excludes stars with dubious distance (dist >= 100000 in HYG)
 *        - excludes very faint records beyond a configurable magnitude cutoff
 *        - excludes IAU-named "famous" stars to avoid confusion
 *        - assigns a visibility_class from apparent magnitude
 *        - balances constellation coverage up to a per-constellation cap
 *   3. Emits supabase/migrations/00XX_seed_catalog.sql with INSERTs.
 *
 * USAGE (on a machine WITH network + the downloaded CSV):
 *   # 1) download once (official mirror):
 *   #    https://codeberg.org/astronexus/hyg  ->  data/hyg/CURRENT/hyg_v44.csv
 *   node scripts/import_hyg.mjs \
 *        --in ./hyg_v44.csv \
 *        --out ./supabase/migrations/0006_seed_catalog.sql \
 *        --max-mag 6.5 --per-constellation 400 --limit 20000
 *
 * This importer deliberately does NOT fetch over the network itself, so it is
 * reproducible and auditable. Point it at a file you downloaded and verified.
 */

import fs from 'node:fs';
import readline from 'node:readline';

// ---- tiny arg parser --------------------------------------------------------
const args = Object.fromEntries(process.argv.slice(2).reduce((a, cur, i, arr) => {
  if (cur.startsWith('--')) a.push([cur.slice(2), arr[i + 1]?.startsWith('--') ? true : arr[i + 1]]);
  return a;
}, []));

const IN   = args.in;
const OUT  = args.out || './supabase/migrations/0006_seed_catalog.sql';
const MAXMAG = Number(args['max-mag'] ?? 6.5);       // 6.5 ~ naked-eye limit
const PER_CON = Number(args['per-constellation'] ?? 400);
const LIMIT  = Number(args.limit ?? 20000);          // premium curated pool size

if (!IN) {
  console.error('ERROR: --in <hyg csv path> is required. See header for usage.');
  process.exit(1);
}

// ---- visibility classification (honest, magnitude-based) --------------------
function visibilityClass(mag) {
  if (mag == null || Number.isNaN(mag)) return 'telescope';
  if (mag <= 6.0) return 'naked_eye';        // dark-sky naked eye
  if (mag <= 9.0) return 'binocular';
  if (mag <= 12.0) return 'small_telescope';
  return 'telescope';
}

// ---- SQL string escaping ----------------------------------------------------
const q = (s) => (s == null || s === '') ? 'null' : `'${String(s).replace(/'/g, "''")}'`;
const n = (x) => (x == null || x === '' || Number.isNaN(Number(x))) ? 'null' : Number(x);

async function main() {
  const rl = readline.createInterface({ input: fs.createReadStream(IN), crlfDelay: Infinity });

  let header = null;
  let col = {};
  const perCon = new Map();
  const rows = [];
  let seen = 0, kept = 0;

  for await (const line of rl) {
    if (!header) {
      header = line.split(',').map((h) => h.trim().toLowerCase());
      header.forEach((h, i) => (col[h] = i));
      // sanity: HYG must have these columns
      for (const req of ['ra', 'dec', 'mag', 'con']) {
        if (!(req in col)) { console.error(`Missing HYG column: ${req}`); process.exit(1); }
      }
      continue;
    }
    seen++;
    const f = line.split(',');

    // HYG RA is in HOURS (0..24). Convert to degrees.
    const raHours = parseFloat(f[col.ra]);
    const dec = parseFloat(f[col.dec]);
    const mag = parseFloat(f[col.mag]);
    const con = (f[col.con] || '').trim();
    const dist = col.dist != null ? parseFloat(f[col.dist]) : NaN;
    const proper = col.proper != null ? (f[col.proper] || '').trim() : '';
    const hip = col.hip != null ? (f[col.hip] || '').trim() : '';
    const hd  = col.hd  != null ? (f[col.hd]  || '').trim() : '';
    const hr  = col.hr  != null ? (f[col.hr]  || '').trim() : '';
    const ci  = col.ci  != null ? parseFloat(f[col.ci]) : NaN;
    const spect = col.spect != null ? (f[col.spect] || '').trim() : '';

    // ---- curation filters ----
    if (!Number.isFinite(raHours) || !Number.isFinite(dec)) continue;   // need coords
    if (dec < -90 || dec > 90) continue;
    if (!Number.isFinite(mag)) continue;                                // need brightness
    if (mag > MAXMAG) continue;                                         // premium cutoff
    if (Number.isFinite(dist) && dist >= 100000) continue;             // dubious parallax
    if (!con) continue;                                                 // need constellation
    if (proper) continue;             // exclude IAU-named famous stars (avoid confusion)
    if (raHours === 0 && dec === 0) continue;                           // skip the Sun (id 0)

    // constellation balancing
    const cKey = con.toLowerCase();
    const cnt = perCon.get(cKey) || 0;
    if (cnt >= PER_CON) continue;
    perCon.set(cKey, cnt + 1);

    const raDeg = ((raHours * 15) % 360 + 360) % 360;

    // build a stable source identifier, prefer HIP > HD > HR
    let srcId = hip ? `HIP ${hip}` : hd ? `HD ${hd}` : hr ? `HR ${hr}` : `HYG ${f[col.id] ?? kept}`;

    rows.push({
      source_catalog: 'HYG',
      source_release: 'HYG v4.4 (2025)',
      source_identifier: srcId,
      ra: raDeg,
      dec,
      con,
      mag,
      ci: Number.isFinite(ci) ? ci : null,
      dist: Number.isFinite(dist) ? dist : null,
      vis: visibilityClass(mag),
      meta: { spect: spect || null, hip: hip || null, hd: hd || null, hr: hr || null },
    });
    kept++;
    if (kept >= LIMIT) break;
  }

  // ---- emit SQL ----
  const head =
`-- =============================================================================
-- TSDR -- Seed catalog (GENERATED by scripts/import_hyg.mjs)
-- Source: HYG Database v4.4  (c) David Nash / astronexus  --  CC BY-SA 4.0
-- https://codeberg.org/astronexus/hyg
-- This derived pool is licensed CC BY-SA 4.0 (share-alike). Do not remove
-- attribution. Curation: mag<=${MAXMAG}, per-constellation<=${PER_CON}, limit=${LIMIT}.
-- Scanned ${seen} HYG rows, kept ${kept} curated stars, ${perCon.size} constellations.
-- =============================================================================
begin;
`;
  const tail = `\ncommit;\n`;

  let body = '';
  const CHUNK = 500;
  for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i += CHUNK) {
    const slice = rows.slice(i, i + CHUNK);
    body += `insert into star_catalog
 (source_catalog, source_release, source_identifier, right_ascension, declination,
  constellation, apparent_magnitude, color_index, distance_parsecs, visibility_class,
  additional_metadata, allocation_status) values\n`;
    body += slice.map((r) =>
      `(${q(r.source_catalog)}, ${q(r.source_release)}, ${q(r.source_identifier)}, ` +
      `${r.ra.toFixed(6)}, ${r.dec.toFixed(6)}, ${q(r.con)}, ${n(r.mag)}, ${n(r.ci)}, ` +
      `${n(r.dist)}, ${q(r.vis)}, ${q(JSON.stringify(r.meta))}::jsonb, 'available')`
    ).join(',\n');
    body += `\non conflict (source_catalog, source_identifier) do nothing;\n\n`;
  }

  fs.writeFileSync(OUT, head + body + tail);
  console.log(`OK: scanned=${seen} kept=${kept} constellations=${perCon.size}`);
  console.log(`Wrote ${OUT}`);
}

main().catch((e) => { console.error(e); process.exit(1); });
=====================================================
FILE: scripts\perf_audit.mjs
=====================================================
#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * TSDR -- performance audit (Run 4). Static, dependency-free checks:
 *   - measures each page's critical weight (HTML + linked CSS + linked JS)
 *   - flags render-blocking scripts (no defer/async in <head>)
 *   - flags unused preconnect (host not used by any link/src)
 *   - flags large assets in public/assets
 *   - confirms scripts use defer and images use loading="lazy" where present
 * Budgets: critical weight <= 150 KB warn, <= 250 KB error per page.
 */
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';

const ROOT = path.resolve(process.argv[2] || 'public');
const WARN_KB = 150, ERR_KB = 250;
const sizeKB = (p) => fs.existsSync(p) ? fs.statSync(p).size / 1024 : 0;

function walk(d){const o=[];for(const e of fs.readdirSync(d,{withFileTypes:true})){
  const p=path.join(d,e.name); if(e.isDirectory())o.push(...walk(p));
  else if(e.name.endsWith('.html'))o.push(p);} return o;}

let errors=0, warns=0;
const err=(f,m)=>{errors++;console.log(`  x [err]  ${f}: ${m}`);};
const warn=(f,m)=>{warns++;console.log(`  ! [warn] ${f}: ${m}`);};

function localPath(href){
  if(!href.startsWith('/')) return null;      // external
  return path.join(ROOT, href.replace(/^\//,''));
}

for (const file of walk(ROOT)) {
  const rel=path.relative(ROOT,file);
  const html=fs.readFileSync(file,'utf8');
  let weight = Buffer.byteLength(html) / 1024;

  // linked CSS
  for(const m of html.matchAll(/<link[^>]*rel="stylesheet"[^>]*href="([^"]+)"/g)){
    const p=localPath(m[1]); if(p) weight += sizeKB(p);
  }
  // linked JS
  const headEnd = html.indexOf('</head>');
  for(const m of html.matchAll(/<script\b([^>]*)\bsrc="([^"]+)"([^>]*)>/g)){
    const attrs = m[1]+m[3];
    const p=localPath(m[2]);
    if(p) weight += sizeKB(p);
    const pos = m.index;
    const blocking = pos < headEnd && !/\bdefer\b|\basync\b/.test(attrs);
    if(blocking) err(rel, `render-blocking script in <head>: ${m[2]}`);
    // external scripts should be defer/async too (perf)
    if(!p && !/\bdefer\b|\basync\b/.test(attrs)) warn(rel, `external script without defer/async: ${m[2]}`);
  }

  // unused preconnect
  for(const m of html.matchAll(/<link[^>]*rel="preconnect"[^>]*href="([^"]+)"/g)){
    const host=m[1];
    const used = html.includes('href="'+host) || html.includes('src="'+host) ||
                 html.includes(host.replace(/^https?:\/\//,''));
    // check if any @font-face / stylesheet actually pulls from it -- heuristic:
    const referenced = new RegExp(host.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,'\\$&')).test(html) &&
                       (html.match(new RegExp(host,'g'))||[]).length>1;
    if(!referenced) warn(rel,`preconnect to ${host} appears unused`);
  }

  // budget
  const kb = Math.round(weight*10)/10;
  if(weight>ERR_KB) err(rel,`critical weight ${kb} KB > ${ERR_KB} KB`);
  else if(weight>WARN_KB) warn(rel,`critical weight ${kb} KB > ${WARN_KB} KB`);
  else console.log(`  \u00b7 ${rel}: ${kb} KB`);
}

// asset sizes
const assets=path.join(ROOT,'assets');
if(fs.existsSync(assets)) for(const f of fs.readdirSync(assets)){
  const kb=sizeKB(path.join(assets,f));
  if(kb>300) warn('assets/'+f, `${Math.round(kb)} KB (optimise/serve responsive)`);
}

console.log(`\n================  PERF: ${errors} errors, ${warns} warnings  ================\n`);
process.exit(errors===0?0:1);
=====================================================
FILE: scripts\seo_audit.mjs
=====================================================
#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * TSDR -- SEO audit (Run 4). Static checks across every .html under public/:
 *   - <title> present and reasonable length
 *   - meta description present (50-170 chars ideal)
 *   - canonical link present (except error/utility pages)
 *   - Open Graph title + description present on indexable pages
 *   - exactly one <h1>
 *   - noindex pages (account/admin/404/reveal) are NOT in sitemap
 *   - every non-noindex page IS in sitemap.xml
 * Warnings don't fail; missing canonical/description on indexable pages = error.
 */
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';

const ROOT = path.resolve(process.argv[2] || 'public');
const SITEMAP = path.join(ROOT, 'sitemap.xml');
const NOINDEX = new Set(['404.html','reveal.html','account/index.html','admin/index.html']);

function walk(d){const o=[];for(const e of fs.readdirSync(d,{withFileTypes:true})){
  const p=path.join(d,e.name); if(e.isDirectory())o.push(...walk(p));
  else if(e.name.endsWith('.html'))o.push(p);} return o;}

let errors=0, warns=0;
const err=(f,m)=>{errors++;console.log(`  x [err]  ${f}: ${m}`);};
const warn=(f,m)=>{warns++;console.log(`  ! [warn] ${f}: ${m}`);};

const sitemap = fs.existsSync(SITEMAP)? fs.readFileSync(SITEMAP,'utf8') : '';

for (const file of walk(ROOT)) {
  const rel=path.relative(ROOT,file).replace(/\\/g,'/');
  const html=fs.readFileSync(file,'utf8');
  const isNoindex = NOINDEX.has(rel) || /<meta[^>]*name="robots"[^>]*noindex/i.test(html);

  const title=/<title>([^<]*)<\/title>/i.exec(html);
  if(!title || !title[1].trim()) err(rel,'missing <title>');
  else if(title[1].length>65) warn(rel,`title long (${title[1].length} chars)`);

  const desc=/<meta[^>]*name="description"[^>]*content="([^"]*)"/i.exec(html);
  if(!isNoindex){
    if(!desc || !desc[1].trim()) err(rel,'missing meta description');
    else if(desc[1].length<50||desc[1].length>180) warn(rel,`description length ${desc[1].length} (aim 50-170)`);
  }

  const canonical=/<link[^>]*rel="canonical"/i.test(html);
  if(!isNoindex && !canonical) err(rel,'missing canonical');

  if(!isNoindex){
    if(!/property="og:title"/.test(html)) warn(rel,'missing og:title');
    if(!/property="og:description"/.test(html)) warn(rel,'missing og:description');
  }

  const h1=(html.match(/<h1\b/gi)||[]).length;
  if(h1!==1) warn(rel,`${h1} <h1> (want exactly 1)`);

  // sitemap membership
  const url='https://www.stardedicationregistry.example/'+(rel==='index.html'?'':rel);
  const inSitemap = sitemap.includes('/'+ (rel==='index.html'?'':rel)) ||
                    (rel==='index.html' && sitemap.includes('.example/</loc>'));
  if(isNoindex && inSitemap && rel!=='index.html') err(rel,'noindex page present in sitemap');
  if(!isNoindex && !inSitemap) warn(rel,'indexable page missing from sitemap');
}

// robots.txt sanity
const robots=path.join(ROOT,'robots.txt');
if(!fs.existsSync(robots)) err('robots.txt','missing');
else{ const r=fs.readFileSync(robots,'utf8');
  if(!/Sitemap:/i.test(r)) warn('robots.txt','no Sitemap: line');
  if(!/Disallow:\s*\/admin\//.test(r)) warn('robots.txt','admin not disallowed');
}

console.log(`\n================  SEO: ${errors} errors, ${warns} warnings  ================\n`);
process.exit(errors===0?0:1);
=====================================================
FILE: scripts\test\mock_db.mjs
=====================================================
/**
 * TSDR -- in-memory mock of the Supabase RPC surface used by the functions.
 * It faithfully re-implements the SQL RPCs' externally observable behaviour so
 * the Netlify handlers can be exercised end-to-end without Postgres.
 * (The real .sql is integration-tested against Supabase in the launch step.)
 */
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
const cryptoRef = crypto;

export function makeMockDb(opts = {}) {
  const stars = [];        // {id, status, constellation, visibility_class, ...}
  const reservations = new Map();
  const dedications = new Map();
  const payments = new Map();   // idempotency_key -> row
  const ordersByRef = new Map(); // order_reference -> { dedication_id, amount, currency }
  const events = [];
  const regNumbers = new Set();
  let rlStore = new Map();
  let seq = 0;

  // seed a pool
  const poolSize = opts.poolSize ?? 5;
  for (let i = 0; i < poolSize; i++) {
    stars.push({
      id: crypto.randomUUID(), status: 'available',
      source_identifier: `DEMO-${i}`, source_catalog: 'TSDR-DEMO',
      constellation: ['Ori', 'Lyr', 'CMa'][i % 3],
      right_ascension: (i * 30) % 360, declination: (i * 5) % 80 - 40,
      apparent_magnitude: 2 + (i % 5) * 0.7, visibility_class: 'naked_eye',
    });
  }

  function genReg() {
    const AL = '0123456789ABCDEFGHJKMNPQRSTVWXYZ';
    for (;;) {
      let c = '';
      for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) c += AL[crypto.randomBytes(1)[0] & 31];
      const cand = `TSDR-${new Date().getFullYear()}-${c}`;
      if (!regNumbers.has(cand)) { regNumbers.add(cand); return cand; }
    }
  }

  const RPC = {
    tsdr_rate_limit_hit({ p_key, p_max, p_window_secs }) {
      const now = Date.now();
      const arr = (rlStore.get(p_key) || []).filter((t) => now - t < p_window_secs * 1000);
      arr.push(now); rlStore.set(p_key, arr);
      return [{ allowed: arr.length <= p_max, hit_count: arr.length }];
    },

    tsdr_reserve_star({ p_session_ref, p_constellation, p_visibility, p_hold_minutes }) {
      const s = stars.find((x) => x.status === 'available'
        && (!p_constellation || x.constellation === p_constellation)
        && (!p_visibility || x.visibility_class === p_visibility));
      if (!s) { const e = new Error('NO_STAR_AVAILABLE'); throw e; }
      s.status = 'reserved';
      const token = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
      const hash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(token).digest('hex');
      const id = crypto.randomUUID();
      reservations.set(id, { id, star_id: s.id, session: p_session_ref, hash,
        status: 'held', expires_at: Date.now() + (p_hold_minutes ?? 20) * 60000 });
      events.push({ type: 'star_reserved', star: s.id });
      return [{
        reservation_id: id, star_id: s.id, reservation_token: token,
        source_identifier: s.source_identifier, constellation: s.constellation,
        right_ascension: s.right_ascension, declination: s.declination,
        visibility_class: s.visibility_class,
        expires_at: new Date(reservations.get(id).expires_at).toISOString(),
      }];
    },

    tsdr_expire_reservations() {
      let freed = 0; const now = Date.now();
      for (const r of reservations.values()) {
        if (r.status === 'held' && r.expires_at < now) {
          r.status = 'expired';
          const s = stars.find((x) => x.id === r.star_id);
          if (s && s.status === 'reserved') { s.status = 'available'; freed++; }
        }
      }
      return freed;
    },

    tsdr_create_draft_dedication(a) {
      const r = reservations.get(a.p_reservation_id);
      if (!r) throw new Error('RESERVATION_NOT_FOUND');
      if (r.status !== 'held') throw new Error('RESERVATION_NOT_HELD');
      if (r.expires_at < Date.now()) throw new Error('RESERVATION_EXPIRED');
      const hash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(a.p_reservation_token).digest('hex');
      if (hash !== r.hash) throw new Error('RESERVATION_TOKEN_MISMATCH');
      const id = crypto.randomUUID();
      dedications.set(id, {
        id, star_id: r.star_id, reservation_id: r.id, status: 'pending_payment',
        regno: null, order_reference: null,
        honouree: a.p_honouree, symbolic: a.p_symbolic_name, occasion: a.p_occasion,
        privacy: a.p_privacy, purchaser: a.p_purchaser || null,
        dedicator: a.p_dedicator || null, message: a.p_message || null,
        dedication_date: a.p_dedication_date || null,
        buyer_email: a.p_buyer_email || null, buyer_phone: a.p_buyer_phone || null,
        tokenHash: a.p_keepsake_token_hash || null,
      });
      return id;
    },

    tsdr_get_price() {
      return 1499;
    },
    tsdr_get_pending_for_payment({ p_dedication_id }) {
      const d = dedications.get(p_dedication_id);
      if (!d || d.status !== 'pending_payment') return [];
      return [{ dedication_id: d.id, registration_status: d.status }];
    },

    tsdr_attach_order({ p_dedication_id, p_order_reference, p_amount, p_currency }) {
      const d = dedications.get(p_dedication_id);
      if (!d) throw new Error('NOT_FOUND');
      if (d.status !== 'pending_payment') throw new Error('NOT_PENDING');
      d.order_reference = p_order_reference;
      ordersByRef.set(p_order_reference, {
        dedication_id: p_dedication_id, amount: p_amount, currency: p_currency, consumed: false,
      });
      return null;
    },

    // Run D: public-safe order-status resolver for the Cashfree return.
    tsdr_order_status({ p_order_reference }) {
      const d = [...dedications.values()].find((x) => x.order_reference === p_order_reference);
      if (!d) return [];
      return [{ registration_status: d.status, registration_number: d.regno || null,
        privacy_level: d.privacy }];
    },

    tsdr_record_payment_event({ p_order_reference, p_status, p_provider_payment_id }) {
      const key = `evt:${p_provider_payment_id || p_order_reference}:${p_status}`;
      if (!payments.has(key)) payments.set(key, { status: p_status });
      return null;
    },

    tsdr_activate_by_order(a) {
      // idempotency short-circuit
      if (payments.has(a.p_idempotency_key)) {
        const d = [...dedications.values()].find((x) => x.order_reference === a.p_order_reference);
        return [{ registration_number: d?.regno, already_processed: true }];
      }
      const order = ordersByRef.get(a.p_order_reference);
      let d;
      if (!order) {
        d = [...dedications.values()].find((x) => x.order_reference === a.p_order_reference);
        if (!d) throw new Error('ORDER_NOT_FOUND');
        if (d.status === 'active') { // already active replay
          payments.set(a.p_idempotency_key, { status: 'paid' });
          return [{ registration_number: d.regno, already_processed: true }];
        }
      } else {
        if (Math.round(a.p_amount * 100) !== Math.round(order.amount * 100))
          throw new Error(`AMOUNT_MISMATCH expected=${order.amount} got=${a.p_amount}`);
        if (String(a.p_currency).toUpperCase() !== String(order.currency).toUpperCase())
          throw new Error(`CURRENCY_MISMATCH`);
        d = dedications.get(order.dedication_id);
      }
      if (d.status !== 'pending_payment') throw new Error('DEDICATION_NOT_PENDING');
      const regno = genReg();
      d.status = 'active'; d.regno = regno;
      const r = reservations.get(d.reservation_id); if (r) r.status = 'consumed';
      const s = stars.find((x) => x.id === d.star_id); if (s) s.status = 'allocated';
      payments.set(a.p_idempotency_key, { status: 'paid' });
      events.push({ type: 'activated', regno });
      return [{ registration_number: regno, already_processed: false }];
    },

    tsdr_verify({ p_regno }) {
      const d = [...dedications.values()].find((x) => x.regno === p_regno
        && ['active', 'revoked', 'refunded'].includes(x.status));
      if (!d) return [];
      const s = stars.find((x) => x.id === d.star_id);
      return [{
        registration_number: d.regno, registration_status: d.status,
        privacy_level: d.privacy, occasion: d.occasion, activated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
        constellation: s.constellation, right_ascension: s.right_ascension,
        declination: s.declination, source_catalog: 'TSDR-DEMO',
        source_identifier: s.source_identifier,
        is_public: d.privacy === 'public', has_message: false,
      }];
    },

    tsdr_account_dedications({ p_user }) {
      return [...dedications.values()].filter((d) => d.purchaser === p_user).map((d) => {
        const s = stars.find((x) => x.id === d.star_id) || {};
        return {
          registration_number: d.regno, symbolic_star_name: d.symbolic,
          honouree_name: d.honouree, occasion: d.occasion, privacy_level: d.privacy,
          registration_status: d.status, dedication_date: d.dedication_date || null,
          constellation: s.constellation || null, source_identifier: s.source_identifier || null,
          reveal_status: d.status === 'active' ? 'ready' : 'pending',
          activated_at: d.status === 'active' ? new Date().toISOString() : null,
        };
      });
    },

    // Run C: owner-authorised resolver (any privacy) -- only for records the
    // caller owns. Mirrors migration 0014 tsdr_owner_record.
    tsdr_owner_record({ p_user, p_regno }) {
      const d = [...dedications.values()].find(
        (x) => x.regno === p_regno && x.purchaser === p_user && x.status === 'active');
      if (!d) return [];
      const s = stars.find((x) => x.id === d.star_id) || {};
      return [{
        dedication_id: d.id, registration_number: d.regno,
        symbolic_star_name: d.symbolic, honouree_name: d.honouree,
        dedicator_name: d.dedicator || null, dedication_message: d.message || null,
        occasion: d.occasion, reveal_mode: d.reveal_mode || null,
        dedication_date: d.dedication_date || null, privacy_level: d.privacy,
        constellation: s.constellation, source_catalog: s.source_catalog || 'TSDR-DEMO',
        source_identifier: s.source_identifier, right_ascension: s.right_ascension,
        declination: s.declination, apparent_magnitude: s.apparent_magnitude ?? 4,
        visibility_class: s.visibility_class,
        certificate_object_key: d.certKey || null, star_map_object_key: d.mapKey || null,
        activated_at: d.status === 'active' ? new Date().toISOString() : null,
      }];
    },

    tsdr_admin_metrics() {
      return {
        stars_available: stars.filter((s) => s.status === 'available').length,
        stars_allocated: stars.filter((s) => s.status === 'allocated').length,
        dedications_active: [...dedications.values()].filter((d) => d.status === 'active').length,
      };
    },

    tsdr_admin_set_status({ p_registration_number, p_status }) {
      const d = [...dedications.values()].find((x) => x.regno === p_registration_number);
      if (!d) throw new Error('NOT_FOUND');
      if (!['revoked', 'refunded', 'active'].includes(p_status)) throw new Error('INVALID_STATUS');
      d.status = p_status; events.push({ type: 'status_changed', to: p_status });
      return null;
    },

    tsdr_admin_correct({ p_registration_number, p_field }) {
      const d = [...dedications.values()].find((x) => x.regno === p_registration_number);
      if (!d) throw new Error('NOT_FOUND');
      if (!['honouree_name','symbolic_star_name','dedicator_name','dedication_message','privacy_level'].includes(p_field))
        throw new Error('FIELD_NOT_CORRECTABLE');
      return null;
    },

    tsdr_admin_review({ p_registration_number, p_decision }) {
      const d = [...dedications.values()].find((x) => x.regno === p_registration_number);
      if (!d) throw new Error('NOT_FOUND');
      if (!['approved','rejected','pending'].includes(p_decision)) throw new Error('INVALID_DECISION');
      return null;
    },

    // ---- Run 3 RPCs ----
    tsdr_reveal_public({ p_regno }) {
      const d = [...dedications.values()].find((x) => x.regno === p_regno
        && x.status === 'active' && x.privacy === 'public');
      if (!d) return [];
      const s = stars.find((x) => x.id === d.star_id);
      return [{ registration_number: d.regno, symbolic_star_name: d.symbolic,
        honouree_name: d.honouree, dedicator_name: d.dedicator || null,
        dedication_message: d.message || null, occasion: d.occasion, reveal_mode: null,
        constellation: s.constellation, source_identifier: s.source_identifier,
        right_ascension: s.right_ascension, declination: s.declination,
        visibility_class: s.visibility_class }];
    },
    tsdr_reveal_by_token({ p_token }) {
      const crypto = cryptoRef;
      const hash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(p_token).digest('hex');
      const d = [...dedications.values()].find((x) => x.tokenHash === hash && x.status === 'active');
      if (!d) return [];
      const s = stars.find((x) => x.id === d.star_id);
      return [{ registration_number: d.regno, symbolic_star_name: d.symbolic,
        honouree_name: d.honouree, dedicator_name: d.dedicator || null,
        dedication_message: d.message || null, occasion: d.occasion, reveal_mode: null,
        constellation: s.constellation, source_identifier: s.source_identifier,
        right_ascension: s.right_ascension, declination: s.declination,
        visibility_class: s.visibility_class }];
    },
    tsdr_star_neighbors({ p_ra, p_dec, p_max }) {
      return stars.slice(0, Math.min(p_max, stars.length)).map((s) => ({
        source_identifier: s.source_identifier, right_ascension: s.right_ascension,
        declination: s.declination, apparent_magnitude: 5 }));
    },
    tsdr_media_register({ p_user, p_dedication_id }) {
      const d = dedications.get(p_dedication_id);
      if (!d || d.purchaser !== p_user) return false;
      return true;
    },
    tsdr_artifact_key({ p_type, p_regno, p_token }) {
      let d;
      if (p_regno) d = [...dedications.values()].find((x) => x.regno === p_regno);
      if (!d && p_token) {
        const hash = cryptoRef.createHash('sha256').update(p_token).digest('hex');
        d = [...dedications.values()].find((x) => x.tokenHash === hash);
      }
      if (!d || d.status !== 'active') return [];
      if (p_regno && d.privacy !== 'public' && d.privacy !== 'limited' && !p_token) return [];
      return [{ object_key: p_type === 'certificate'
        ? (d.certKey || 'cert/' + d.regno) : (d.mapKey || 'map/' + d.regno) }];
    },
    // ---- Run A RPCs (migration 0012) ----
    tsdr_promo_quote({ p_code, p_amount }) {
      const code = String(p_code || '').toUpperCase();
      if (code === 'TESTFREE100') {
        return [{ valid: true, percent_off: 100, final_amount: 0, reason: 'OK' }];
      }
      return [{ valid: false, percent_off: 0, final_amount: p_amount, reason: 'PROMO_INVALID' }];
    },
    tsdr_redeem_free_dedication({ p_dedication_id, p_idempotency_key }) {
      if (payments.has(p_idempotency_key)) {
        const d0 = dedications.get(p_dedication_id);
        return [{ registration_number: d0 && d0.regno, already_processed: true }];
      }
      const d = dedications.get(p_dedication_id);
      if (!d) throw new Error('DEDICATION_NOT_FOUND');
      if (d.status !== 'pending_payment' && d.status !== 'active') throw new Error('DEDICATION_NOT_PENDING');
      if (d.status !== 'active') {
        d.status = 'active'; d.regno = genReg();
        const r = reservations.get(d.reservation_id); if (r) r.status = 'consumed';
        const s = stars.find((x) => x.id === d.star_id); if (s) s.status = 'allocated';
      }
      payments.set(p_idempotency_key, { status: 'paid' });
      return [{ registration_number: d.regno, already_processed: false }];
    },
    tsdr_certificate_view({ p_regno, p_token }) {
      let d;
      if (p_token) {
        const hash = cryptoRef.createHash('sha256').update(p_token).digest('hex');
        d = [...dedications.values()].find((x) => x.tokenHash === hash);
      }
      if (!d && p_regno) {
        d = [...dedications.values()].find((x) => x.regno === p_regno && x.privacy === 'public');
      }
      if (!d || d.status !== 'active') return [];
      const s = stars.find((x) => x.id === d.star_id);
      return [{
        registration_number: d.regno, symbolic_star_name: d.symbolic, honouree_name: d.honouree,
        dedicator_name: d.dedicator || null, dedication_message: d.message || null,
        occasion: d.occasion, dedication_date: d.dedication_date || null, privacy_level: d.privacy,
        constellation: s.constellation, source_catalog: 'TSDR-DEMO', source_identifier: s.source_identifier,
        right_ascension: s.right_ascension, declination: s.declination,
        apparent_magnitude: 4.2, visibility_class: s.visibility_class,
        activated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      }];
    },
    // ---- Run B RPCs (migrations 0009/0010 + 0013) ----
    tsdr_cert_source({ p_dedication_id }) {
      const d = dedications.get(p_dedication_id);
      if (!d || d.status !== 'active') return [];
      const s = stars.find((x) => x.id === d.star_id);
      return [{
        registration_number: d.regno, symbolic_star_name: d.symbolic, honouree_name: d.honouree,
        dedicator_name: d.dedicator || null, dedication_message: d.message || null,
        occasion: d.occasion, dedication_date: d.dedication_date || null,
        constellation: s.constellation, source_catalog: s.source_catalog || 'TSDR-DEMO',
        source_identifier: s.source_identifier, right_ascension: s.right_ascension,
        declination: s.declination, apparent_magnitude: s.apparent_magnitude ?? 4,
        visibility_class: s.visibility_class,
      }];
    },
    tsdr_record_certificate({ p_dedication_id, p_object_key }) {
      const d = dedications.get(p_dedication_id);
      if (!d) throw new Error('DEDICATION_NOT_FOUND');
      d.certKey = p_object_key;
      d.certVersion = (d.certVersion || 0) + 1;
      return d.certVersion;
    },
    tsdr_attach_starmap({ p_dedication_id, p_object_key }) {
      const d = dedications.get(p_dedication_id);
      if (d) d.mapKey = p_object_key;
      return null;
    },
    tsdr_artifacts_target({ p_regno, p_token }) {
      let d;
      if (p_token) {
        const hash = cryptoRef.createHash('sha256').update(p_token).digest('hex');
        d = [...dedications.values()].find((x) => x.tokenHash === hash);
      }
      if (!d && p_regno) d = [...dedications.values()].find((x) => x.regno === p_regno);
      if (!d || d.status !== 'active') return [];
      // Private/unlisted require a token; rn alone reveals nothing.
      if (!p_token && d.privacy !== 'public' && d.privacy !== 'limited') return [];
      return [{ dedication_id: d.id, certificate_object_key: d.certKey || null,
        star_map_object_key: d.mapKey || null, privacy_level: d.privacy }];
    },
    tsdr_keepsake_contact({ p_token }) {
      const hash = cryptoRef.createHash('sha256').update(p_token).digest('hex');
      const d = [...dedications.values()].find((x) => x.tokenHash === hash && x.status === 'active');
      if (!d) return [];
      return [{ registration_number: d.regno, buyer_email: d.buyer_email || null,
        symbolic_star_name: d.symbolic, honouree_name: d.honouree }];
    },
  };

  const db = {
    async rpc(fn, args) {
      try {
        if (!RPC[fn]) return { data: null, error: { message: `no such rpc ${fn}` } };
        const data = RPC[fn](args || {});
        return { data, error: null };
      } catch (e) {
        return { data: null, error: { message: e.message } };
      }
    },
    _state: { stars, reservations, dedications, payments, ordersByRef, events },
  };
  return db;
}

FILE: scripts\test\test_artifacts.mjs
=====================================================
#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * TSDR -- artifact generation tests (EXECUTED, real files produced).
 * Renders a real star map (SVG+PNG) and a real certificate PDF, then:
 *   - validates star-map projection (centre star at chart centre; points in-bounds)
 *   - rasterises the map with sharp and checks PNG dimensions
 *   - builds the certificate PDF with pdf-lib, re-parses it with pdfjs-dist and
 *     asserts page count + that key text (registration number) is present
 *   - confirms the verification QR embedded in the PDF decodes to the verify URL
 * Requires NODE_PATH=/opt/oai-docgen/node_modules (pdf-lib, sharp, pdfjs-dist).
 */
import fs from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { createRequire } from 'node:module';
const require = createRequire(import.meta.url);
const { PDFDocument, StandardFonts, rgb } = require('pdf-lib');
const sharp = require('sharp');

import { buildStarMapSVG, selectNeighbors, gnomonic, angularSep } from '../../lib/starmap.mjs';
import { encodeQR, qrToSvg } from '../../lib/qr.mjs';
import { decodeQR } from '../../lib/qr_decode.mjs';
import { generateCertificate } from '../../lib/certificate.mjs';

let PASS = 0, FAIL = 0;
const ok = (c, m) => { if (c) { PASS++; console.log(`  OK ${m}`); } else { FAIL++; console.log(`  x FAIL: ${m}`); } };
const HERE = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const PROJECT_ROOT = path.resolve(HERE, '../..');
const OUT = path.join(HERE, '_artifacts');
fs.mkdirSync(OUT, { recursive: true });

// Load the checked-in demo JSON when present. Source dumps intentionally omit it,
// so generate an isolated temporary copy instead of depending on a developer path.
let poolPath = path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, 'scripts', '_demo_pool.json');
if (!fs.existsSync(poolPath)) {
  const tempDir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'tsdr-artifacts-'));
  poolPath = path.join(tempDir, '_demo_pool.json');
  execFileSync(process.execPath, [
    path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, 'scripts', 'generate_demo_pool.mjs'),
    '--count', '300',
    '--sql', path.join(tempDir, '0005_seed_demo_pool.sql'),
    '--json', poolPath,
  ], { stdio: 'ignore' });
}
const pool = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(poolPath, 'utf8'));

const star = {
  right_ascension: 101.287, declination: -16.716, constellation: 'CMa',
  source_identifier: 'HIP 32349', apparent_magnitude: 3.9, source_catalog: 'HYG',
};
const record = {
  registration_number: 'TSDR-2026-K7M2P9QR',
  symbolic_star_name: "Ada's Light",
  honouree_name: 'Ada Lovelace',
  dedicator_name: 'The Analytical Society',
  occasion: 'birthday',
  dedication_date: '2026-12-10',
  dedication_message: 'For the first programmer -- may your light guide every mind that follows.',
  constellation: star.constellation,
  right_ascension: star.right_ascension, declination: star.declination,
  source_catalog: 'HYG', source_identifier: star.source_identifier,
};

async function main() {
  console.log('\n[starmap] projection maths');
  {
    // centre projects to (0,0)
    const g = gnomonic(star.right_ascension, star.declination, star.right_ascension, star.declination);
    ok(Math.abs(g.x) < 1e-9 && Math.abs(g.y) < 1e-9, 'centre star projects to tangent-plane origin');
    // a star 1\u00b0 north projects to +y
    const north = gnomonic(star.right_ascension, star.declination + 1, star.right_ascension, star.declination);
    ok(north.y > 0 && Math.abs(north.x) < 1e-6, '1\u00b0 north -> +y, ~0 x');
    // far hemisphere returns null
    ok(gnomonic(star.right_ascension + 180, -star.declination, star.right_ascension, star.declination) === null,
      'opposite point (>90\u00b0) is culled');
    // angular separation symmetric & zero on self
    ok(Math.abs(angularSep(10, 20, 10, 20)) < 1e-9, 'angularSep self = 0');
  }

  console.log('\n[starmap] SVG build + coordinate validation');
  let mapPng;
  {
    const neighbors = selectNeighbors(star, pool, 14, 200);
    const { svg, plotted, centerPx } = buildStarMapSVG({
      star, neighbors, fovDeg: 14, size: 900, registration: record,
    });
    ok(Math.abs(centerPx.px - 450) < 0.5 && Math.abs(centerPx.py - 450) < 0.5,
      `allocated star at chart centre (got ${centerPx.px.toFixed(1)},${centerPx.py.toFixed(1)})`);
    ok(plotted.every((p) => p.px >= 0 && p.px <= 900 && p.py >= 0 && p.py <= 900),
      `all ${plotted.length} plotted neighbours within canvas bounds`);
    fs.writeFileSync(`${OUT}/starmap.svg`, svg);
    mapPng = await sharp(Buffer.from(svg)).png().toBuffer();
    fs.writeFileSync(`${OUT}/starmap.png`, mapPng);
    const meta = await sharp(mapPng).metadata();
    ok(meta.width === 900 && meta.height === 900, `rasterised PNG is 900x900 (got ${meta.width}x${meta.height})`);
  }

  console.log('\n[certificate] PDF generation');
  let pdfBytes, certHash, qrPngBytes;
  {
    const verifyUrl = `https://tsdr.example/verify.html?rn=${record.registration_number}`;
    const { matrix } = encodeQR(verifyUrl, 'M');
    const qrSvg = qrToSvg(matrix, { moduleSize: 6, quiet: 4 });

    const res = await generateCertificate(
      { PDFDocument, StandardFonts, rgb, sharp },
      { record, verifyUrl, qrSvg, starMapPng: mapPng },
    );
    pdfBytes = res.pdfBytes; certHash = res.certificateHash; qrPngBytes = res.qrPngBytes;
    fs.writeFileSync(`${OUT}/certificate.pdf`, pdfBytes);
    ok(pdfBytes.length > 3000, `PDF produced (${pdfBytes.length} bytes)`);
    ok(/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(certHash), 'certificate authenticity hash is sha-256');

    // QR embedded must decode back to the verify URL
    const dec = decodeQR(matrix);
    ok(dec.text === verifyUrl && dec.syndromesZero, 'embedded QR decodes to the verify URL (RS valid)');
  }

  console.log('\n[certificate] re-parse with pdfjs-dist');
  {
    // resolve pdfjs by absolute path (NODE_PATH isn't honoured by dynamic import)
    let pdfjsPath;
    try { pdfjsPath = require.resolve('pdfjs-dist/legacy/build/pdf.mjs'); }
    catch { pdfjsPath = require.resolve('pdfjs-dist/legacy/build/pdf.js'); }
    const pdfjs = await import('file://' + pdfjsPath);
    const task = pdfjs.getDocument({ data: new Uint8Array(pdfBytes), useSystemFonts: true });
    const pdf = await task.promise;
    ok(pdf.numPages === 1, `PDF has 1 page (got ${pdf.numPages})`);
    const pageObj = await pdf.getPage(1);
    const content = await pageObj.getTextContent();
    const text = content.items.map((i) => i.str).join(' ');
    ok(text.includes(record.registration_number), 'registration number present in PDF text');
    ok(/symbolic dedication/i.test(text), 'symbolic-dedication disclosure present in PDF text');
    ok(text.includes(record.honouree_name), 'honouree name present in PDF text');
  }

  console.log(`\nArtifacts written to ${OUT}/ (starmap.svg/png, certificate.pdf)`);
  console.log(`\n================  RESULT: ${PASS} passed, ${FAIL} failed  ================\n`);
  process.exit(FAIL === 0 ? 0 : 1);
}
main().catch((e) => { console.error(e); process.exit(1); });

FILE: scripts\test\test_cert_pipeline.mjs

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * TSDR -- generate-certificate pipeline test (EXECUTED).
 * Wires the real certificate function core() with real libs (pdf-lib, sharp,
 * qr, starmap, certificate) but a MOCK R2 put + mock DB, proving the whole
 * server-side generation path works and emits a valid PDF + records a version.
 */
import { createRequire } from 'node:module';
const require = createRequire(import.meta.url);
const { PDFDocument, StandardFonts, rgb } = require('pdf-lib');
const sharp = require('sharp');

import { core as genCore } from '../../netlify/functions/generate-certificate.mjs';
import { buildStarMapSVG, selectNeighbors } from '../../lib/starmap.mjs';
import { encodeQR, qrToSvg } from '../../lib/qr.mjs';
import { generateCertificate } from '../../lib/certificate.mjs';

let PASS = 0, FAIL = 0;
const ok = (c, m) => { if (c) { PASS++; console.log(`  OK ${m}`); } else { FAIL++; console.log(`  x FAIL: ${m}`); } };

const ENV = { TSDR_PUBLIC_BASE_URL: 'https://tsdr.example', TSDR_INTERNAL_SECRET: 'sekret',
  R2_ACCOUNT_ID: 'a', R2_BUCKET: 'b', R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID: 'k', R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY: 's' };

// mock DB with the two cert RPCs + neighbours + record
const stored = {};
const events = [];
const db = { async rpc(fn, args) {
  if (fn === 'tsdr_cert_source') return { data: [{
    registration_number: 'TSDR-2026-P0B00001', symbolic_star_name: "Ada's Light",
    honouree_name: 'Ada Lovelace', dedicator_name: 'Society',
    dedication_message: 'For the first programmer.', occasion: 'birthday',
    dedication_date: '2026-12-10', constellation: 'CMa', source_catalog: 'HYG',
    source_identifier: 'HIP 32349', right_ascension: 101.287, declination: -16.716,
    apparent_magnitude: 3.9, visibility_class: 'naked_eye' }], error: null };
  if (fn === 'tsdr_star_neighbors') return { data: [
    { source_identifier: 'HIP 1', right_ascension: 101.5, declination: -16.5, apparent_magnitude: 4 },
    { source_identifier: 'HIP 2', right_ascension: 100.9, declination: -17.0, apparent_magnitude: 5 },
  ], error: null };
  if (fn === 'tsdr_record_certificate') { events.push(['cert', args]); return { data: 1, error: null }; }
  if (fn === 'tsdr_attach_starmap') { events.push(['map', args]); return { data: null, error: null }; }
  return { data: null, error: { message: 'no rpc ' + fn } };
} };

const putObject = async (key, buf, ct) => { stored[key] = { bytes: buf.length, ct }; return key; };
const rpc = async (d, fn, a) => { const { data, error } = await d.rpc(fn, a); if (error) throw new Error(error.message); return data; };

const evt = (over = {}) => ({ httpMethod: 'POST', headers: { 'x-tsdr-internal': 'sekret' },
  body: JSON.stringify({ dedication_id: 'd1' }), ...over });

async function main() {
  console.log('\n[generate-certificate pipeline]');

  // unauthorised (no internal secret)
  let r = await genCore(evt({ headers: {} }), { db, env: ENV, deps3: {} });
  ok(r.statusCode === 401, 'missing internal secret -> 401');

  // full run
  r = await genCore(evt(), { db, env: ENV, deps3: {
    rpc, buildStarMapSVG, selectNeighbors, encodeQR, qrToSvg, generateCertificate,
    putObject, PDFDocument, StandardFonts, rgb, sharp,
  } });
  const body = JSON.parse(r.body);
  ok(r.statusCode === 200 && body.ok, 'pipeline returns 200 ok');
  ok(/^certificates\/TSDR-2026-P0B00001\.pdf$/.test(body.certificate_key), 'certificate key formed');
  ok(/^starmaps\/TSDR-2026-P0B00001\.png$/.test(body.star_map_key), 'star-map key formed');
  ok(/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(body.certificate_hash), 'certificate hash is sha-256');
  ok(stored[body.certificate_key] && stored[body.certificate_key].bytes > 3000, 'PDF uploaded to R2 (mock)');
  ok(stored[body.star_map_key] && stored[body.star_map_key].bytes > 1000, 'star-map PNG uploaded to R2 (mock)');
  ok(events.some((e) => e[0] === 'cert') && events.some((e) => e[0] === 'map'),
    'certificate version + star-map attach recorded');

  console.log(`\n================  RESULT: ${PASS} passed, ${FAIL} failed  ================\n`);
  process.exit(FAIL === 0 ? 0 : 1);
}
main().catch((e) => { console.error(e); process.exit(1); });

FILE: scripts\test\test_e2e.mjs

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * TSDR -- END-TO-END journey test (Run 4, EXECUTED).
 * Runs the WHOLE happy path through the real function cores + real libs against
 * one shared mock DB, in order:
 *   reserve -> draft -> pay-init -> pay-webhook(activate) -> verify -> reveal ->
 *   generate-certificate -> download
 * Plus negative checks that private stays private end-to-end.
 *
 * HONEST SCOPE: DB is the in-memory mock mirroring the RPC contract; Cashfree
 * HTTP + R2 are mocked (signature algorithm + PDF/QR/starmap generation are REAL).
 * A live pass is documented in docs/INTEGRATION-TESTING.md.
 */
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import { createRequire } from 'node:module';
const require = createRequire(import.meta.url);
const { PDFDocument, StandardFonts, rgb } = require('pdf-lib');
const sharp = require('sharp');

import { makeMockDb } from './mock_db.mjs';
import { memoryHitImpl } from '../../netlify/functions/_shared/ratelimit.mjs';
import { core as reserve } from '../../netlify/functions/reserve.mjs';
import { core as draft } from '../../netlify/functions/draft.mjs';
import { core as payInit, PRICE_INR } from '../../netlify/functions/pay-init.mjs';
import { core as webhook } from '../../netlify/functions/pay-webhook.mjs';
import { core as verify } from '../../netlify/functions/verify.mjs';
import { core as reveal } from '../../netlify/functions/reveal.mjs';
import { core as download } from '../../netlify/functions/download.mjs';
import { core as genCert } from '../../netlify/functions/generate-certificate.mjs';
import { buildStarMapSVG, selectNeighbors } from '../../lib/starmap.mjs';
import { encodeQR, qrToSvg } from '../../lib/qr.mjs';
import { decodeQR } from '../../lib/qr_decode.mjs';
import { generateCertificate } from '../../lib/certificate.mjs';
import { rpc } from '../../netlify/functions/_shared/supabase.mjs';

let PASS = 0, FAIL = 0;
const ok = (c, m) => { if (c) { PASS++; console.log(`  \u2713 ${m}`); } else { FAIL++; console.log(`  \u2717 FAIL: ${m}`); } };
const ENV = {
  TSDR_PUBLIC_BASE_URL: 'https://tsdr.example', CASHFREE_MODE: 'sandbox',
  CASHFREE_APP_ID: 'app', CASHFREE_SECRET_KEY: 'secret', TSDR_INTERNAL_SECRET: 'internal',
  R2_ACCOUNT_ID: 'a', R2_BUCKET: 'b', R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID: 'k', R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY: 's',
};
const ORIGIN = ENV.TSDR_PUBLIC_BASE_URL;
const J = (r) => JSON.parse(r.body);
const ev = (o = {}) => ({ httpMethod: 'POST', headers: { origin: ORIGIN, 'x-nf-client-connection-ip': '7.7.7.7' },
  queryStringParameters: {}, body: '{}', ...o });
const sign = (raw, ts) => crypto.createHmac('sha256', ENV.CASHFREE_SECRET_KEY).update(ts + raw).digest('base64');

async function main() {
  const db = makeMockDb({ poolSize: 5 });
  const hitImpl = memoryHitImpl();
  const deps = { db, hitImpl, env: ENV };
  const st = db._state;

  console.log('\n[E2E] 1. reserve');
  let r = await reserve(ev({ body: JSON.stringify({ session_ref: 'e2e', constellation: 'Ori' }) }), deps);
  ok(r.statusCode === 200, 'star reserved');
  const reservationId = J(r).reservation_id, token = J(r).reservation_token;

  console.log('[E2E] 2. draft (public dedication)');
  r = await draft(ev({ body: JSON.stringify({
    reservation_id: reservationId, reservation_token: token, purchaser_user_id: 'user-1',
    buyer_email: 'buyer@example.com',
    honouree_name: 'Ada Lovelace', symbolic_star_name: "Ada's Light",
    dedicator_name: 'Society', occasion: 'birthday',
    dedication_message: 'For the first programmer.', privacy_level: 'public',
  }) }), deps);
  ok(r.statusCode === 200, 'draft created');
  const dedicationId = J(r).dedication_id;

  console.log('[E2E] 3. pay-init (server price)');
  let orderId;
  r = await payInit(ev({ body: JSON.stringify({ dedication_id: dedicationId }) }), {
    ...deps, createOrderImpl: async ({ orderId: o, amount }) => {
      orderId = o; ok(amount === PRICE_INR.standard, 'server-set price used'); return { payment_session_id: 'sess_' + o }; },
  });
  ok(r.statusCode === 200 && J(r).payment_session_id, 'payment session created');

  console.log('[E2E] 4. pay-webhook (verified + idempotent activation)');
  const payload = { type: 'PAYMENT_SUCCESS_WEBHOOK', data: {
    order: { order_id: orderId, cf_order_id: 'cf', order_amount: PRICE_INR.standard, order_currency: 'INR' },
    payment: { cf_payment_id: 'pay1', payment_status: 'SUCCESS' } } };
  const raw = JSON.stringify(payload), ts = '1700000000000';
  r = await webhook(ev({ body: raw, headers: { 'x-webhook-signature': sign(raw, ts), 'x-webhook-timestamp': ts } }), { db, env: ENV });
  ok(r.statusCode === 200 && J(r).registration_number, 'activated with registration number');
  const regno = J(r).registration_number;
  // idempotent
  const r2 = await webhook(ev({ body: raw, headers: { 'x-webhook-signature': sign(raw, ts), 'x-webhook-timestamp': ts } }), { db, env: ENV });
  ok(J(r2).already_processed === true && J(r2).registration_number === regno, 'duplicate webhook idempotent');

  console.log('[E2E] 5. verify (public-safe, no PII)');
  r = await verify(ev({ httpMethod: 'GET', queryStringParameters: { rn: regno } }), deps);
  ok(r.statusCode === 200 && J(r).registration_number === regno, 'verify returns record');
  ok(!('honouree_name' in J(r)) && !('dedication_message' in J(r)), 'verify never leaks names/message');

  console.log('[E2E] 6. reveal (public by rn)');
  r = await reveal(ev({ httpMethod: 'GET', queryStringParameters: { rn: regno } }), deps);
  ok(r.statusCode === 200 && J(r).symbolic_star_name === "Ada's Light", 'reveal returns public content');
  ok(J(r).mode === 'celebration', 'reveal mode derived (birthday->celebration)');

  console.log('[E2E] 7. generate-certificate (real PDF + map + QR)');
  // wire cert RPCs into this mock
  const ded = [...st.dedications.values()].find((d) => d.regno === regno);
  const star = st.stars.find((s) => s.id === ded.star_id);
  const certDb = { async rpc(fn, args) {
    if (fn === 'tsdr_cert_source') return { data: [{
      registration_number: regno, symbolic_star_name: ded.symbolic, honouree_name: ded.honouree,
      dedicator_name: 'Society', dedication_message: 'For the first programmer.', occasion: 'birthday',
      dedication_date: '2026-12-10', constellation: star.constellation, source_catalog: 'HYG',
      source_identifier: star.source_identifier, right_ascension: star.right_ascension,
      declination: star.declination, apparent_magnitude: 4, visibility_class: 'naked_eye' }], error: null };
    if (fn === 'tsdr_star_neighbors') return { data: [], error: null };
    if (fn === 'tsdr_record_certificate') { ded.certKey = args.p_object_key; return { data: 1, error: null }; }
    if (fn === 'tsdr_attach_starmap') { ded.mapKey = args.p_object_key; return { data: null, error: null }; }
    return { data: null, error: { message: 'no rpc ' + fn } };
  } };
  const stored = {};
  const putObject = async (key, buf) => { stored[key] = buf.length; return key; };
  r = await genCert(ev({ headers: { 'x-tsdr-internal': 'internal' }, body: JSON.stringify({ dedication_id: 'd' }) }), {
    db: certDb, env: ENV, deps3: { rpc, buildStarMapSVG, selectNeighbors, encodeQR, qrToSvg,
      generateCertificate, putObject, PDFDocument, StandardFonts, rgb, sharp } });
  ok(r.statusCode === 200 && J(r).certificate_hash, 'certificate generated + stored');
  ok(stored[J(r).certificate_key] > 3000, 'PDF bytes stored to (mock) R2');
  // QR embedded decodes to verify URL
  const { matrix } = encodeQR(`${ENV.TSDR_PUBLIC_BASE_URL}/verify.html?rn=${regno}`, 'M');
  ok(decodeQR(matrix).text.endsWith(regno), 'verification QR encodes the registry URL');

  console.log('[E2E] 8. download (signed URL for public cert)');
  ded.certKey = ded.certKey || ('certificates/' + regno + '.pdf');
  r = await download(ev({ httpMethod: 'GET', queryStringParameters: { type: 'certificate', rn: regno } }), deps);
  ok(r.statusCode === 200 && /X-Amz-Signature=/.test(J(r).url), 'signed certificate download URL issued');

  console.log('[E2E] 9. negative: private dedication stays private end-to-end');
  {
    // make a private dedication with a token
    const s2 = st.stars.find((x) => x.status === 'available');
    const t = 'ptok_' + crypto.randomBytes(6).toString('hex');
    st.dedications.set('dp', { id: 'dp', star_id: s2.id, status: 'active', regno: 'TSDR-2026-PRV00009',
      symbolic: 'Hidden', honouree: 'Secret', occasion: 'memorial', privacy: 'private',
      tokenHash: crypto.createHash('sha256').update(t).digest('hex') });
    let rr = await reveal(ev({ httpMethod: 'GET', queryStringParameters: { rn: 'TSDR-2026-PRV00009' } }), deps);
    ok(rr.statusCode === 404, 'private reveal by rn -> 404 (no leak)');
    rr = await download(ev({ httpMethod: 'GET', queryStringParameters: { type: 'certificate', rn: 'TSDR-2026-PRV00009' } }), deps);
    ok(rr.statusCode === 404, 'private certificate by rn -> 404');
    rr = await reveal(ev({ httpMethod: 'GET', queryStringParameters: { t } }), deps);
    ok(rr.statusCode === 200 && J(rr).symbolic_star_name === 'Hidden', 'private reveal WITH token -> 200');
  }

  console.log(`\n================  E2E RESULT: ${PASS} passed, ${FAIL} failed  ================\n`);
  process.exit(FAIL === 0 ? 0 : 1);
}
main().catch((e) => { console.error(e); process.exit(1); });

FILE: scripts\test\test_functions.mjs

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * TSDR -- Run 2 function tests (EXECUTED here).
 * -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * These import the REAL handler `core()` functions and the REAL shared modules
 * (sanitise, cashfree signature, cors, ratelimit) and run them against a mock
 * DB that reproduces the RPC contract. Cashfree HTTP is mocked; the SIGNATURE
 * algorithm is the real one from cashfree.mjs.
 *
 * HONEST SCOPE: this validates handler + security-gate LOGIC end-to-end without
 * Postgres/Cashfree. A live Supabase + Cashfree sandbox run is still required at
 * launch (documented in RUN-2-REPORT.md -> "What depends on live credentials").
 */
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import { makeMockDb } from './mock_db.mjs';
import { memoryHitImpl } from '../../netlify/functions/_shared/ratelimit.mjs';
import { verifyWebhookSignature } from '../../netlify/functions/_shared/cashfree.mjs';
import { validateDedication, escapeHtml, normalizeRegNo } from '../../netlify/functions/_shared/sanitize.mjs';

import { core as reserveCore } from '../../netlify/functions/reserve.mjs';
import { core as draftCore } from '../../netlify/functions/draft.mjs';
import { core as payInitCore, PRICE_INR } from '../../netlify/functions/pay-init.mjs';
import { core as webhookCore } from '../../netlify/functions/pay-webhook.mjs';
import { core as verifyCore } from '../../netlify/functions/verify.mjs';
import { core as accountCore } from '../../netlify/functions/account.mjs';
import { core as adminCore } from '../../netlify/functions/admin.mjs';

let PASS = 0, FAIL = 0;
const ok = (c, m) => { if (c) { PASS++; console.log(`  \u2713 ${m}`); } else { FAIL++; console.log(`  \u2717 FAIL: ${m}`); } };
const ENV = {
  TSDR_PUBLIC_BASE_URL: 'https://tsdr.example',
  CASHFREE_MODE: 'sandbox',
  CASHFREE_APP_ID: 'app_test',
  CASHFREE_SECRET_KEY: 'secret_test_key',
  TSDR_ADMIN_ALLOWED_EMAILS: 'admin@tsdr.example',
  TSDR_RESERVATION_MINUTES: '20',
};
const ORIGIN = ENV.TSDR_PUBLIC_BASE_URL;
const evt = (over = {}) => ({
  httpMethod: 'POST', headers: { origin: ORIGIN, 'x-nf-client-connection-ip': '1.2.3.4' },
  body: '{}', queryStringParameters: {}, ...over,
});
const body = (o) => JSON.stringify(o);
const J = (res) => JSON.parse(res.body);

async function run() {
  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- reserve
  console.log('\n[reserve]');
  {
    const db = makeMockDb({ poolSize: 2 });
    const deps = { db, hitImpl: memoryHitImpl(), env: ENV };

    let r = await reserveCore(evt({ httpMethod: 'GET' }), deps);
    ok(r.statusCode === 405, 'GET rejected (405)');

    r = await reserveCore(evt({ body: body({}) }), deps);
    ok(r.statusCode === 400, 'missing session_ref -> 400');

    r = await reserveCore(evt({ body: body({ session_ref: 's1' }) }), deps);
    ok(r.statusCode === 200 && J(r).reservation_token, 'reserve success returns token');
    ok(J(r).star.constellation, 'reserve returns star facts');

    await reserveCore(evt({ body: body({ session_ref: 's2' }) }), deps); // exhaust pool (size 2)
    r = await reserveCore(evt({ body: body({ session_ref: 's3' }) }), deps);
    ok(r.statusCode === 409 && J(r).code === 'TSDR_NO_STAR_AVAILABLE', 'pool exhausted -> 409 NO_STAR_AVAILABLE');
  }

  // rate limiting
  {
    const db = makeMockDb({ poolSize: 100 });
    const deps = { db, hitImpl: memoryHitImpl(), env: ENV };
    let last;
    for (let i = 0; i < 31; i++) last = await reserveCore(evt({ body: body({ session_ref: 's' + i }) }), deps);
    ok(last.statusCode === 429, 'reserve rate limit trips after 30/10min');
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- draft
  console.log('\n[draft]');
  let sharedDb, dedicationId, reservationToken, reservationId;
  {
    const db = makeMockDb({ poolSize: 3 });
    sharedDb = db;
    const deps = { db, hitImpl: memoryHitImpl(), env: ENV };
    const res = await reserveCore(evt({ body: body({ session_ref: 'buyer' }) }), deps);
    reservationToken = J(res).reservation_token;
    reservationId = J(res).reservation_id;

    // wrong token
    let r = await draftCore(evt({ body: body({
      reservation_id: reservationId, reservation_token: 'deadbeef',
      buyer_email: 'buyer@example.com',
      honouree_name: 'Ada', symbolic_star_name: 'Ada\'s Star',
      occasion: 'birthday', privacy_level: 'private',
    }) }), deps);
    ok(r.statusCode === 400 && J(r).code === 'TSDR_RESERVATION_INVALID', 'wrong reservation token -> 400 (IDOR blocked)');

    // invalid occasion
    r = await draftCore(evt({ body: body({
      reservation_id: reservationId, reservation_token: reservationToken,
      buyer_email: 'buyer@example.com',
      honouree_name: 'Ada', symbolic_star_name: 'X', occasion: 'nope', privacy_level: 'private',
    }) }), deps);
    ok(r.statusCode === 400 && J(r).code === 'TSDR_VALIDATION', 'invalid occasion -> 400 validation');

    // success
    r = await draftCore(evt({ body: body({
      reservation_id: reservationId, reservation_token: reservationToken,
      buyer_email: 'buyer@example.com',
      honouree_name: 'Ada Lovelace', symbolic_star_name: 'Ada\'s Light',
      occasion: 'birthday', privacy_level: 'public',
    }) }), deps);
    ok(r.statusCode === 200 && J(r).dedication_id, 'draft success returns dedication_id');
    dedicationId = J(r).dedication_id;
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- pay-init
  console.log('\n[pay-init]');
  let orderId;
  {
    const deps = { db: sharedDb, hitImpl: memoryHitImpl(), env: ENV,
      createOrderImpl: async ({ orderId: oid, amount }) => {
        ok(amount === PRICE_INR.standard, 'pay-init used SERVER price (client cannot set amount)');
        return { payment_session_id: 'sess_' + oid, cf_order_id: 'cf_' + oid };
      } };
    const r = await payInitCore(evt({ body: body({ dedication_id: dedicationId, customer_phone: '9999999999' }) }), deps);
    ok(r.statusCode === 200 && J(r).payment_session_id, 'pay-init returns payment_session_id');
    orderId = J(r).order_id;
    ok(/^TSDRORD-/.test(orderId) && orderId !== J(r).payment_session_id, 'order_id is unpredictable & != session');
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- webhook
  console.log('\n[pay-webhook]');
  const signBody = (raw, ts, secret = ENV.CASHFREE_SECRET_KEY) =>
    crypto.createHmac('sha256', secret).update(ts + raw).digest('base64');
  {
    const deps = { db: sharedDb, env: ENV };
    const payload = { type: 'PAYMENT_SUCCESS_WEBHOOK', data: {
      order: { order_id: orderId, cf_order_id: 'cf1', order_amount: PRICE_INR.standard, order_currency: 'INR' },
      payment: { cf_payment_id: 'pay_1', payment_status: 'SUCCESS' } } };
    const raw = JSON.stringify(payload);
    const ts = '1700000000000';

    // bad signature
    let r = await webhookCore(evt({ body: raw, headers: {
      'x-webhook-signature': 'wrong', 'x-webhook-timestamp': ts } }), deps);
    ok(r.statusCode === 401 && J(r).code === 'TSDR_SIGNATURE_INVALID', 'invalid signature -> 401');

    // valid signature -> activation
    const sig = signBody(raw, ts);
    r = await webhookCore(evt({ body: raw, headers: {
      'x-webhook-signature': sig, 'x-webhook-timestamp': ts } }), deps);
    const first = J(r);
    ok(r.statusCode === 200 && first.registration_number && first.already_processed === false,
      'valid webhook activates & returns registration number');

    // duplicate (idempotent)
    r = await webhookCore(evt({ body: raw, headers: {
      'x-webhook-signature': sig, 'x-webhook-timestamp': ts } }), deps);
    ok(J(r).already_processed === true && J(r).registration_number === first.registration_number,
      'duplicate webhook is idempotent (same reg number)');

    // amount tampering (fresh order)
    // create a new draft+order to test mismatch
    const d2 = makeMockDb({ poolSize: 1 });
    const dep2 = { db: d2, hitImpl: memoryHitImpl(), env: ENV };
    const rr = await reserveCore(evt({ body: body({ session_ref: 'z' }) }), dep2);
    const dr = await draftCore(evt({ body: body({ reservation_id: J(rr).reservation_id,
      reservation_token: J(rr).reservation_token, buyer_email: 'buyer@example.com',
      honouree_name: 'B', symbolic_star_name: 'S',
      occasion: 'other', privacy_level: 'private' }) }), dep2);
    let oid2;
    await payInitCore(evt({ body: body({ dedication_id: J(dr).dedication_id }) }),
      { ...dep2, createOrderImpl: async ({ orderId: o }) => { oid2 = o; return { payment_session_id: 's' }; } });
    const tamper = { type: 'x', data: { order: { order_id: oid2, order_amount: 1, order_currency: 'INR' },
      payment: { cf_payment_id: 'p2', payment_status: 'SUCCESS' } } };
    const rawT = JSON.stringify(tamper);
    const sigT = signBody(rawT, ts);
    r = await webhookCore(evt({ body: rawT, headers: { 'x-webhook-signature': sigT, 'x-webhook-timestamp': ts } }),
      { db: d2, env: ENV });
    ok(r.statusCode === 400 && J(r).code === 'TSDR_AMOUNT_MISMATCH', 'amount tampering -> 400 AMOUNT_MISMATCH');

    // failed payment event is acknowledged (200) but not activated
    const failP = { type: 'x', data: { order: { order_id: orderId, order_amount: PRICE_INR.standard, order_currency: 'INR' },
      payment: { cf_payment_id: 'p3', payment_status: 'FAILED' } } };
    const rawF = JSON.stringify(failP); const sigF = signBody(rawF, ts);
    r = await webhookCore(evt({ body: rawF, headers: { 'x-webhook-signature': sigF, 'x-webhook-timestamp': ts } }),
      { db: sharedDb, env: ENV });
    ok(r.statusCode === 200 && J(r).ignored === true, 'FAILED payment acknowledged & ignored (not activated)');
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- verify
  console.log('\n[verify]');
  {
    const deps = { db: sharedDb, hitImpl: memoryHitImpl(), env: ENV };
    // grab the active reg number from state
    const active = [...sharedDb._state.dedications.values()].find((d) => d.status === 'active');

    let r = await verifyCore(evt({ httpMethod: 'GET', queryStringParameters: { rn: 'not-a-number' } }), deps);
    ok(r.statusCode === 400, 'bad format -> 400 (no DB hit)');

    r = await verifyCore(evt({ httpMethod: 'GET', queryStringParameters: { rn: 'TSDR-2026-ABCDEFGH' } }), deps);
    ok(r.statusCode === 404, 'nonexistent valid-format -> 404 uniform');

    r = await verifyCore(evt({ httpMethod: 'GET', queryStringParameters: { rn: active.regno } }), deps);
    const v = J(r);
    ok(r.statusCode === 200 && v.registration_number === active.regno, 'existing -> 200 verified');
    ok(!('honouree_name' in v) && !('dedication_message' in v), 'verify NEVER leaks names/message');

    // rate limit (15 / 5min)
    const deps2 = { db: sharedDb, hitImpl: memoryHitImpl(), env: ENV };
    let last;
    for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++)
      last = await verifyCore(evt({ httpMethod: 'GET', queryStringParameters: { rn: 'TSDR-2026-ZZZZZZZZ' } }), deps2);
    ok(last.statusCode === 429, 'verify rate limit trips after 15/5min (enumeration resistance)');
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- account
  console.log('\n[account]');
  {
    const deps = { db: sharedDb, env: ENV,
      verifyImpl: async (jwt) => jwt === 'good' ? { user: { id: 'u1', email: 'x@y.z' } } : { user: null } };
    let r = await accountCore(evt({ httpMethod: 'GET', headers: { origin: ORIGIN } }), deps);
    ok(r.statusCode === 401, 'no token -> 401');
    r = await accountCore(evt({ httpMethod: 'GET', headers: { origin: ORIGIN, authorization: 'Bearer good' } }), deps);
    ok(r.statusCode === 200 && Array.isArray(J(r).dedications), 'valid token -> 200 list');
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- admin
  console.log('\n[admin]');
  {
    const active = [...sharedDb._state.dedications.values()].find((d) => d.status === 'active');
    const verifyImpl = async (jwt) => {
      if (jwt === 'admin') return { user: { id: 'a', email: 'admin@tsdr.example' } };
      if (jwt === 'user') return { user: { id: 'u', email: 'user@tsdr.example' } };
      return { user: null };
    };
    const H = (jwt) => ({ origin: ORIGIN, authorization: `Bearer ${jwt}` });
    const deps = { db: sharedDb, env: ENV, verifyImpl };

    let r = await adminCore(evt({ headers: H('user'), body: body({ action: 'metrics' }) }), deps);
    ok(r.statusCode === 403, 'non-admin -> 403');

    r = await adminCore(evt({ headers: H('admin'), body: body({ action: 'metrics' }) }), deps);
    ok(r.statusCode === 200 && J(r).metrics, 'admin metrics -> 200');

    r = await adminCore(evt({ headers: H('admin'), body: body({ action: 'set_status',
      registration_number: active.regno, status: 'revoked' }) }), deps);
    ok(r.statusCode === 400, 'destructive set_status without confirm -> 400');

    r = await adminCore(evt({ headers: H('admin'), body: body({ action: 'set_status',
      registration_number: active.regno, status: 'revoked', confirm: true, reason: 'test' }) }), deps);
    ok(r.statusCode === 200, 'set_status with confirm -> 200 (audited)');

    r = await adminCore(evt({ headers: H('admin'), body: body({ action: 'correct',
      registration_number: active.regno, field: 'star_catalog_id', new_value: 'x' }) }), deps);
    ok(r.statusCode === 400 && /FIELD_NOT_CORRECTABLE/.test(J(r).message), 'cannot correct star (immutable) -> 400');
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- unit: sanitise + sig
  console.log('\n[unit: sanitise + signature + cors]');
  {
    ok(escapeHtml('<script>alert(1)</script>') === '&lt;script&gt;alert(1)&lt;/script&gt;', 'escapeHtml neutralises XSS');
    const { errors } = validateDedication({ honouree_name: 'x'.repeat(200), symbolic_star_name: 'S', occasion: 'birthday', privacy_level: 'private' });
    ok(errors.some((e) => /honouree_name exceeds/.test(e)), 'length limit enforced');
    ok(normalizeRegNo('tsdr 2026 abcdefgh').ok === true, 'normalizeRegNo tolerates spacing/case');

    const raw = '{"a":1}'; const ts = '123';
    const good = crypto.createHmac('sha256', 'k').update(ts + raw).digest('base64');
    ok(verifyWebhookSignature(raw, good, ts, { CASHFREE_SECRET_KEY: 'k' }) === true, 'signature verify accepts valid');
    ok(verifyWebhookSignature(raw, good, ts, { CASHFREE_SECRET_KEY: 'WRONG' }) === false, 'signature verify rejects wrong key');
    ok(verifyWebhookSignature(raw + 'x', good, ts, { CASHFREE_SECRET_KEY: 'k' }) === false, 'signature verify rejects tampered body');

    // CORS: disallowed origin
    const db = makeMockDb({ poolSize: 1 });
    const r = await reserveCore(evt({ headers: { origin: 'https://evil.example', 'x-nf-client-connection-ip': '9.9.9.9' },
      body: body({ session_ref: 's' }) }), { db, hitImpl: memoryHitImpl(), env: ENV });
    ok(r.headers['Access-Control-Allow-Origin'] === 'null', 'disallowed origin gets ACAO: null');
  }

  console.log(`\n================  RESULT: ${PASS} passed, ${FAIL} failed  ================\n`);
  process.exit(FAIL === 0 ? 0 : 1);
}

run().catch((e) => { console.error(e); process.exit(1); });

#!/usr/bin/env node
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
const root = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '../..');
let pass=0, fail=0;
function ok(c,m){ if(c){pass++;console.log(`  OK ${m}`)}else{fail++;console.log(`  x FAIL: ${m}`)} }
const migration=fs.readFileSync(path.join(root,'supabase/migrations/0016_hardening_owner_updates.sql'),'utf8');
const pkg=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root,'package.json'),'utf8'));
const artifact=fs.readFileSync(path.join(root,'scripts/test/test_artifacts.mjs'),'utf8');
const cashfree=fs.readFileSync(path.join(root,'netlify/functions/_shared/cashfree.mjs'),'utf8');
console.log('\n[Hardening]');
ok(/drop policy if exists dedications_owner_update/i.test(migration),'broad owner UPDATE policy is removed');
ok(/revoke update on table public\.dedications from anon, authenticated/i.test(migration),'browser roles lose direct dedication UPDATE');
ok(/test:cert/.test(pkg.scripts['verify:all']) && /test:artifacts/.test(pkg.scripts['verify:all']),'verify:all includes certificate and artifact suites');
ok(!artifact.includes('/home/claude/'),'artifact test has no developer-specific absolute path');
ok(/fileURLToPath/.test(artifact) && /PROJECT_ROOT/.test(artifact),'artifact paths resolve from the repository');
ok(/CASHFREE_SECRET_KEY/.test(cashfree),'Cashfree webhook verifier uses the documented API secret');
console.log(`\n================  HARDENING RESULT: ${pass} passed, ${fail} failed  ================\n`);
if(fail) process.exit(1);
