-- =============================================================================
-- TSDR -- Migration 0002 -- Row Level Security
-- =============================================================================
-- Principle: DENY BY DEFAULT. RLS is enabled on every table and NO permissive
-- policy is granted to `anon`. Anonymous users reach data ONLY through:
--   * the public-safe VIEWS in 0003 (which expose whitelisted columns), and
--   * the SECURITY DEFINER RPCs in 0004 (called by trusted Netlify Functions).
-- Authenticated end-users may read/manage ONLY their own dedications & media.
-- The service role bypasses RLS (by design) and is used only in backend funcs.
-- =============================================================================

alter table star_catalog          enable row level security;
alter table star_reservations     enable row level security;
alter table dedications           enable row level security;
alter table dedication_media      enable row level security;
alter table registry_events       enable row level security;
alter table payments              enable row level security;
alter table certificate_versions  enable row level security;
alter table content_reports       enable row level security;
alter table ref_occasions         enable row level security;

-- Also FORCE rls so even the table owner is subject to it (defence in depth).
alter table star_catalog          force row level security;
alter table star_reservations     force row level security;
alter table dedications           force row level security;
alter table dedication_media      force row level security;
alter table payments              force row level security;
alter table certificate_versions  force row level security;

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- ref_occasions -- read-only reference data is safe to expose to everyone.
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
drop policy if exists occasions_read on ref_occasions;
create policy occasions_read on ref_occasions
  for select to anon, authenticated
  using (active = true);

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- dedications -- owners can read their own rows. NO anon access (public reads
-- go through the v_public_dedications view which is owned by the definer).
-- Writes only ever happen through SECURITY DEFINER functions, so no INSERT/
-- UPDATE policy is granted here.
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
drop policy if exists dedications_owner_select on dedications;
create policy dedications_owner_select on dedications
  for select to authenticated
  using (purchaser_user_id = auth.uid());

-- Owners may update ONLY a narrow set of fields via a guarded policy. We still
-- prefer routing corrections through an RPC, but this allows privacy toggles
-- within allowed transitions for active, owned rows.
drop policy if exists dedications_owner_update on dedications;
create policy dedications_owner_update on dedications
  for update to authenticated
  using (purchaser_user_id = auth.uid())
  with check (purchaser_user_id = auth.uid());

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- dedication_media -- owner-scoped via the parent dedication.
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
drop policy if exists media_owner_select on dedication_media;
create policy media_owner_select on dedication_media
  for select to authenticated
  using (exists (
    select 1 from dedications d
    where d.id = dedication_media.dedication_id
      and d.purchaser_user_id = auth.uid()));

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- certificate_versions -- owner may list their own certificate history.
-- (Download itself is via signed URL from a backend function, not direct.)
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
drop policy if exists cert_owner_select on certificate_versions;
create policy cert_owner_select on certificate_versions
  for select to authenticated
  using (exists (
    select 1 from dedications d
    where d.id = certificate_versions.dedication_id
      and d.purchaser_user_id = auth.uid()));

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- content_reports -- anyone may FILE a report (insert), but nobody but admin/
-- service may read them. We allow anon INSERT with a strict WITH CHECK so a
-- report cannot be back-read.
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
drop policy if exists reports_insert on content_reports;
create policy reports_insert on content_reports
  for insert to anon, authenticated
  with check (char_length(reason) between 3 and 2000);

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- star_catalog / star_reservations / registry_events / payments:
--   NO policies for anon or authenticated  ==>  fully denied to clients.
--   All access is via service-role backend functions only.
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- (intentionally left with RLS enabled and zero permissive policies)

-- =============================================================================
-- End of 0002
-- =============================================================================
=====================================================
FILE: supabase\migrations\0003_public_views.sql
=====================================================
-- =============================================================================
-- TSDR -- Migration 0003 -- Public-safe views & verification helpers
-- =============================================================================
-- These views expose ONLY whitelisted, non-sensitive columns. They are the
-- sanctioned surface for public exploration and for verification results.
-- NEVER expose: order_reference, purchaser_user_id, *_token_hash, object keys,
-- email/phone, payment references, admin notes, raw metadata.
-- =============================================================================

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- v_public_dedications -- the ONLY dedications visible to the world.
--   Rows appear here ONLY when active AND privacy_level = 'public' AND content
--   review has passed (approved or not_required). Message/name exposure is
--   further governed by the 'limited' rules in the app layer; 'limited' rows are
--   intentionally EXCLUDED from this fully-public view.
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
create or replace view v_public_dedications
with (security_invoker = false) as
select
  d.registration_number,
  d.public_slug,
  d.symbolic_star_name,
  d.honouree_name,
  d.dedicator_name,
  d.occasion,
  d.dedication_date,
  d.dedication_message,
  sc.constellation,
  sc.right_ascension,
  sc.declination,
  sc.source_catalog,
  sc.source_identifier,
  sc.visibility_class,
  d.activated_at
from dedications d
join star_catalog sc on sc.id = d.star_catalog_id
where d.registration_status = 'active'
  and d.privacy_level = 'public'
  and d.content_review_status in ('approved','not_required');

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- v_verification -- minimal, safe payload returned by the Verify endpoint for
--   ANY active registration regardless of privacy. It confirms EXISTENCE and
--   integrity of a registration WITHOUT leaking private content: names and
--   messages are omitted here; only structural + astronomical facts + status.
--   The backend adds private fields only when an access token/ownership check
--   passes.
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
create or replace view v_verification
with (security_invoker = false) as
select
  d.registration_number,
  d.registration_status,
  d.privacy_level,
  d.occasion,
  d.activated_at,
  sc.constellation,
  sc.right_ascension,
  sc.declination,
  sc.source_catalog,
  sc.source_identifier,
  -- surface whether the record carries private content, without revealing it
  (d.privacy_level in ('public'))                as is_public,
  (d.dedication_message is not null)             as has_message
from dedications d
join star_catalog sc on sc.id = d.star_catalog_id
where d.registration_status in ('active','revoked','refunded');

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- v_catalog_availability -- aggregate, non-identifying stats for the homepage
--   ("stars available", constellation coverage). Exposes NO individual record.
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
create or replace view v_catalog_availability
with (security_invoker = false) as
select
  count(*) filter (where allocation_status = 'available')  as available_count,
  count(*) filter (where allocation_status = 'allocated')  as allocated_count,
  count(distinct constellation)                            as constellation_count
from star_catalog
where allocation_status in ('available','allocated');

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Grants: expose ONLY the views to anon/authenticated. Base tables remain
-- unreachable (RLS deny-by-default + no table grants).
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
grant select on v_public_dedications  to anon, authenticated;
grant select on v_verification         to anon, authenticated;
grant select on v_catalog_availability to anon, authenticated;

-- Note: these views are SECURITY DEFINER (security_invoker=false) so they read
-- base tables as the (privileged) view owner, but only ever return whitelisted
-- columns for rows that pass the WHERE guards above. This is the standard
-- "expose a curated view, hide the table" pattern.

-- =============================================================================
-- End of 0003
-- =============================================================================
=====================================================
FILE: supabase\migrations\0004_allocation_functions.sql
=====================================================
-- =============================================================================
-- TSDR -- Migration 0004 -- Allocation, reservation, activation (SECURITY DEFINER)
-- =============================================================================
-- These functions are the ONLY sanctioned path for anonymous/authenticated
-- clients to touch allocation state. They run as owner (SECURITY DEFINER) so
-- RLS on the base tables can stay locked down. Netlify Functions call these
-- via RPC using the service role; the browser never calls them directly with
-- privileged effect. See docs/SECURITY-BASELINE.md.
-- =============================================================================

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Secure, non-sequential registration number:  TSDR-YYYY-XXXXXXXX
--   * 8 chars from a Crockford-ish base32 alphabet (no I,L,O,U to avoid confusion)
--   * ~1.1e12 space per year -> not enumerable, collisions retried
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
create or replace function tsdr_gen_registration_number()
returns text
language plpgsql
volatile
as $$
declare
  alphabet constant text := '0123456789ABCDEFGHJKMNPQRSTVWXYZ'; -- 32 chars
  yr       text := to_char(now(), 'YYYY');
  code     text;
  i        int;
  b        int;
  candidate text;
begin
  for attempt in 1..10 loop
    code := '';
    for i in 1..8 loop
      -- one uniformly random byte -> index 0..31 via mask on 5 bits
      b := (get_byte(gen_random_bytes(1), 0)) & 31;
      code := code || substr(alphabet, b + 1, 1);
    end loop;
    candidate := 'TSDR-' || yr || '-' || code;
    -- ensure uniqueness against existing registrations
    perform 1 from dedications where registration_number = candidate;
    if not found then
      return candidate;
    end if;
  end loop;
  raise exception 'Could not generate a unique registration number after 10 attempts';
end;
$$;

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Atomic reservation:
--   Picks ONE eligible available star (optionally filtered by constellation /
--   visibility), locks it with FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED so concurrent callers can
--   never grab the same row, flips it to 'reserved', and writes a hold row.
--   Returns the star id + the RAW reservation token (caller stores hash only).
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
create or replace function tsdr_reserve_star(
  p_session_ref     text,
  p_constellation   text default null,
  p_visibility      text default null,
  p_hold_minutes    int  default 20
)
returns table (
  star_id            uuid,
  reservation_id     uuid,
  reservation_token  text,
  source_identifier  text,
  constellation      text,
  right_ascension    double precision,
  declination        double precision,
  visibility_class   text,
  expires_at         timestamptz
)
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
declare
  v_star   star_catalog%rowtype;
  v_token  text;
  v_hash   text;
  v_exp    timestamptz;
  v_resv   uuid;
begin
  if p_session_ref is null or length(p_session_ref) = 0 then
    raise exception 'session_ref required';
  end if;

  -- Lock a single eligible star; SKIP LOCKED guarantees no two callers collide.
  select * into v_star
  from star_catalog s
  where s.allocation_status = 'available'
    and (p_constellation is null or s.constellation = p_constellation)
    and (p_visibility   is null or s.visibility_class = p_visibility)
  order by random()
  limit 1
  for update skip locked;

  if not found then
    raise exception 'NO_STAR_AVAILABLE' using errcode = 'P0001';
  end if;

  v_exp   := now() + make_interval(mins => p_hold_minutes);
  -- 32 bytes of entropy, hex-encoded, given to the client once.
  v_token := encode(gen_random_bytes(32), 'hex');
  v_hash  := encode(digest(v_token, 'sha256'), 'hex');

  update star_catalog set allocation_status = 'reserved', updated_at = now()
   where id = v_star.id;

  insert into star_reservations
    (star_catalog_id, session_or_order_ref, reservation_token_hash, status, expires_at)
  values
    (v_star.id, p_session_ref, v_hash, 'held', v_exp)
  returning id into v_resv;

  insert into registry_events (dedication_id, actor_type, actor_id, event_type, event_data)
  values (null, 'system', p_session_ref, 'star_reserved',
          jsonb_build_object('star_id', v_star.id, 'expires_at', v_exp));

  return query select
    v_star.id, v_resv, v_token, v_star.source_identifier, v_star.constellation,
    v_star.right_ascension, v_star.declination, v_star.visibility_class, v_exp;
end;
$$;

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Expire stale holds. Called by a scheduled function (Netlify cron) or on demand.
--   Returns the number of reservations expired.
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
create or replace function tsdr_expire_reservations()
returns integer
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
declare
  v_count int := 0;
begin
  with expired as (
    update star_reservations
       set status = 'expired'
     where status = 'held' and expires_at < now()
    returning star_catalog_id
  ),
  freed as (
    update star_catalog s
       set allocation_status = 'available', updated_at = now()
      from expired e
     where s.id = e.star_catalog_id
       and s.allocation_status = 'reserved'
    returning s.id
  )
  select count(*) into v_count from freed;

  if v_count > 0 then
    insert into registry_events (actor_type, event_type, event_data)
    values ('system','reservations_expired', jsonb_build_object('count', v_count));
  end if;
  return v_count;
end;
$$;

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Create a DRAFT dedication bound to a valid held reservation.
--   Verifies the caller-supplied raw token matches the stored hash and the hold
--   is still live. Returns the new dedication id. No registration number yet --
--   that is assigned ONLY on verified payment activation.
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
create or replace function tsdr_create_draft_dedication(
  p_reservation_id    uuid,
  p_reservation_token text,
  p_purchaser         uuid,
  p_honouree          text,
  p_symbolic_name     text,
  p_dedicator         text,
  p_occasion          text,
  p_dedication_date   date,
  p_message           text,
  p_privacy           text
)
returns uuid
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
declare
  v_resv  star_reservations%rowtype;
  v_hash  text;
  v_ded   uuid;
begin
  select * into v_resv from star_reservations where id = p_reservation_id for update;
  if not found then raise exception 'RESERVATION_NOT_FOUND'; end if;
  if v_resv.status <> 'held' then raise exception 'RESERVATION_NOT_HELD'; end if;
  if v_resv.expires_at < now() then raise exception 'RESERVATION_EXPIRED'; end if;

  v_hash := encode(digest(p_reservation_token, 'sha256'), 'hex');
  if v_hash <> v_resv.reservation_token_hash then
    raise exception 'RESERVATION_TOKEN_MISMATCH';
  end if;

  if p_privacy not in ('public','limited','private','unlisted') then
    raise exception 'INVALID_PRIVACY';
  end if;

  insert into dedications
    (star_catalog_id, purchaser_user_id, honouree_name, symbolic_star_name,
     dedicator_name, occasion, dedication_date, dedication_message,
     privacy_level, registration_status,
     content_review_status)
  values
    (v_resv.star_catalog_id, p_purchaser, p_honouree, p_symbolic_name,
     p_dedicator, p_occasion, p_dedication_date, p_message,
     p_privacy, 'pending_payment',
     case when p_privacy in ('public','limited') then 'pending' else 'not_required' end)
  returning id into v_ded;

  insert into registry_events (dedication_id, actor_type, actor_id, event_type, event_data)
  values (v_ded, 'customer', coalesce(p_purchaser::text,'anon'), 'draft_created',
          jsonb_build_object('reservation_id', p_reservation_id));

  return v_ded;
end;
$$;

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Idempotent activation on verified payment.
--   * Uses payments.idempotency_key uniqueness to make duplicate/out-of-order
--     webhooks safe: a second call with the same key is a no-op that returns the
--     already-assigned registration number.
--   * Only this function assigns registration_number, flips the star to
--     'allocated', consumes the reservation, and marks the dedication active.
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
create or replace function tsdr_activate_paid_dedication(
  p_dedication_id    uuid,
  p_idempotency_key  text,
  p_provider         text,
  p_provider_order   text,
  p_provider_payment text,
  p_amount           numeric,
  p_currency         text,
  p_raw_event_ref    text default null
)
returns table (registration_number text, already_processed boolean)
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
declare
  v_ded   dedications%rowtype;
  v_regno text;
  v_pay   payments%rowtype;
begin
  -- Idempotency short-circuit: if we've already recorded this event, return.
  select * into v_pay from payments where idempotency_key = p_idempotency_key;
  if found then
    select * into v_ded from dedications where id = v_pay.dedication_id;
    return query select v_ded.registration_number, true;
    return;
  end if;

  select * into v_ded from dedications where id = p_dedication_id for update;
  if not found then raise exception 'DEDICATION_NOT_FOUND'; end if;

  -- If somehow already active (e.g. concurrent), return existing number.
  if v_ded.registration_status = 'active' and v_ded.registration_number is not null then
    insert into payments (provider, provider_order_id, provider_payment_id,
                          dedication_id, amount, currency, payment_status,
                          idempotency_key, raw_event_reference)
    values (p_provider, p_provider_order, p_provider_payment, v_ded.id,
            p_amount, p_currency, 'paid', p_idempotency_key, p_raw_event_ref)
    on conflict (idempotency_key) do nothing;
    return query select v_ded.registration_number, true;
    return;
  end if;

  if v_ded.registration_status <> 'pending_payment' then
    raise exception 'DEDICATION_NOT_PENDING (status=%)', v_ded.registration_status;
  end if;

  v_regno := tsdr_gen_registration_number();

  update dedications
     set registration_status = 'active',
         registration_number = v_regno,
         reveal_status = 'ready',
         activated_at = now(),
         updated_at = now()
   where id = v_ded.id;

  -- Consume the reservation & permanently allocate the star.
  update star_reservations set status = 'consumed'
   where star_catalog_id = v_ded.star_catalog_id and status = 'held';
  update star_catalog set allocation_status = 'allocated', updated_at = now()
   where id = v_ded.star_catalog_id;

  insert into payments (provider, provider_order_id, provider_payment_id,
                        dedication_id, amount, currency, payment_status,
                        idempotency_key, raw_event_reference)
  values (p_provider, p_provider_order, p_provider_payment, v_ded.id,
          p_amount, p_currency, 'paid', p_idempotency_key, p_raw_event_ref);

  insert into registry_events (dedication_id, actor_type, actor_id, event_type, event_data)
  values (v_ded.id, 'system', 'payment', 'activated',
          jsonb_build_object('registration_number', v_regno,
                             'provider_order', p_provider_order,
                             'amount', p_amount, 'currency', p_currency));

  return query select v_regno, false;
end;
$$;

-- Lock down EXECUTE: only the service role (used by Netlify Functions) may call.
revoke all on function tsdr_reserve_star(text,text,text,int)             from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke all on function tsdr_expire_reservations()                        from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke all on function tsdr_create_draft_dedication(uuid,text,uuid,text,text,text,text,date,text,text) from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke all on function tsdr_activate_paid_dedication(uuid,text,text,text,text,numeric,text,text)        from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke all on function tsdr_gen_registration_number()                    from public, anon, authenticated;

-- =============================================================================
-- End of 0004

FILE: supabase\migrations\0007_rate_limit_and_admin.sql
=====================================================
-- =============================================================================
-- TSDR -- Migration 0007 -- Rate limiting, verification RPC, account + admin ops
-- =============================================================================
-- Adds:
--   * rate_limits table + tsdr_rate_limit_hit() atomic rolling-window limiter
--   * tsdr_verify() -- enumeration-resistant public verification via safe view
--   * tsdr_account_dedications() -- owner-scoped list for the account area
--   * admin ops (correct / revoke / reissue-request / review) -- all audited
-- All functions are SECURITY DEFINER and EXECUTE-revoked from anon/authenticated;
-- only the service role (backend functions) may call them.
-- =============================================================================

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Rate limiting
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
create table if not exists rate_limits (
  id         bigint generated always as identity primary key,
  key        text        not null,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
create index if not exists ix_rl_key_time on rate_limits (key, created_at);

alter table rate_limits enable row level security;
alter table rate_limits force row level security;
-- no policies => clients cannot read/write; only service role via the function.

-- Atomic: records a hit, counts hits in the window, returns allow decision.
create or replace function tsdr_rate_limit_hit(
  p_key text, p_max int, p_window_secs int
)
returns table (allowed boolean, hit_count int)
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
declare
  v_count int;
begin
  -- opportunistic cleanup of old rows for this key (keeps table small)
  delete from rate_limits
   where key = p_key and created_at < now() - make_interval(secs => p_window_secs);

  insert into rate_limits (key) values (p_key);

  select count(*) into v_count
    from rate_limits
   where key = p_key and created_at >= now() - make_interval(secs => p_window_secs);

  return query select (v_count <= p_max), v_count;
end;
$$;

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Verification (enumeration-resistant): returns ONLY safe fields, and returns
-- ZERO rows for anything not active/revoked/refunded. The calling function maps
-- "no row" to a uniform 404 so valid-but-private and non-existent look identical.
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
create or replace function tsdr_verify(p_regno text)
returns table (
  registration_number text,
  registration_status text,
  privacy_level       text,
  occasion            text,
  activated_at        timestamptz,
  constellation       text,
  right_ascension     double precision,
  declination         double precision,
  source_catalog      text,
  source_identifier   text,
  is_public           boolean,
  has_message         boolean
)
language sql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
  select registration_number, registration_status, privacy_level, occasion,
         activated_at, constellation, right_ascension, declination,
         source_catalog, source_identifier, is_public, has_message
    from v_verification
   where registration_number = p_regno
   limit 1;
$$;

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Account: list the caller's dedications. We pass the user id from the verified
-- JWT (resolved in the function) rather than trusting the client.
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
create or replace function tsdr_account_dedications(p_user uuid)
returns table (
  registration_number text,
  symbolic_star_name  text,
  honouree_name       text,
  occasion            text,
  privacy_level       text,
  registration_status text,
  dedication_date     date,
  constellation       text,
  source_identifier   text,
  reveal_status       text,
  activated_at        timestamptz,
  created_at          timestamptz
)
language sql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
  select d.registration_number, d.symbolic_star_name, d.honouree_name, d.occasion,
         d.privacy_level, d.registration_status, d.dedication_date,
         sc.constellation, sc.source_identifier, d.reveal_status,
         d.activated_at, d.created_at
    from dedications d
    join star_catalog sc on sc.id = d.star_catalog_id
   where d.purchaser_user_id = p_user
   order by d.created_at desc;
$$;

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Admin: correct eligible text fields. Writes an audit event. Cannot change the
-- allocated star or the registration number.
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
create or replace function tsdr_admin_correct(
  p_admin text, p_registration_number text,
  p_field text, p_new_value text, p_reason text
)
returns void
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
declare
  v_id uuid;
  v_old text;
begin
  if p_field not in ('honouree_name','symbolic_star_name','dedicator_name',
                     'dedication_message','privacy_level') then
    raise exception 'FIELD_NOT_CORRECTABLE: %', p_field;
  end if;
  select id into v_id from dedications where registration_number = p_registration_number;
  if v_id is null then raise exception 'NOT_FOUND'; end if;

  execute format('select ($1).%I::text', p_field) into v_old
    using (select d from dedications d where d.id = v_id);

  execute format('update dedications set %I = $1, updated_at = now() where id = $2', p_field)
    using p_new_value, v_id;

  insert into registry_events (dedication_id, actor_type, actor_id, event_type, event_data)
  values (v_id, 'admin', p_admin, 'corrected',
          jsonb_build_object('field', p_field, 'old', v_old, 'new', p_new_value,
                             'reason', p_reason));
end;
$$;

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Admin: revoke / refund. Never hard-deletes; retains history + audit.
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
create or replace function tsdr_admin_set_status(
  p_admin text, p_registration_number text, p_status text, p_reason text
)
returns void
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
declare v_id uuid; v_old text;
begin
  if p_status not in ('revoked','refunded','active') then
    raise exception 'INVALID_STATUS: %', p_status;
  end if;
  select id, registration_status into v_id, v_old
    from dedications where registration_number = p_registration_number;
  if v_id is null then raise exception 'NOT_FOUND'; end if;

  update dedications set registration_status = p_status, updated_at = now()
   where id = v_id;

  -- On revoke/refund, free the star? NO -- keep it allocated to preserve the
  -- one-active-dedication history; a new dedication requires a new star.
  insert into registry_events (dedication_id, actor_type, actor_id, event_type, event_data)
  values (v_id, 'admin', p_admin, 'status_changed',
          jsonb_build_object('old', v_old, 'new', p_status, 'reason', p_reason));
end;
$$;

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Admin: content review decision for public/limited dedications.
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
create or replace function tsdr_admin_review(
  p_admin text, p_registration_number text, p_decision text, p_notes text
)
returns void
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
declare v_id uuid;
begin
  if p_decision not in ('approved','rejected','pending') then
    raise exception 'INVALID_DECISION'; end if;
  select id into v_id from dedications where registration_number = p_registration_number;
  if v_id is null then raise exception 'NOT_FOUND'; end if;

  update dedications set content_review_status = p_decision, updated_at = now()
   where id = v_id;

  insert into registry_events (dedication_id, actor_type, actor_id, event_type, event_data)
  values (v_id, 'admin', p_admin, 'content_reviewed',
          jsonb_build_object('decision', p_decision, 'notes', p_notes));
end;
$$;

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Admin: overview metrics for the dashboard (aggregates only).
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
create or replace function tsdr_admin_metrics()
returns jsonb
language sql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
  select jsonb_build_object(
    'stars_available', (select count(*) from star_catalog where allocation_status='available'),
    'stars_reserved',  (select count(*) from star_catalog where allocation_status='reserved'),
    'stars_allocated', (select count(*) from star_catalog where allocation_status='allocated'),
    'dedications_active',  (select count(*) from dedications where registration_status='active'),
    'dedications_pending', (select count(*) from dedications where registration_status='pending_payment'),
    'reservations_held',   (select count(*) from star_reservations where status='held'),
    'reservations_expired',(select count(*) from star_reservations where status='expired'),
    'payments_paid',    (select count(*) from payments where payment_status='paid'),
    'payments_failed',  (select count(*) from payments where payment_status='failed'),
    'content_open',     (select count(*) from content_reports where review_status='open')
  );
$$;

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Lock down EXECUTE on everything added here.
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
revoke all on function tsdr_rate_limit_hit(text,int,int)             from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke all on function tsdr_verify(text)                             from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke all on function tsdr_account_dedications(uuid)                from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke all on function tsdr_admin_correct(text,text,text,text,text)  from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke all on function tsdr_admin_set_status(text,text,text,text)    from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke all on function tsdr_admin_review(text,text,text,text)        from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke all on function tsdr_admin_metrics()                          from public, anon, authenticated;

-- =============================================================================
-- End of 0007

=====================================================
FILE: supabase\migrations\0008_payment_rpcs.sql
=====================================================
-- =============================================================================
-- TSDR -- Migration 0008 -- Payment-flow RPCs (order attach + webhook activation)
-- =============================================================================
-- Builds on 0004's tsdr_activate_paid_dedication. Adds:
--   * tsdr_get_pending_for_payment  -- safe lookup used by pay-init
--   * tsdr_attach_order             -- records the expected amount/currency/order
--   * tsdr_record_payment_event     -- logs non-success events for the audit
--   * tsdr_activate_by_order        -- matches amount/currency then activates
--                                     idempotently (wraps tsdr_activate_paid_...)
-- =============================================================================

-- Look up a dedication that is awaiting payment (no sensitive fields returned).
create or replace function tsdr_get_pending_for_payment(p_dedication_id uuid)
returns table (dedication_id uuid, registration_status text)
language sql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
  select id, registration_status
    from dedications
   where id = p_dedication_id
     and registration_status = 'pending_payment'
   limit 1;
$$;

-- Attach the intended order + expected price to the dedication and pre-create a
-- 'created' payment row so the webhook can match amount/currency later.
create or replace function tsdr_attach_order(
  p_dedication_id uuid, p_order_reference text,
  p_amount numeric, p_currency text
)
returns void
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
declare v_status text;
begin
  select registration_status into v_status from dedications where id = p_dedication_id for update;
  if v_status is null then raise exception 'NOT_FOUND'; end if;
  if v_status <> 'pending_payment' then raise exception 'NOT_PENDING'; end if;

  update dedications set order_reference = p_order_reference, updated_at = now()
   where id = p_dedication_id;

  insert into payments (provider, provider_order_id, dedication_id, amount, currency,
                        payment_status, idempotency_key)
  values ('cashfree', p_order_reference, p_dedication_id, p_amount, p_currency,
          'created', 'order-created:' || p_order_reference)
  on conflict (idempotency_key) do nothing;

  insert into registry_events (dedication_id, actor_type, actor_id, event_type, event_data)
  values (p_dedication_id, 'system', 'pay-init', 'order_attached',
          jsonb_build_object('order_reference', p_order_reference,
                             'amount', p_amount, 'currency', p_currency));
end;
$$;

-- Record a non-success (or informational) payment event for the audit trail.
create or replace function tsdr_record_payment_event(
  p_order_reference text, p_provider text, p_provider_payment_id text,
  p_status text, p_amount numeric, p_currency text
)
returns void
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
declare v_ded uuid;
begin
  select dedication_id into v_ded from payments
   where provider_order_id = p_order_reference limit 1;

  insert into payments (provider, provider_order_id, provider_payment_id, dedication_id,
                        amount, currency, payment_status, idempotency_key)
  values (p_provider, p_order_reference, p_provider_payment_id, v_ded,
          coalesce(p_amount,0), coalesce(p_currency,'INR'), p_status,
          'evt:' || coalesce(p_provider_payment_id, p_order_reference) || ':' || p_status)
  on conflict (idempotency_key) do nothing;

  insert into registry_events (dedication_id, actor_type, actor_id, event_type, event_data)
  values (v_ded, 'system', 'payment', 'payment_event',
          jsonb_build_object('status', p_status, 'order_reference', p_order_reference));
end;
$$;

-- Match amount/currency against the expected values, then activate idempotently.
create or replace function tsdr_activate_by_order(
  p_order_reference   text,
  p_idempotency_key   text,
  p_provider          text,
  p_provider_payment_id text,
  p_amount            numeric,
  p_currency          text
)
returns table (registration_number text, already_processed boolean)
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
declare
  v_ded_id  uuid;
  v_expect  numeric;
  v_expcur  text;
begin
  -- Find the dedication + expected price from the pre-created order row.
  select dedication_id, amount, currency
    into v_ded_id, v_expect, v_expcur
    from payments
   where provider_order_id = p_order_reference
     and payment_status = 'created'
   order by created_at asc
   limit 1;

  if v_ded_id is null then
    -- Maybe already activated (idempotent replay) -- defer to activate function
    -- which will short-circuit on the idempotency key.
    select d.id into v_ded_id from dedications d where d.order_reference = p_order_reference limit 1;
    if v_ded_id is null then raise exception 'ORDER_NOT_FOUND'; end if;
  else
    -- GATE 3: amount + currency must match server-recorded expectation.
    if round(p_amount::numeric, 2) <> round(v_expect::numeric, 2) then
      insert into registry_events (dedication_id, actor_type, actor_id, event_type, event_data)
      values (v_ded_id, 'system', 'payment', 'amount_mismatch',
              jsonb_build_object('expected', v_expect, 'got', p_amount));
      raise exception 'AMOUNT_MISMATCH expected=% got=%', v_expect, p_amount;
    end if;
    if upper(coalesce(p_currency,'')) <> upper(coalesce(v_expcur,'INR')) then
      raise exception 'CURRENCY_MISMATCH expected=% got=%', v_expcur, p_currency;
    end if;
  end if;

  -- GATE 4: idempotent activation (assigns reg number, allocates star, etc.)
  return query
    select * from tsdr_activate_paid_dedication(
      v_ded_id, p_idempotency_key, p_provider, p_order_reference,
      p_provider_payment_id, p_amount, p_currency, null);
end;
$$;

revoke all on function tsdr_get_pending_for_payment(uuid)                   from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke all on function tsdr_attach_order(uuid,text,numeric,text)            from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke all on function tsdr_record_payment_event(text,text,text,text,numeric,text) from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke all on function tsdr_activate_by_order(text,text,text,text,numeric,text)    from public, anon, authenticated;

-- =============================================================================
-- End of 0008
-- =============================================================================

FILE: supabase\migrations\0009_reveal_media_artifacts.sql
=====================================================
-- =============================================================================
-- TSDR -- Migration 0009 -- Reveal, media, artifacts, neighbours (Run 3)
-- =============================================================================
-- Adds:
--   * dedications.reveal_mode (per-occasion cinematic mode override)
--   * tsdr_reveal_public / tsdr_reveal_by_token  -- privacy-filtered reveal data
--   * tsdr_star_neighbors  -- public-safe nearby stars for the viewer/map
--   * tsdr_media_register  -- ownership-checked media row + object key
--   * tsdr_artifact_key    -- authorised certificate/star-map object key lookup
--   * tsdr_record_certificate -- append a certificate_versions row
-- All SECURITY DEFINER, EXECUTE-revoked from anon/authenticated.
-- =============================================================================

alter table dedications
  add column if not exists reveal_mode text
    check (reveal_mode in ('celebration','romance','friendship','memorial','newborn','achievement'));

-- Public reveal: only active + public (+ review-passed) records, by reg number.
create or replace function tsdr_reveal_public(p_regno text)
returns table (
  registration_number text, symbolic_star_name text, honouree_name text,
  dedicator_name text, dedication_message text, occasion text, reveal_mode text,
  constellation text, source_identifier text, right_ascension double precision,
  declination double precision, visibility_class text
)
language sql security definer set search_path = public as $$
  select d.registration_number, d.symbolic_star_name, d.honouree_name,
         d.dedicator_name, d.dedication_message, d.occasion, d.reveal_mode,
         sc.constellation, sc.source_identifier, sc.right_ascension,
         sc.declination, sc.visibility_class
    from dedications d join star_catalog sc on sc.id = d.star_catalog_id
   where d.registration_number = p_regno
     and d.registration_status = 'active'
     and d.privacy_level = 'public'
     and d.content_review_status in ('approved','not_required')
   limit 1;
$$;

-- Private/unlisted reveal: requires the high-entropy access token (hash match).
create or replace function tsdr_reveal_by_token(p_token text)
returns table (
  registration_number text, symbolic_star_name text, honouree_name text,
  dedicator_name text, dedication_message text, occasion text, reveal_mode text,
  constellation text, source_identifier text, right_ascension double precision,
  declination double precision, visibility_class text
)
language sql security definer set search_path = public as $$
  select d.registration_number, d.symbolic_star_name, d.honouree_name,
         d.dedicator_name, d.dedication_message, d.occasion, d.reveal_mode,
         sc.constellation, sc.source_identifier, sc.right_ascension,
         sc.declination, sc.visibility_class
    from dedications d join star_catalog sc on sc.id = d.star_catalog_id
   where d.private_access_token_hash = encode(digest(p_token, 'sha256'), 'hex')
     and d.registration_status = 'active'
   limit 1;
$$;

-- Nearby stars (public-safe: coordinates + magnitude only). Bounding-box + exact
-- angular filter approximated for index use; caller uses gnomonic client-side.
create or replace function tsdr_star_neighbors(
  p_ra double precision, p_dec double precision, p_fov double precision, p_max int
)
returns table (
  source_identifier text, right_ascension double precision,
  declination double precision, apparent_magnitude double precision
)
language sql security definer set search_path = public as $$
  with box as (
    select p_fov * 0.62 as radius,
           greatest(0.0001, cos(radians(p_dec))) as cosd
  )
  select sc.source_identifier, sc.right_ascension, sc.declination, sc.apparent_magnitude
    from star_catalog sc, box
   where sc.declination between p_dec - box.radius and p_dec + box.radius
     and sc.right_ascension between p_ra - box.radius / box.cosd
                               and p_ra + box.radius / box.cosd
   order by sc.apparent_magnitude asc nulls last
   limit p_max;
$$;

-- Register a media row after verifying the caller owns the dedication.
create or replace function tsdr_media_register(
  p_user uuid, p_dedication_id uuid, p_media_type text,
  p_object_key text, p_mime text, p_bytes bigint
)
returns boolean
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare v_owner uuid;
begin
  select purchaser_user_id into v_owner from dedications where id = p_dedication_id;
  if v_owner is null or v_owner <> p_user then return false; end if;

  insert into dedication_media (dedication_id, media_type, object_key, media_status, metadata)
  values (p_dedication_id, p_media_type, p_object_key, 'pending',
          jsonb_build_object('mime', p_mime, 'bytes', p_bytes));

  insert into registry_events (dedication_id, actor_type, actor_id, event_type, event_data)
  values (p_dedication_id, 'customer', p_user::text, 'media_registered',
          jsonb_build_object('type', p_media_type, 'object_key', p_object_key));
  return true;
end;
$$;

-- Authorised lookup of a certificate/star-map object key.
create or replace function tsdr_artifact_key(
  p_type text, p_regno text, p_token text
)
returns table (object_key text)
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare v record;
begin
  select certificate_object_key, star_map_object_key, privacy_level,
         registration_status
    into v
    from dedications
   where (p_regno is not null and registration_number = p_regno)
      or (p_token is not null and
          private_access_token_hash = encode(digest(p_token, 'sha256'), 'hex'))
   limit 1;
  if not found then return; end if;
  if v.registration_status <> 'active' then return; end if;
  -- public records: rn is enough. private/unlisted: a token was required to match.
  if p_regno is not null and v.privacy_level not in ('public','limited')
     and p_token is null then
    return; -- private record requested by rn without token -> nothing
  end if;
  if p_type = 'certificate' then return query select v.certificate_object_key;
  else return query select v.star_map_object_key; end if;
end;
$$;

-- Append a certificate version (called after generation stores the PDF to R2).
create or replace function tsdr_record_certificate(
  p_dedication_id uuid, p_object_key text, p_hash text, p_reason text, p_by text
)
returns int
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare v_ver int;
begin
  select coalesce(max(version_number),0)+1 into v_ver
    from certificate_versions where dedication_id = p_dedication_id;
  insert into certificate_versions (dedication_id, version_number, object_key,
                                    certificate_hash, reason, created_by)
  values (p_dedication_id, v_ver, p_object_key, p_hash, p_reason, p_by);
  update dedications set certificate_object_key = p_object_key, updated_at = now()
   where id = p_dedication_id;
  insert into registry_events (dedication_id, actor_type, actor_id, event_type, event_data)
  values (p_dedication_id, 'system', coalesce(p_by,'cert'), 'certificate_issued',
          jsonb_build_object('version', v_ver, 'hash', p_hash));
  return v_ver;
end;
$$;

revoke all on function tsdr_reveal_public(text)                         from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke all on function tsdr_reveal_by_token(text)                       from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke all on function tsdr_star_neighbors(double precision,double precision,double precision,int) from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke all on function tsdr_media_register(uuid,uuid,text,text,text,bigint) from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke all on function tsdr_artifact_key(text,text,text)                from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke all on function tsdr_record_certificate(uuid,text,text,text,text) from public, anon, authenticated;

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Explore listing (public-safe): a small list of public, review-passed records.
-- Reads the whitelisted view only.
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
create or replace function tsdr_explore_public(p_limit int)
returns table (
  registration_number text, symbolic_star_name text, honouree_name text,
  occasion text, constellation text, source_identifier text
)
language sql security definer set search_path = public as $$
  select registration_number, symbolic_star_name, honouree_name,
         occasion, constellation, source_identifier
    from v_public_dedications
   order by activated_at desc nulls last
   limit greatest(1, least(p_limit, 60));
$$;

revoke all on function tsdr_explore_public(int) from public, anon, authenticated;

FILE: supabase\migrations\0011_promo_codes.sql
=====================================================
-- =============================================================================
-- TSDR -- Migration 0011 -- Promo codes (server-authoritative discounts + free)
-- =============================================================================
-- Adds a promo_codes table and two SECURITY DEFINER RPCs:
--   * tsdr_promo_quote(code, amount)        -- validate + compute discounted price
--                                             (read-only; consumes nothing)
--   * tsdr_redeem_free_dedication(...)      -- for 100%-off codes: consume one use
--                                             and activate the dedication for FREE,
--                                             reusing the idempotent activation.
-- The price is ALWAYS computed on the server (pay-init passes PRICE_INR.standard);
-- the browser never sets the amount. A 100% code makes the bill 0 and lets the
-- flow proceed WITHOUT Cashfree -- for true end-to-end testing before payments are
-- configured (mirrors the ZivoGift approach).
--
-- Apply AFTER 0001-0010 (needs dedications + tsdr_activate_paid_dedication).
-- =============================================================================

create table if not exists promo_codes (
  code         text primary key,                 -- stored/compared UPPERCASE
  percent_off  int  not null check (percent_off between 1 and 100),
  active       boolean not null default true,
  max_uses     int,                              -- null = unlimited
  uses         int  not null default 0,
  expires_at   timestamptz,                      -- null = never expires
  note         text,
  created_at   timestamptz not null default now()
);

-- Lock it down: clients can never read/write; only the service role via the RPCs.
alter table promo_codes enable row level security;
alter table promo_codes force  row level security;
-- (intentionally no policies -> fully denied to anon/authenticated)

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Quote a promo against a server-provided amount. Returns validity + final price.
-- Never consumes a use. final_amount is rounded to 2 dp (paise-safe INR).
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
create or replace function tsdr_promo_quote(p_code text, p_amount numeric)
returns table (valid boolean, percent_off int, final_amount numeric, reason text)
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
declare
  v promo_codes%rowtype;
begin
  select * into v from promo_codes where code = upper(trim(p_code));
  if not found then
    return query select false, 0, p_amount, 'PROMO_INVALID'; return;
  end if;
  if not v.active then
    return query select false, 0, p_amount, 'PROMO_INACTIVE'; return;
  end if;
  if v.expires_at is not null and v.expires_at < now() then
    return query select false, 0, p_amount, 'PROMO_EXPIRED'; return;
  end if;
  if v.max_uses is not null and v.uses >= v.max_uses then
    return query select false, 0, p_amount, 'PROMO_EXHAUSTED'; return;
  end if;
  return query
    select true, v.percent_off,
           round((p_amount * (100 - v.percent_off) / 100.0)::numeric, 2),
           'OK';
end;
$$;

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Redeem a 100%-off code: consume ONE use (atomically) and activate the
-- dedication for free, delegating to the existing idempotent activation so
-- duplicate calls return the same registration number and never double-consume.
-- Only percent_off = 100 qualifies here (partial discounts go through Cashfree).
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
create or replace function tsdr_redeem_free_dedication(
  p_dedication_id   uuid,
  p_code            text,
  p_idempotency_key text
)
returns table (registration_number text, already_processed boolean)
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
declare
  v        promo_codes%rowtype;
  v_seen   boolean;
begin
  -- Has this exact activation already happened? (idempotent replay)
  select exists(select 1 from payments where idempotency_key = p_idempotency_key)
    into v_seen;

  -- Lock the promo row for a consistent uses check/increment.
  select * into v from promo_codes where code = upper(trim(p_code)) for update;
  if not found            then raise exception 'PROMO_INVALID';   end if;
  if not v.active         then raise exception 'PROMO_INACTIVE';  end if;
  if v.expires_at is not null and v.expires_at < now()
                          then raise exception 'PROMO_EXPIRED';   end if;
  if v.max_uses is not null and v.uses >= v.max_uses
                          then raise exception 'PROMO_EXHAUSTED'; end if;
  if v.percent_off < 100  then raise exception 'PROMO_NOT_FREE';  end if;

  -- Consume exactly once per unique activation.
  if not v_seen then
    update promo_codes set uses = uses + 1 where code = v.code;
  end if;

  -- Reuse the idempotent activation (assigns reg number, allocates star,
  -- consumes the reservation, writes the audit trail). amount = 0, provider = 'promo'.
  return query
    select * from tsdr_activate_paid_dedication(
      p_dedication_id,
      p_idempotency_key,
      'promo',
      'PROMO-' || v.code,
      null,
      0,
      'INR',
      null);
end;
$$;

-- Only the service role (Netlify Functions) may call these.
revoke all on function tsdr_promo_quote(text, numeric)                 from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke all on function tsdr_redeem_free_dedication(uuid, text, text)   from public, anon, authenticated;

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Seed a ready-to-use 100% testing code. Change/disable before launch.
--   Use it on the dedication form's "Promo code" box to complete a FREE order.
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
insert into promo_codes (code, percent_off, active, max_uses, note)
values ('TESTFREE100', 100, true, 1000, 'End-to-end testing -- 100% off, skips Cashfree')
on conflict (code) do nothing;

-- =============================================================================
-- End of 0011
-- =============================================================================

FILE: supabase\migrations\0012_buyer_contact_and_price.sql
=====================================================
-- =============================================================================
-- TSDR -- Migration 0012 -- Buyer contact, keepsake token, admin-editable price
-- =============================================================================
-- Run A additions (apply AFTER 0001-0011). This migration:
--   1. Adds buyer_email (required at draft time) + buyer_phone to dedications.
--   2. Adds a keepsake access token at draft time (hash stored in the existing
--      private_access_token_hash column) so the buyer can open their reveal +
--      certificate for ANY privacy level, using ?rn=..&t=.. links. The raw token
--      is generated server-side in the draft Function and never stored raw.
--   3. Makes the price admin-editable via a registry_settings table + RPCs,
--      replacing the hard-coded PRICE_INR in pay-init.mjs.
--   4. Adds a certificate/keepsake view RPC + a keepsake-contact RPC (for email
--      delivery) and extends the reveal RPCs with dedication_date + magnitude.
-- All functions are SECURITY DEFINER and EXECUTE-revoked from anon/authenticated
-- (service role only), matching the rest of the schema.
-- =============================================================================

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 1. Buyer contact columns
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
alter table dedications
  add column if not exists buyer_email text,
  add column if not exists buyer_phone text;

do $$
begin
  if not exists (select 1 from pg_constraint where conname = 'ck_ded_buyer_email_len') then
    alter table dedications
      add constraint ck_ded_buyer_email_len
      check (buyer_email is null or char_length(buyer_email) <= 254);
  end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from pg_constraint where conname = 'ck_ded_buyer_phone_len') then
    alter table dedications
      add constraint ck_ded_buyer_phone_len
      check (buyer_phone is null or char_length(buyer_phone) <= 32);
  end if;
end $$;

-- Index for looking a buyer up by email (support / re-send keepsake by email).
create index if not exists ix_ded_buyer_email on dedications (lower(buyer_email))
  where buyer_email is not null;

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 2. Admin-editable price
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
create table if not exists registry_settings (
  key        text primary key,
  value      jsonb not null,
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

alter table registry_settings enable row level security;
alter table registry_settings force  row level security;
-- (no policies -> only the service role via RPCs can read/write)

insert into registry_settings (key, value)
values ('price_inr', to_jsonb(1499::numeric))
on conflict (key) do nothing;

-- Server-authoritative price used by pay-init (falls back to 1499 if unset).
create or replace function tsdr_get_price()
returns numeric
language sql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
  select coalesce(
    (select (value #>> '{}')::numeric from registry_settings where key = 'price_inr'),
    1499::numeric);
$$;

-- Admin: update the standard price. Audited.
create or replace function tsdr_admin_set_price(p_admin text, p_amount numeric)
returns numeric
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
begin
  if p_amount is null or p_amount < 0 then
    raise exception 'INVALID_PRICE';
  end if;
  insert into registry_settings (key, value, updated_at)
  values ('price_inr', to_jsonb(round(p_amount, 2)), now())
  on conflict (key) do update set value = excluded.value, updated_at = now();
  insert into registry_events (actor_type, actor_id, event_type, event_data)
  values ('admin', p_admin, 'price_changed', jsonb_build_object('amount', round(p_amount, 2)));
  return round(p_amount, 2);
end;
$$;

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 3. Draft dedication (v2): stores buyer contact + a keepsake token hash.
--    New 13-arg signature (the original 10-arg version from 0004 is left intact
--    but is no longer called). Returns the dedication id, as before.
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
create or replace function tsdr_create_draft_dedication(
  p_reservation_id     uuid,
  p_reservation_token  text,
  p_purchaser          uuid,
  p_honouree           text,
  p_symbolic_name      text,
  p_dedicator          text,
  p_occasion           text,
  p_dedication_date    date,
  p_message            text,
  p_privacy            text,
  p_buyer_email        text,
  p_buyer_phone        text,
  p_keepsake_token_hash text
)
returns uuid
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
declare
  v_resv  star_reservations%rowtype;
  v_hash  text;
  v_ded   uuid;
begin
  select * into v_resv from star_reservations where id = p_reservation_id for update;
  if not found then raise exception 'RESERVATION_NOT_FOUND'; end if;
  if v_resv.status <> 'held' then raise exception 'RESERVATION_NOT_HELD'; end if;
  if v_resv.expires_at < now() then raise exception 'RESERVATION_EXPIRED'; end if;

  v_hash := encode(digest(p_reservation_token, 'sha256'), 'hex');
  if v_hash <> v_resv.reservation_token_hash then
    raise exception 'RESERVATION_TOKEN_MISMATCH';
  end if;

  if p_privacy not in ('public','limited','private','unlisted') then
    raise exception 'INVALID_PRIVACY';
  end if;
  if p_buyer_email is null or char_length(trim(p_buyer_email)) = 0 then
    raise exception 'BUYER_EMAIL_REQUIRED';
  end if;

  insert into dedications
    (star_catalog_id, purchaser_user_id, honouree_name, symbolic_star_name,
     dedicator_name, occasion, dedication_date, dedication_message,
     privacy_level, registration_status, content_review_status,
     buyer_email, buyer_phone, private_access_token_hash)
  values
    (v_resv.star_catalog_id, p_purchaser, p_honouree, p_symbolic_name,
     p_dedicator, p_occasion, p_dedication_date, p_message,
     p_privacy, 'pending_payment',
     case when p_privacy in ('public','limited') then 'pending' else 'not_required' end,
     lower(trim(p_buyer_email)), p_buyer_phone, p_keepsake_token_hash)
  returning id into v_ded;

  insert into registry_events (dedication_id, actor_type, actor_id, event_type, event_data)
  values (v_ded, 'customer', coalesce(p_purchaser::text,'anon'), 'draft_created',
          jsonb_build_object('reservation_id', p_reservation_id, 'has_email', true));

  return v_ded;
end;
$$;

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 4a. Reveal RPCs (v2): add dedication_date + apparent_magnitude.
--     Return type changes, so drop + recreate (create-or-replace cannot widen).
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
drop function if exists tsdr_reveal_public(text);
create function tsdr_reveal_public(p_regno text)
returns table (
  registration_number text, symbolic_star_name text, honouree_name text,
  dedicator_name text, dedication_message text, occasion text, reveal_mode text,
  dedication_date date, constellation text, source_identifier text,
  right_ascension double precision, declination double precision,
  apparent_magnitude double precision, visibility_class text
)
language sql security definer set search_path = public as $$
  select d.registration_number, d.symbolic_star_name, d.honouree_name,
         d.dedicator_name, d.dedication_message, d.occasion, d.reveal_mode,
         d.dedication_date, sc.constellation, sc.source_identifier,
         sc.right_ascension, sc.declination, sc.apparent_magnitude, sc.visibility_class
    from dedications d join star_catalog sc on sc.id = d.star_catalog_id
   where d.registration_number = p_regno
     and d.registration_status = 'active'
     and d.privacy_level = 'public'
     and d.content_review_status in ('approved','not_required')
   limit 1;
$$;

drop function if exists tsdr_reveal_by_token(text);
create function tsdr_reveal_by_token(p_token text)
returns table (
  registration_number text, symbolic_star_name text, honouree_name text,
  dedicator_name text, dedication_message text, occasion text, reveal_mode text,
  dedication_date date, constellation text, source_identifier text,
  right_ascension double precision, declination double precision,
  apparent_magnitude double precision, visibility_class text
)
language sql security definer set search_path = public as $$
  select d.registration_number, d.symbolic_star_name, d.honouree_name,
         d.dedicator_name, d.dedication_message, d.occasion, d.reveal_mode,
         d.dedication_date, sc.constellation, sc.source_identifier,
         sc.right_ascension, sc.declination, sc.apparent_magnitude, sc.visibility_class
    from dedications d join star_catalog sc on sc.id = d.star_catalog_id
   where d.private_access_token_hash = encode(digest(p_token, 'sha256'), 'hex')
     and d.registration_status = 'active'
   limit 1;
$$;

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 4b. Certificate / keepsake view: full record for the HTML certificate page.
--     Authorised by keepsake token (any privacy) OR by rn (public only).
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
create or replace function tsdr_certificate_view(p_regno text, p_token text)
returns table (
  registration_number text, symbolic_star_name text, honouree_name text,
  dedicator_name text, dedication_message text, occasion text, dedication_date date,
  privacy_level text, constellation text, source_catalog text, source_identifier text,
  right_ascension double precision, declination double precision,
  apparent_magnitude double precision, visibility_class text,
  activated_at timestamptz
)
language sql security definer set search_path = public as $$
  select d.registration_number, d.symbolic_star_name, d.honouree_name,
         d.dedicator_name, d.dedication_message, d.occasion, d.dedication_date,
         d.privacy_level, sc.constellation, sc.source_catalog, sc.source_identifier,
         sc.right_ascension, sc.declination, sc.apparent_magnitude, sc.visibility_class,
         d.activated_at
    from dedications d join star_catalog sc on sc.id = d.star_catalog_id
   where d.registration_status = 'active'
     and (
       (p_token is not null
         and d.private_access_token_hash = encode(digest(p_token, 'sha256'), 'hex'))
       or (p_regno is not null and d.registration_number = p_regno
         and d.privacy_level = 'public'
         and d.content_review_status in ('approved','not_required'))
     )
   limit 1;
$$;

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 4c. Keepsake contact: buyer email for delivery, resolved by keepsake token.
--     Used ONLY server-side by send-keepsake; buyer_email is never exposed to
--     the browser or to reveal/verify.
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
create or replace function tsdr_keepsake_contact(p_token text)
returns table (
  registration_number text, buyer_email text,
  symbolic_star_name text, honouree_name text
)
language sql security definer set search_path = public as $$
  select registration_number, buyer_email, symbolic_star_name, honouree_name
    from dedications
   where registration_status = 'active'
     and private_access_token_hash = encode(digest(p_token, 'sha256'), 'hex')
   limit 1;
$$;

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Lock down EXECUTE on everything added here (service role only).
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
revoke all on function tsdr_get_price()                              from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke all on function tsdr_admin_set_price(text, numeric)           from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke all on function tsdr_create_draft_dedication(uuid,text,uuid,text,text,text,text,date,text,text,text,text,text)
                                                                     from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke all on function tsdr_reveal_public(text)                      from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke all on function tsdr_reveal_by_token(text)                    from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke all on function tsdr_certificate_view(text, text)             from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke all on function tsdr_keepsake_contact(text)                   from public, anon, authenticated;

=====================================================
FILE: supabase\migrations\0013_artifacts_target.sql
=====================================================
-- =============================================================================
-- TSDR -- Migration 0013 -- Artifact resolver for on-demand PDF/starmap issuing
-- =============================================================================
-- Run B. The certificate page (and account page) trigger idempotent artifact
-- generation on view. To do that safely we must resolve the dedication id from
-- the SAME public-safe inputs the download endpoint accepts (public reg number,
-- or the owner's keepsake token), applying the SAME privacy authorisation as
-- tsdr_artifact_key (0009): a private/unlisted record can only be targeted with
-- a valid token, never by registration number alone.
--
-- Returns the dedication id plus whichever artifact keys already exist, so the
-- caller can short-circuit when the certificate/star-map have already been made.
-- SECURITY DEFINER; EXECUTE revoked from anon/authenticated (service role only).
-- =============================================================================

create or replace function tsdr_artifacts_target(p_regno text, p_token text)
returns table (
  dedication_id uuid,
  certificate_object_key text,
  star_map_object_key text,
  privacy_level text
)
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
declare v record;
begin
  select id, certificate_object_key, star_map_object_key, privacy_level,
         registration_status, content_review_status
    into v
    from dedications
   where (p_token is not null
           and private_access_token_hash = encode(digest(p_token, 'sha256'), 'hex'))
      or (p_regno is not null and registration_number = p_regno)
   limit 1;

  if not found then return; end if;
  if v.registration_status <> 'active' then return; end if;

  -- Private/unlisted records require the token; rn alone must reveal nothing.
  if p_token is null and v.privacy_level not in ('public','limited') then
    return;
  end if;
  -- Public-by-rn additionally must have passed review (matches reveal/keepsake).
  if p_token is null and v.privacy_level in ('public','limited')
     and v.content_review_status not in ('approved','not_required') then
    return;
  end if;

  return query select v.id, v.certificate_object_key, v.star_map_object_key, v.privacy_level;
end;
$$;

revoke all on function tsdr_artifacts_target(text, text) from public, anon, authenticated;

-- =============================================================================
-- End of 0013
-- =============================================================================
=====================================================
FILE: supabase\migrations\0014_owner_access.sql
=====================================================
-- =============================================================================
-- TSDR -- Migration 0014 -- Owner-authorised account access (Run C)
-- =============================================================================
-- Run C adds an authenticated account experience: a signed-in buyer can REPLAY
-- the reveal and RE-DOWNLOAD their certificate / star map for ANY privacy level
-- of a dedication they OWN -- without the keepsake token (which is intentionally
-- never stored in raw form; only its SHA-256 hash is kept, per Run A design).
--
-- The single resolver below returns the full reveal + certificate fields plus
-- the existing artifact object keys and the dedication id, but ONLY when the
-- caller's verified user id matches dedications.purchaser_user_id. The Netlify
-- functions pass p_user from the VERIFIED Supabase JWT (never from the client
-- body), so this cannot be used to read someone else's record.
--
-- SECURITY DEFINER; EXECUTE revoked from anon/authenticated (service role only,
-- called by trusted Netlify Functions). Additive; safe to re-run.
--
-- Apply AFTER 0001-0013.
-- =============================================================================

create or replace function tsdr_owner_record(p_user uuid, p_regno text)
returns table (
  dedication_id           uuid,
  registration_number     text,
  symbolic_star_name      text,
  honouree_name           text,
  dedicator_name          text,
  dedication_message      text,
  occasion                text,
  reveal_mode             text,
  dedication_date         date,
  privacy_level           text,
  constellation           text,
  source_catalog          text,
  source_identifier       text,
  right_ascension         double precision,
  declination             double precision,
  apparent_magnitude      double precision,
  visibility_class        text,
  certificate_object_key  text,
  star_map_object_key     text,
  activated_at            timestamptz
)
language sql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
  select d.id, d.registration_number, d.symbolic_star_name, d.honouree_name,
         d.dedicator_name, d.dedication_message, d.occasion, d.reveal_mode,
         d.dedication_date, d.privacy_level,
         sc.constellation, sc.source_catalog, sc.source_identifier,
         sc.right_ascension, sc.declination, sc.apparent_magnitude, sc.visibility_class,
         d.certificate_object_key, d.star_map_object_key, d.activated_at
    from dedications d
    join star_catalog sc on sc.id = d.star_catalog_id
   where d.registration_number = p_regno
     and d.purchaser_user_id = p_user
     and d.registration_status = 'active'
   limit 1;
$$;

revoke all on function tsdr_owner_record(uuid, text) from public, anon, authenticated;

-- End of 0014

=====================================================
FILE: supabase\migrations\0015_order_status.sql
=====================================================
-- =============================================================================
-- TSDR -- Migration 0015 -- Order status resolver for the payment return (Run D)
-- =============================================================================
-- After a Cashfree checkout, the gateway redirects the buyer back to
--   /reveal.html?order=<order_reference>
-- The browser needs to turn that order reference into the reveal it just paid
-- for. This resolver returns ONLY public-safe status fields:
--   registration_status  ('pending_payment' | 'active' | 'revoked' | ...)
--   registration_number  (null until the webhook activates the dedication)
--   privacy_level         (so the client knows whether rn alone opens the reveal)
--
-- It deliberately returns NO names, message, email, phone, token, or star coords
-- -- purely enough to route the returning buyer to /reveal.html?rn=... (public)
-- or, for private/unlisted, to combine with the keepsake token the client stored
-- locally before checkout. The order_reference itself is a high-entropy,
-- server-generated value (never exposed in any public listing), so it behaves
-- like a one-time claim ticket for the returning payer.
--
-- The webhook (pay-webhook -> tsdr_activate_by_order) remains the ONLY path that
-- activates a dedication and assigns the registration number; this function only
-- reads status. SECURITY DEFINER; EXECUTE revoked from anon/authenticated
-- (service role only, called by the trusted pay-status function).
--
-- Apply AFTER 0001-0014.
-- =============================================================================

create or replace function tsdr_order_status(p_order_reference text)
returns table (
  registration_status text,
  registration_number text,
  privacy_level       text
)
language sql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
  select d.registration_status, d.registration_number, d.privacy_level
    from dedications d
   where d.order_reference = p_order_reference
   limit 1;
$$;

revoke all on function tsdr_order_status(text) from public, anon, authenticated;

-- =============================================================================
-- End of 0015
-- =============================================================================

=====================================================
FILE: supabase\migrations\0016_hardening_owner_updates.sql
=====================================================
-- =============================================================================
-- TSDR -- Migration 0016 -- Harden owner updates
-- =============================================================================
-- The registry row is the source of truth. Migration 0002 created an owner UPDATE
-- policy whose comment described narrow privacy changes, but PostgreSQL RLS does
-- not provide column-level restrictions. Remove direct client updates entirely.
-- Future owner corrections/privacy changes must go through a narrowly validated,
-- audited server-side RPC or Netlify Function.
-- =============================================================================

drop policy if exists dedications_owner_update on public.dedications;

-- Explicitly remove table-level UPDATE from browser roles even if a project-level
-- default grant was added outside these migrations.
revoke update on table public.dedications from anon, authenticated;

-- Owners retain SELECT through dedications_owner_select. Service-role backend
-- operations and SECURITY DEFINER registry RPCs remain the only mutation paths.
-- =============================================================================
-- End of 0016
-- =============================================================================
